     2	import asyncio
     3	import os
     4	import random
     5	import time
     6	import secrets
     7	from datetime import datetime

     9	from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, BackgroundTasks, HTTPException, Response, Cookie

    13	app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade Master Secured Cockpit", version="2.5.0")

    15	# Security Secret
    16	AUTH_PIN = os.getenv("ATRIA_AUTH_PIN", "atria2026")
    17	ACTIVE_SESSIONS = set()

    19	# ================= Global Trading State Engine =================
    20	class TradingState:
    21	    def __init__(self):
    22	        self.bot_status: str = "STOPPED"  # RUNNING, PAUSED, STOPPED
    23	        self.mode: str = "AUTO"           # AUTO, MANUAL, SEMI_AUTO
    24	        self.active_exchange: str = "Nobitex (Paper)"  # Nobitex, Binance, Wallex
    25	        self.balance_usdt: float = 10000.0
    26	        self.initial_balance: float = 10000.0
    27	        self.equity: float = 10000.0
    28	        self.vault_balance: float = 0.0
    29	        self.total_withdrawn: float = 0.0
    30	        self.current_symbol: str = "BTC/USDT"
    31	        self.current_price: float = 64250.0
    32	        self.leverage: int = 5
    33	        self.positions: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    34	        self.trades_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    35	        self.withdraw_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    36	        self.logs: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    37	        self.active_strategy: str = "ICT Macro & SMC OrderBlock"
    38	        self.risk_per_trade: float = 1.5  # 1.5%
    39	        self.chart_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    40	        self.last_signal: str = "SMC_BULLISH_SWEEP"
    41	        self.win_count: int = 0
    42	        self.loss_count: int = 0
    43	        
    44	        # Initialize Chart Seed Data
    45	        base_price = 63900.0
    46	        now = int(time.time())
    47	        for i in range(40):
    48	            t = now - (40 - i) * 60
    49	            base_price += random.uniform(-40, 45)
    50	            self.chart_history.append({"time": t, "price": round(base_price, 2)})
    51	        self.current_price = self.chart_history[-1]["price"]
    52	        self.add_log("System", "AtriaTrade Secured Core Active. Ready for Zero-Risk Simulated Testing.")
    53	
    54	    def add_log(self, level: str, message: str):
    55	        now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
    56	        self.logs.insert(0, {"time": now_str, "level": level, "msg": message})
    57	        if len(self.logs) > 60:
    58	            self.logs.pop()
    59	
    60	    def update_price(self):
    61	        delta = random.uniform(-35, 37)
    62	        self.current_price = round(max(100.0, self.current_price + delta), 2)
    63	        now = int(time.time())
    64	        self.chart_history.append({"time": now, "price": self.current_price})
    65	        if len(self.chart_history) > 60:
    66	            self.chart_history.pop(0)
    67	
    68	        # Update open position unrealized PnL with leverage
    69	        if "BTC/USDT" in self.positions:
    70	            pos = self.positions["BTC/USDT"]
    71	            entry = pos["entry_price"]
    72	            amt = pos["amount"]
    73	            side = pos["side"]
    74	            lev = pos.get("leverage", 1)
    75	            raw_diff = (self.current_price - entry) if side == "BUY" else (entry - self.current_price)
    76	            pos["unrealized_pnl"] = round(raw_diff * amt * lev, 2)
    77	            pos["pnl_pct"] = round((raw_diff / entry) * 100 * lev, 2)
    78	            self.equity = round(self.balance_usdt + pos["unrealized_pnl"], 2)
    79	        else:
    80	            self.equity = round(self.balance_usdt, 2)
    81	
    82	    def execute_market_order(self, symbol: str, side: str, amount: Optional[float] = None) -> bool:
    83	        if amount is None or amount <= 0:
    84	            cost = self.balance_usdt * (self.risk_per_trade / 100.0)
    85	            amount = round(cost / self.current_price, 4)
    86	
    87	        if amount <= 0:
    88	            self.add_log("Risk", "Order rejected: Amount too small.")
    89	            return False
    90	
    91	        cost = amount * self.current_price
    92	        if side == "BUY":
    93	            if self.balance_usdt < cost:
    94	                self.add_log("Risk", f"Order rejected: Insufficient Balance (${self.balance_usdt:.2f} < ${cost:.2f})")
    95	                return False
    96	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt - cost, 2)
    97	            self.positions[symbol] = {
    98	                "symbol": symbol,
    99	                "side": "BUY",
   100	                "amount": amount,
   101	                "leverage": self.leverage,
   102	                "entry_price": self.current_price,
   103	                "unrealized_pnl": 0.0,
   104	                "pnl_pct": 0.0,
   105	                "time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
   106	            }
   107	            self.add_log("Trade", f"Executed MARKET BUY {amount} {symbol} ({self.leverage}x) @ ${self.current_price:,.2f}")
   108	            return True
   109	        elif side == "SELL":
   110	            if symbol not in self.positions:
   111	                self.add_log("Trade", f"No open position to close for {symbol}")
   112	                return False
   113	            pos = self.positions.pop(symbol)
   114	            lev = pos.get("leverage", 1)
   115	            pnl = ((self.current_price - pos["entry_price"]) if pos["side"] == "BUY" else (pos["entry_price"] - self.current_price)) * pos["amount"] * lev
   116	            pnl = round(pnl, 2)
   117	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt + (pos["amount"] * pos["entry_price"]) + pnl, 2)
   118	            
   119	            # Safe Vault auto-transfer 25% of net profit
   120	            vault_cut = 0.0
   121	            if pnl > 0:
   122	                vault_cut = round(pnl * 0.25, 2)
   123	                self.vault_balance = round(self.vault_balance + vault_cut, 2)
   124	                self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt - vault_cut, 2)
   125	                self.win_count += 1
   126	            else:
   127	                self.loss_count += 1
   128	
   129	            trade_record = {
   130	                "id": len(self.trades_history) + 1,
   131	                "symbol": symbol,
   132	                "side": pos["side"],
   133	                "entry": pos["entry_price"],
   134	                "exit": self.current_price,
   135	                "amount": pos["amount"],
   136	                "pnl": pnl,
   137	                "vault_cut": vault_cut,
   138	                "pnl_pct": round((pnl / (pos["amount"] * pos["entry_price"])) * 100, 2),
   139	                "time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
   140	            }
   141	            self.trades_history.insert(0, trade_record)
   142	            if len(self.trades_history) > 30:
   143	                self.trades_history.pop()
   144	            
   145	            msg = f"Closed {symbol} Net PnL: ${pnl:+.2f} ({trade_record['pnl_pct']:+.2f}%)"
   146	            if vault_cut > 0:
   147	                msg += f" | Vault Reserve: +${vault_cut:.2f}"
   148	            self.add_log("Trade", msg)
   149	            return True
   150	        return False
   151	
   152	    def deposit_funds(self, amount: float):
   153	        if amount > 0:
   154	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt + amount, 2)
   155	            self.equity = round(self.equity + amount, 2)
   156	            self.add_log("Wallet", f"Deposit Confirmed: +${amount:,.2f} USDT")
   157	            return True
   158	        return False
   159	
   160	    def withdraw_funds(self, amount: float, target_address: str):
   161	        if amount <= 0:
   162	            return False, "Invalid amount"
   163	        if amount > self.vault_balance and amount > self.balance_usdt:
   164	            return False, "Insufficient balance"
   165	        
   166	        # Withdraw priority: from Vault first, then balance
   167	        if self.vault_balance >= amount:
   168	            self.vault_balance = round(self.vault_balance - amount, 2)
   169	        else:
   170	            rem = amount - self.vault_balance
   171	            self.vault_balance = 0.0
   172	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt - rem, 2)
   173	
   174	        self.total_withdrawn = round(self.total_withdrawn + amount, 2)
   175	        rec = {
   176	            "amount": amount,
   177	            "address": target_address[:6] + "..." + target_address[-4:] if len(target_address) > 10 else target_address,
   178	            "time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
   179	            "status": "COMPLETED (Simulated)"
   180	        }
   181	        self.withdraw_history.insert(0, rec)
   182	        self.add_log("Withdraw", f"Transferred ${amount:,.2f} USDT to {rec['address']}")
   183	        return True, "Success"
   184	
   185	    def panic_close_all(self):
   186	        symbols = list(self.positions.keys())
   187	        for sym in symbols:
   188	            self.execute_market_order(sym, "SELL")
   189	        self.bot_status = "STOPPED"
   190	        self.add_log("PANIC", "EMERGENCY PANIC TRIGGERED: All active positions liquidated immediately.")
   191	
   192	state = TradingState()
   193	
   194	# ================= Background Simulation Loop =================
   195	async def trading_engine_loop():
   196	    while True:
   197	        state.update_price()
   198	        if state.bot_status == "RUNNING" and state.mode in ["AUTO", "SEMI_AUTO"]:
   199	            roll = random.random()
   200	            if "BTC/USDT" not in state.positions:
   201	                if roll < 0.14:  # Entry Signal
   202	                    strategies = ["ICT Silver Bullet FVG", "SMC Liquidity Sweep", "OrderBlock Mitigation"]
   203	                    state.last_signal = f"STRONG BUY ({random.choice(strategies)})"
   204	                    state.execute_market_order("BTC/USDT", "BUY")
   205	            else:
   206	                pos = state.positions["BTC/USDT"]
   207	                # Take profit / Stop loss or Auto Trail
   208	                if pos["pnl_pct"] >= 2.2 or pos["pnl_pct"] <= -1.1 or roll < 0.09:
   209	                    state.last_signal = "TAKE PROFIT / EXIT TRIGGER"
   210	                    state.execute_market_order("BTC/USDT", "SELL")
   211	        await asyncio.sleep(2)
   212	
   213	@app.on_event("startup")
   214	async def startup_event():
   215	    asyncio.create_task(trading_engine_loop())

   283	# ================= Security & Auth Endpoints =================
   284	@app.post("/api/auth/login")
   285	def login(req: LoginRequest, response: Response):
   286	    if req.pin == AUTH_PIN:
   287	        token = secrets.token_hex(16)
   288	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.add(token)
   289	        response.set_cookie(key="atria_session", value=token, httponly=True, max_age=86400*7)
   290	        return {"status": "ok", "token": token}
   291	    return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"error": "Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª"})
   292	
   293	@app.get("/api/auth/check")
   294	def check_auth(atria_session: Optional[str] = Cookie(None)):
   295	    if atria_session and atria_session in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
   296	        return {"authenticated": True}
   297	    return {"authenticated": False}
   298	
   299	# ================= Management Endpoints =================
   300	@app.get("/api/status")
   301	def getadd_log("Engine", f"Bot started in {state.mode} on {state.active_exchange}.")
   302	    return {"status": "ok", "bot_status": state.bot_status}
   303	
   304	@app.post("/api/bot/stop")
   305	def stop_bot():
   306	    state.bot_status = "STOPPED"
   307	    state.add_log("Engine", "Bot paused by operator.")
   308	    return {"status": "ok", "bot_status": state.bot_status}
   309	
   310	@app.post("/api/bot/set_mode")
   311	def set_mode(req: ModeRequest):
   312	    if req.mode in ["AUTO", "MANUAL", "SEMI_AUTO"]:
   313	        state.mode = req.mode
   314	        state.add_log("Mode", f"Operation mode switched to {req.mode}")
   315	        return {"status": "ok", "mode": state.mode}
   316	    return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": "Invalid mode"})
   317	
   318	@app.post("/api/bot/config")
   319	def update_config(req: ConfigRequest):
   320	    if req.exchange: state.active_exchange = req.exchange
   321	    if req.strategy: state.active_strategy = req.strategy
   322	    if req.leverage: state.leverage = req.leverage
   323	    if req.risk: state.risk_per_trade = req.risk
   324	    state.add_log("Config", f"Config updated: Exch={state.active_exchange}, Lev={state.leverage}x, Strat={state.active_strategy}")
   325	    return {"status": "ok"}
   326	
   327	@app.post("/api/trade/market")
   328	def place_market_order(req: OrderRequest):
   329	    success = state.execute_market_order(req.symbol, req.side.upper(), req.amount)
   330	    return {"status": "ok" if success else "failed"}
   331	
   332	@app.post("/api/trade/panic")
   333	def panic_kill():
   334	    state.panic_close_all()
   335	    return {"status": "ok", "message": "All positions liquidated"}
   336	
   337	@app.post("/api/wallet/deposit")
   338	def wallet_deposit(req: DepositRequest):
   339	    ok = state.deposit_funds(req.amount)
   340	    return {"status": "ok" if ok else "failed"}
   341	
   342	@app.post("/api/wallet/withdraw")
   343	def wallet_withdraw(req: WithdrawRequest):
   344	    ok, msg = state.withdraw_funds(req.amount, req.address)
   345	    if ok:
   346	        return {"status": "ok", "message": msg}
   347	    return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": msg})

   702	<script>
   703	    let chartCanvas = document.getElementById('priceChart');
   704	    let ctx = chartCanvas.getContext('2d');
   705	
   706	    async function checkAuth() {
   707	        try {
   708	            const res = await fetch('/api/auth/check');
   709	            const data = await res.json();
   710	            if (data.authenticated) {
   711	                document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'none';
   712	            } else {
   713	                document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
   714	            }
   715	        } catch(e){}
   716	    }
   717	
   718	    async function doLogin() {
   719	        const pin = document.getElementById('pinInput').value;
   720	        const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
   721	            method: 'POST',
   722	            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   723	            body: JSON.stringify({pin: pin})
   724	        });
   725	        if (res.ok) {
   726	            document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'none';
   727	            fetchStatus();
   728	        } else {
   729	            document.getElementById('loginErr').innerText = 'Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª!';
   730	        }
   731	    }
   732	
   733	    function renderChart(history) {
   734	        if (!history || history.length < 2) return;
   735	        const width = chartCanvas.clientWidth;
   736	        const height = chartCanvas.clientHeight;
   737	        chartCanvas.width = width;
   738	        chartCanvas.height = height;
   739	
   740	        const prices = history.map(h => h.price);
   741	        const minP = Math.min(...prices) * 0.9995;
   742	        const maxP = Math.max(...prices) * 1.0005;
   743	
   744	        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
   745	
   746	        // Grid
   747	        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)";
   748	        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   749	        for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
   750	            let y = (height / 5) * i;
   751	            ctx.beginPath();
   752	            ctx.moveTo(0, y);
   753	            ctx.lineTo(width, y);
   754	            ctx.stroke();
   755	        }
   756	
   757	        // Line
   758	        ctx.beginPath();
   759	        history.forEach((pt, i) => {
   760	            let x = (i / (history.length - 1)) * width;
   761	            let y = height - ((pt.price - minP) / (maxP - minP)) * height;
   762	            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
   763	            else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
   764	        });
   765	
   766	        let grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
   767	        grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35)");
   768	        grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.0)");
   769	        ctx.strokeStyle = "#38bdf8";
   770	        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
   771	        ctx.stroke();
   772	
   773	        ctx.lineTo(width, height);
   774	        ctx.lineTo(0, height);
   775	        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
   776	        ctx.fill();
   777	    }
   778	
   779	    async function fetchStatus() {
   780	        try {
   781	            const res = await fetch('/api/status');
   782	            const data = await res.json();
   783	
   784	            // Status Badge
   785	            const dot = document.getElementById('statusDot');
   786	            const stText = document.getElementById('statusText');
   787	            if (data.bot_status === "RUNNING") {
   788	                dot.className = "status-dot dot-running";
   789	                stText.innerText = "RUNNING (" + data.mode + ")";
   790	            } else {
   791	                dot.className = "status-dot dot-stopped";
   792	                stText.innerText = data.bot_status;
   793	            }
   794	
   795	            document.getElementById('exchBadge').innerText = data.exchange;
   796	            document.getElementById('signalBadge').innerText = data.last_signal;
   797	            document.getElementById('metricBalance').innerText = data.balance_usdt.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
   798	            document.getElementById('metricEquity').innerText = data.equity.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
   799	            document.getElementById('metricVault').innerText = data.vault_balance.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
   800	            document.getElementById('metricWithdrawn').innerText = data.total_withdrawn.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
   801	            
   802	            const pnlEl = document.getElementById('metricPnL');
   803	            pnlEl.innerText = (data.total_pnl >= 0 ? "+" : "") + data.total_pnl.toFixed(2) + " USDT";
   804	            pnlEl.style.color = data.total_pnl >= 0 ? "var(--accent-green)" : "var(--accent-red)";
   805	            
   806	            document.getElementById('metricWinRate').innerText = data.win_rate + "% (" + data.total_trades + " T)";
   807	            document.getElementById('livePrice').innerText = "$" + data.current_price.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
   808	
   809	            // Table
   810	            const tbody = document.getElementById('tradesBody');
   811	            let rows = "";
   812	            for (const [sym, pos] of Object.entries(data.positions)) {
   813	                rows += `<tr style="background: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.12);">
   814	                    <td style="font-weight:700;">${sym} (${pos.leverage || 1}x)</td>
   815	                    <td style="color:#60a5fa;">${pos.side}</td>
   816	                    <td>$${pos.entry_price.toFixed(2)}</td>
   817	                    <td>-</td>
   818	                    <td class="${pos.unrealized_pnl >= 0 ? 'pnl-pos' : 'pnl-neg'}">${pos.unrealized_pnl >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${pos.unrealized_pnl} (${pos.pnl_pct}%)</td>
   819	                    <td>${pos.time}</td>
   820	                </tr>`;
   821	            }
   822	            data.trades.forEach(tr => {
   823	                rows += `<tr>
   824	                    <td>${tr.symbol}</td>
   825	                    <td>${tr.side}</td>
   826	                    <td>$${tr.entry.toFixed(2)}</td>
   827	                    <td>$${tr.exit.toFixed(2)}</td>
   828	                    <td class="${tr.pnl >= 0 ? 'pnl-pos' : 'pnl-neg'}">${tr.pnl >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${tr.pnl} (${tr.pnl_pct}%)</td>
   829	                    <td>${tr.time}</td>
   830	                </tr>`;
   831	            });
   832	            tbody.innerHTML = rows || `<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted);">Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ Ø§Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ù… Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡</td></tr>`;
   833	
   834	            // Logs
   835	            const logsBox = document.getElementById('logsBox');
   836	            logsBox.innerHTML = data.logs.map(l => `
   837	                <div class="log-item">
   838	                    <span class="log-time">${l.time}</span>
   839	                    <span class="log-lvl lvl-${l.level.toLowerCase()}">${l.level}</span>
   840	                    <span>${l.msg}</span>
   841	                </div>
   842	            `).join('');
   843	
   844	            // Chart
   845	            renderChart(data.chart);
   846	        } catch (e) {
   847	            console.error(e);
   848	        }
   849	    }
   850	
   851	    async function startBot() { await fetch('/api/bot/start', {method: 'POST'}); fetchStatus(); }
   852	    async function stopBot() { await fetch('/api/bot/stop', {method: 'POST'}); fetchStatus(); }
   853	    async function panicKill() { await fetch('/api/trade/panic', {method: 'POST'}); fetchStatus(); }
   854	    async function setMode(mode) {
   855	        await fetch('/api/bot/set_mode', {
   856	            method: 'POST',
   857	            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   858	            body: JSON.stringify({mode: mode})
   859	        });
   860	        document.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
   861	        event.target.classList.add('active');
   862	        fetchStatus();
   863	    }
   864	    async function marketOrder(side) {
   865	        await fetch('/api/trade/market', {
   866	            method: 'POST',
   867	            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   868	            body: JSON.stringify({symbol: "BTC/USDT", side: side})
   869	        });
   870	        fetchStatus();
   871	    }
   872	    async function updateConfig() {
   873	        const exch = document.getElementById('selectExchange').value;
   874	        const lev = parseInt(document.getElementById('selectLeverage').value);
   875	        await fetch('/api/bot/config', {
   876	            method: 'POST',
   877	            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   878	            body: JSON.stringify({exchange: exch, leverage: lev})
   879	        });
   880	        fetchStatus();
   881	    }
   882	    async function doDeposit() {
   883	        const amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('depositAmt').value);
   884	        if (amt > 0) {
   885	            await fetch('/api/wallet/deposit', {
   886	                method: 'POST',
   887	                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   888	                body: JSON.stringify({amount: amt})
   889	            });
   890	            fetchStatus();
   891	        }
   892	    }
   893	    async function doWithdraw() {
   894	        const amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('withdrawAmt').value);
   895	        const addr = document.getElementById('withdrawAddr').value;
   896	        if (amt > 0 && addr) {
   897	            await fetch('/api/wallet/withdraw', {
   898	                method: 'POST',
   899	                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   900	                body: JSON.stringify({amount: amt, address: addr})
   901	            });
   902	            fetchStatus();
   903	        }
   904	    }
   905	
   906	    checkAuth();
   907	    setInterval(fetchStatus, 1500);
   908	    fetchStatus();
   909	</script>

   912	"""
   913	
   914	===== app.py.backup_20260905_112213 =====
   915	import asyncio
   916	import os
   917	import random
   918	import time
   919	import secrets
   920	from datetime import datetime
   921	from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
   922	from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, BackgroundTasks, HTTPException, Response, Cookie
   923	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse
   924	from pydantic import BaseModel
   925	
   926	app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade Master Secured Cockpit", version="2.5.0")
   927	
   928	# Security Secret
   929	AUTH_PIN = os.getenv("ATRIA_AUTH_PIN", "atria2026")
   930	ACTIVE_SESSIONS = set()
   931	
   932	# ================= Global Trading State Engine =================
   933	class TradingState:
   934	    def __init__(self):
   935	        self.bot_status: str = "STOPPED"  # RUNNING, PAUSED, STOPPED
   936	        self.mode: str = "AUTO"           # AUTO, MANUAL, SEMI_AUTO
   937	        self.active_exchange: str = "Nobitex (Paper)"  # Nobitex, Binance, Wallex
   938	        self.balance_usdt: float = 10000.0
   939	        self.initial_balance: float = 10000.0
   940	        self.equity: float = 10000.0
   941	        self.vault_balance: float = 0.0
   942	        self.total_withdrawn: float = 0.0
   943	        self.current_symbol: str = "BTC/USDT"
   944	        self.current_price: float = 64250.0
   945	        self.leverage: int = 5
   946	        self.positions: Dict[str, Any] = {}
   947	        self.trades_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   948	        self.withdraw_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   949	        self.logs: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
   950	        self.active_strategy: str = "ICT Macro & SMC OrderBlock"
   951	        self.risk_per_trade: float = 1.5  # 1.5%
   952	        self.chart_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   953	        self.last_signal: str = "SMC_BULLISH_SWEEP"
   954	        self.win_count: int = 0
   955	        self.loss_count: int = 0
   956	        
   957	        # Initialize Chart Seed Data
   958	        base_price = 63900.0
   959	        now = int(time.time())
   960	        for i in range(40):
   961	            t = now - (40 - i) * 60
   962	            base_price += random.uniform(-40, 45)
   963	            self.chart_history.append({"time": t, "price": round(base_price, 2)})
   964	        self.current_price = self.chart_history[-1]["price"]
   965	        self.add_log("System", "AtriaTrade Secured Core Active. Ready for Zero-Risk Simulated Testing.")
   966	
   967	    def add_log(self, level: str, message: str):
   968	        now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
   969	        self.logs.insert(0, {"time": now_str, "level": level, "msg": message})
   970	        if len(self.logs) > 60:
   971	            self.logs.pop()
   972	
   973	    def update_price(self):
   974	        delta = random.uniform(-35, 37)
   975	        self.current_price = round(max(100.0, self.current_price + delta), 2)
   976	        now = int(time.time())
   977	        self.chart_history.append({"time": now, "price": self.current_price})
   978	        if len(self.chart_history) > 60:
   979	            self.chart_history.pop(0)
   980	
   981	        # Update open position unrealized PnL with leverage
   982	        if "BTC/USDT" in self.positions:
   983	            pos = self.positions["BTC/USDT"]
   984	            entry = pos["entry_price"]
   985	            amt = pos["amount"]
   986	            side = pos["side"]
   987	            lev = pos.get("leverage", 1)
   988	            raw_diff = (self.current_price - entry) if side == "BUY" else (entry - self.current_price)
   989	            pos["unrealized_pnl"] = round(raw_diff * amt * lev, 2)
   990	            pos["pnl_pct"] = round((raw_diff / entry) * 100 * lev, 2)
   991	            self.equity = round(self.balance_usdt + pos["unrealized_pnl"], 2)
   992	        else:
   993	            self.equity = round(self.balance_usdt, 2)
   994	
   995	    def execute_market_order(self, symbol: str, side: str, amount: Optional[float] = None) -> bool:
   996	        if amount is None or amount <= 0:
   997	            cost = self.balance_usdt * (self.risk_per_trade / 100.0)
   998	            amount = round(cost / self.current_price, 4)
   999	
  1000	        if amount <= 0:
  1001	            self.add_log("Risk", "Order rejected: Amount too small.")
  1002	            return False
  1003	
  1004	        cost = amount * self.current_price
  1005	        if side == "BUY":
  1006	            if self.balance_usdt < cost:
  1007	                self.add_log("Risk", f"Order rejected: Insufficient Balance (${self.balance_usdt:.2f} < ${cost:.2f})")
  1008	                return False
  1009	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt - cost, 2)
  1010	            self.positions[symbol] = {
  1011	                "symbol": symbol,
  1012	                "side": "BUY",
  1013	                "amount": amount,
  1014	                "leverage": self.leverage,
  1015	                "entry_price": self.current_price,
  1016	                "unrealized_pnl": 0.0,
  1017	                "pnl_pct": 0.0,
  1018	                "time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
  1019	            }
  1020	            self.add_log("Trade", f"Executed MARKET BUY {amount} {symbol} ({self.leverage}x) @ ${self.current_price:,.2f}")
  1021	            return True
  1022	        elif side == "SELL":
  1023	            if symbol not in self.positions:
  1024	                self.add_log("Trade", f"No open position to close for {symbol}")
  1025	                return False
  1026	            pos = self.positions.pop(symbol)
  1027	            lev = pos.get("leverage", 1)
  1028	            pnl = ((self.current_price - pos["entry_price"]) if pos["side"] == "BUY" else (pos["entry_price"] - self.current_price)) * pos["amount"] * lev
  1029	            pnl = round(pnl, 2)
  1030	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt + (pos["amount"] * pos["entry_price"]) + pnl, 2)
  1031	            
  1032	            # Safe Vault auto-transfer 25% of net profit
  1033	            vault_cut = 0.0
  1034	            if pnl > 0:
  1035	                vault_cut = round(pnl * 0.25, 2)
  1036	                self.vault_balance = round(self.vault_balance + vault_cut, 2)
  1037	                self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt - vault_cut, 2)
  1038	                self.win_count += 1
  1039	            else:
  1040	                self.loss_count += 1
  1041	
  1042	            trade_record = {
  1043	                "id": len(self.trades_history) + 1,
  1044	                "symbol": symbol,
  1045	                "side": pos["side"],
  1046	                "entry": pos["entry_price"],
  1047	                "exit": self.current_price,
  1048	                "amount": pos["amount"],
  1049	                "pnl": pnl,
  1050	                "vault_cut": vault_cut,
  1051	                "pnl_pct": round((pnl / (pos["amount"] * pos["entry_price"])) * 100, 2),
  1052	                "time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
  1053	            }
  1054	            self.trades_history.insert(0, trade_record)
  1055	            if len(self.trades_history) > 30:
  1056	                self.trades_history.pop()
  1057	            
  1058	            msg = f"Closed {symbol} Net PnL: ${pnl:+.2f} ({trade_record['pnl_pct']:+.2f}%)"
  1059	            if vault_cut > 0:
  1060	                msg += f" | Vault Reserve: +${vault_cut:.2f}"
  1061	            self.add_log("Trade", msg)
  1062	            return True
  1063	        return False
  1064	
  1065	    def deposit_funds(self, amount: float):
  1066	        if amount > 0:
  1067	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt + amount, 2)
  1068	            self.equity = round(self.equity + amount, 2)
  1069	            self.add_log("Wallet", f"Deposit Confirmed: +${amount:,.2f} USDT")
  1070	            return True
  1071	        return False
  1072	
  1073	    def withdraw_funds(self, amount: float, target_address: str):
  1074	        if amount <= 0:
  1075	            return False, "Invalid amount"
  1076	        if amount > self.vault_balance and amount > self.balance_usdt:
  1077	            return False, "Insufficient balance"
  1078	        
  1079	        # Withdraw priority: from Vault first, then balance
  1080	        if self.vault_balance >= amount:
  1081	            self.vault_balance = round(self.vault_balance - amount, 2)
  1082	        else:
  1083	            rem = amount - self.vault_balance
  1084	            self.vault_balance = 0.0
  1085	            self.balance_usdt = round(self.balance_usdt - rem, 2)
  1086	
  1087	        self.total_withdrawn = round(self.total_withdrawn + amount, 2)
  1088	        rec = {
  1089	            "amount": amount,
  1090	            "address": target_address[:6] + "..." + target_address[-4:] if len(target_address) > 10 else target_address,
  1091	            "time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
  1092	            "status": "COMPLETED (Simulated)"
  1093	        }
  1094	        self.withdraw_history.insert(0, rec)
  1095	        self.add_log("Withdraw", f"Transferred ${amount:,.2f} USDT to {rec['address']}")
  1096	        return True, "Success"
  1097	
  1098	    def panic_close_all(self):
  1099	        symbols = list(self.positions.keys())
  1100	        for sym in symbols:
  1101	            self.execute_market_order(sym, "SELL")
  1102	        self.bot_status = "STOPPED"
  1103	        self.add_log("PANIC", "EMERGENCY PANIC TRIGGERED: All active positions liquidated immediately.")
  1104	
  1105	state = TradingState()
  1106	
  1107	# ================= Background Simulation Loop =================
  1108	async def trading_engine_loop():
  1109	    while True:
  1110	        state.update_price()
  1111	        if state.bot_status == "RUNNING" and state.mode in ["AUTO", "SEMI_AUTO"]:
  1112	            roll = random.random()
  1113	            if "BTC/USDT" not in state.positions:
  1114	                if roll < 0.14:  # Entry Signal
  1115	                    strategies = ["ICT Silver Bullet FVG", "SMC Liquidity Sweep", "OrderBlock Mitigation"]
  1116	                    state.last_signal = f"STRONG BUY ({random.choice(strategies)})"
  1117	                    state.execute_market_order("BTC/USDT", "BUY")
  1118	            else:
  1119	                pos = state.positions["BTC/USDT"]
  1120	                # Take profit / Stop loss or Auto Trail
  1121	                if pos["pnl_pct"] >= 2.2 or pos["pnl_pct"] <= -1.1 or roll < 0.09:
  1122	                    state.last_signal = "TAKE PROFIT / EXIT TRIGGER"
  1123	                    state.execute_market_order("BTC/USDT", "SELL")
  1124	        await asyncio.sleep(2)
  1125	
  1126	@app.on_event("startup")
  1127	async def startup_event():
  1128	    asyncio.create_task(trading_engine_loop())

  1208	    if atria_session and atria_session in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
  1209	        return {"authenticated": True}
  1210	    return {"authenticated": False}

  1228	        return {"status": "ok", "mode": state.mode}

  1231	@app.post("/api/bot/config")
  1232	def update_config(req: ConfigRequest):
  1233	    if req.exchange: state.active_exchange = req.exchange
  1234	    if req.strategy: state.active_strategy = req.strategy
  1235	    if req.leverage: state.leverage = req.leverage
  1236	    if req.risk: state.risk_per_trade = req.risk
  1237	    state.add_log("Config", f"Config updated: Exch={state.active_exchange}, Lev={state.leverage}x, Strat={state.active_strategy}")
  1238	    return {"status": "ok"}

  1615	<script>
  1616	    let chartCanvas = document.getElementById('priceChart');
  1617	    let ctx = chartCanvas.getContext('2d');
  1618	
  1619	    async function checkAuth() {
  1620	        try {
  1621	            const res = await fetch('/api/auth/check');
  1622	            const data = await res.json();
  1623	            if (data.authenticated) {
  1624	                document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'none';
  1625	            } else {
  1626	                document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
  1627	            }
  1628	        } catch(e){}
  1629	    }
  1630	
  1631	    async function doLogin() {
  1632	        const pin = document.getElementById('pinInput').value;
  1633	        const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
  1634	            method: 'POST',
  1635	            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
  1636	            body: JSON.stringify({pin: pin})
  1637	        });
  1638	        if (res.ok) {
  1639	            document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'none';
  1640	            fetchStatus();
  1641	        } else {
  1642	            document.getElementById('loginErr').innerText = 'Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª!';
  1643	        }
  1644	    }
  1645	
  1646	    function renderChart(history) {
  1647	        if (!history || history.length < 2) return;
  1648	        const width = chartCanvas.clientWidth;
  1649	        const height = chartCanvas.clientHeight;
  1650	        chartCanvas.width = width;
  1651	        chartCanvas.height = height;
  1652	
  1653	        const prices = history.map(h => h.price);
  1654	        const minP = Math.min(...prices) * 0.9995;
  1655	        const maxP = Math.max(...prices) * 1.0005;
  1656	
  1657	        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
  1658	
  1659	        // Grid
  1660	        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)";
  1661	        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  1662	        for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
  1663	            let y = (height / 5) * i;
  1664	            ctx.beginPath();
  1665	            ctx.moveTo(0, y);
  1666	            ctx.lineTo(width, y);
  1667	            ctx.stroke();
  1668	        }
  1669	
  1670	        // Line
  1671	        ctx.beginPath();
  1672	        history.forEach((pt, i) => {
  1673	            let x = (i / (history.length - 1)) * width;
  1674	            let y = height - ((pt.price - minP) / (maxP - minP)) * height;
  1675	            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
  1676	            else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
  1677	        });
  1678	
  1679	        let grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
  1680	        grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35)");
  1681	        grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.0)");
  1682	        ctx.strokeStyle = "#38bdf8";
  1683	        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  1684	        ctx.stroke();
  1685	
  1686	        ctx.lineTo(width, height);
  1687	        ctx.lineTo(0, height);
  1688	        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  1689	        ctx.fill();
  1690	    }
  1691	
  1692	    async function fetchStatus() {
  1693	        try {
  1694	            const res = await fetch('/api/status');
  1695	            const data = await res.json();
  1696	
  1697	            // Status Badge
  1698	            const dot = document.getElementById('statusDot');
  1699	            const stText = document.getElementById('statusText');
  1700	            if (data.bot_status === "RUNNING") {
  1701	                dot.className = "status-dot dot-running";
  1702	                stText.innerText = "RUNNING (" + data.mode + ")";
  1703	            } else {
  1704	                dot.className = "status-dot dot-stopped";
  1705	                stText.innerText = data.bot_status;
  1706	            }
  1707	
  1708	            document.getElementById('exchBadge').innerText = data.exchange;

  1799	                method: 'POST',
  1800	                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
  1801	                body: JSON.stringify({amount: amt})
  1802	            });

  1811	                method: 'POST',
  1812	                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
  1813	                body: JSON.stringify({amount: amt, address: addr})
  1814	            });
  1815	            fetchStatus();
  1816	        }
  1817	    }

  1829	from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, Form, Depends, HTTPException, status
  1830	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse, JSONResponse
  1831	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
  1832	from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
  1833	import uvicorn
  1834	import os
  1835	
  1836	app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade Web Console")
  1837	
  1838	# Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª ØªÙˆÚ©Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø³Ø´Ù† Ø³Ø§Ø¯Ù‡
  1839	ACTIVE_SESSIONS = set()
  1840	ADMIN_PASSWORD = "mehdi1234"
  1841	
  1842	# ØªØ¹Ø±ÛŒÙ Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± ØªÙ…Ù¾Ù„ÛŒØªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§

  1844	templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=os.path.join(current_dir, "templates"))
  1845	
  1846	# ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ù…Ø§Ú© Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ³Øª Ø§ÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙ‡
  1847	bot_status = {
  1848	    "is_running": False,
  1849	    "active_strategy": "SMC + Momentum Engine",
  1850	    "market": "BTC/USDT",
  1851	    "balance_usdt": 1000.0,
  1852	    "profit_reserve": 0.0,
  1853	    "current_pnl": 0.0,
  1854	    "trade_mode": "Paper Trading (Sandbox)",
  1855	    "logs": [
  1856	        "[SYSTEM] Security layer initialized.",
  1857	        "[AUTH] Console locked. Awaiting admin session."
  1858	    ]
  1859	}
  1860	
  1861	def check_auth(request: Request):
  1862	    session_token = request.cookies.get("session_token")
  1863	    if not session_token or session_token not in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
  1864	        raise HTTPException(
  1865	            status_code=status.HTTP_307_TEMPORARY_REDIRECT,
  1866	            headers={"Location": "/login"}
  1867	        )
  1868	    return True
  1869	
  1870	@app.get("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
  1871	async def login_page(request: Request):
  1872	    return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {"request": request, "error": None})
  1873	
  1874	@app.post("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
  1875	async def handle_login(request: Request, password: str = Form(...)):
  1876	    if password == ADMIN_PASSWORD:
  1877	        token = secrets.token_hex(16)
  1878	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.add(token)
  1879	        bot_status["logs"].append("[AUTH] Admin successfully authenticated.")
  1880	        response = RedirectResponse(url="/", status_code=status.HTTP_302_FOUND)
  1881	        response.set_cookie(key="session_token", value=token, httponly=True)
  1882	        return response
  1883	    else:
  1884	        return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {
  1885	            "request": request, 
  1886	            "error": "Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª! Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ ØºÛŒØ±Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯."
  1887	        })
  1888	
  1889	@app.get("/logout")
  1890	async def logout(request: Request):
  1891	    session_token = request.cookies.get("session_token")
  1892	    if session_token in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
  1893	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.remove(session_token)
  1894	    response = RedirectResponse(url="/login")
  1895	    response.delete_cookie("session_token")
  1896	    return response
  1897	
  1898	@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
  1899	async def dashboard(request: Request, authenticated: bool = Depends(check_auth)):
  1900	    return templates.TemplateResponse("dashboard.html", {
  1901	        "request": request,
  1902	        "status": bot_status

  1909	@app.post("/api/bot/start")
  1910	async def start_bot(authenticated: bool = Depends(check_auth)):
  1911	    bot_status["is_running"] = True
  1912	    bot_status["logs"].append("[ENGINE] Bot execution started by Admin.")

  1925	import os
  1926	from pathlib import Path
  1927	from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
  1928	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
  1929	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
  1930	from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
  1931	from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
  1932	
  1933	# Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ±Ø¯Ù‡ÛŒ Ù¾ÙˆÛŒØ§ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Termux
  1934	WEB_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  1935	TEMPLATES_DIR = WEB_DIR / "templates"
  1936	STATIC_DIR = WEB_DIR / "static"
  1937	
  1938	app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade Dashboard")
  1939	
  1940	app.add_middleware(
  1941	    CORSMiddleware,
  1942	    allow_origins=["*"],
  1943	    allow_credentials=True,
  1944	    allow_methods=["*"],
  1945	    allow_headers=["*"],
  1946	)
  1947	
  1948	# mount static files
  1949	app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(STATIC_DIR)), name="static")
  1950	templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(TEMPLATES_DIR))
  1951	
  1952	@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
  1953	async def dashboard(request: Request):
  1954	    return templates.TemplateResponse("index.html", {"request": request, "status": "Paper Mode (Safe)"})
  1955	
  1956	@app.get("/health")
  1957	async def health_check():
  1958	    return {"status": "ok", "mode": "Paper Trading", "security": "No Real API Keys"}

     3	     1	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     4	     2	"""
     5	     3	FastAPI application for AtriaTrade Dashboard.
     6	     4	Integrates DashboardServer for bot telemetry and control.
     7	     5	This file serves as the main entry point for the web API.
     8	     6	"""
     9	     7	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, Depends
    10	     8	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
    11	     9	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    12	    10	from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
    13	    11	import uvicorn
    14	    12	import os
    15	    13	import time # Added for timestamp in public status
    16	    14	
    17	    15	# --- Project Imports ---
    18	    16	# Assuming the structure:
    19	    17	# AtriaTrade/
    20	    18	# â”œâ”€â”€ src/
    21	    19	# â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ dashboard/
    22	    20	# â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ server.py
    23	    21	# â”‚   â”‚   â””â”€â”€ auth.py
    24	    22	# â”‚   â””â”€â”€ ... (other modules like core, strategy, etc.)
    25	    23	# â””â”€â”€ venv/
    26	    24	
    27	    25	# Attempt to import necessary components, providing guidance if they are missing.
    28	    26	try:
    29	    27	    from src.dashboard.server import DashboardServer
    30	    28	    from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
    31	    29	except ImportError as e:
    32	    30	    print(f"âŒ ImportError: Could not import dashboard components. {e}")
    33	    31	    print("   Please ensure:")
    34	    32	    print("   1. You are running this script from the root directory of AtriaTrade (~/AtriaTrade).")
    35	    33	    print("   2. The directory structure includes src/dashboard/server.py and src/dashboard/auth.py.")
    36	    34	    exit(1) # Exit if essential components are not found
    37	    35	
    38	    36	
    39	    37	# --- Configuration ---
    40	    38	# Define the name of the header used for authentication tokens.
    41	    39	AUTH_TOKEN_HEADER_NAME = "X-Auth-Token"
    42	    40	
    43	    41	# Placeholder for an administrator token.
    44	    42	# IMPORTANT: Replace 'SET_YOUR_ADMIN_TOKEN_HERE' with a securely generated token.
    45	    43	# You can generate one using the Python console:
    46	    44	# >>> from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
    47	    45	# >>> auth_manager = SecureAuthManager()
    48	    46	# >>> print(auth_manager.generate_token_for_user('admin_user', role='admin'))
    49	    47	ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST = "SET_YOUR_ADMIN_TOKEN_HERE" # <<<--- !!! REPLACE THIS !!!
    50	    48	
    51	    49	# --- Initialization ---
    52	    50	# Initialize the authentication manager.
    53	    51	# Default settings: 24-hour token validity, 5 failed login attempts before lockout, 15-minute lockout duration.
    54	    52	auth_manager = SecureAuthManager()
    55	    53	
    56	    54	# Initialize the dashboard server, injecting the authentication manager.
    57	    55	dashboard_server = DashboardServer(auth_manager=auth_manager)
    58	    56	
    59	    57	# --- FastAPI Application Setup ---
    60	    58	app = FastAPI(
    61	    59	    title="AtriaTrade Dashboard API",
    62	    60	    description="API for managing and monitoring the AtriaTrade bot. Provides real-time data and control capabilities.",
    63	    61	    version="1.1.0",
    64	    62	    contact={
    65	    63	        "name": "AtriaTrade Support Team",
    66	    64	        "url": "https://atria.trade/support",
    67	    65	        "email": "support@atria.trade",
    68	    66	    },
    69	    67	    license_info={
    70	    68	        "name": "MIT License",
    71	    69	        "url": "https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT",
    72	    70	    },
    73	    71	)
    74	    72	
    75	    73	# --- Security Dependencies ---
    76	    74	# Configure FastAPI's API Key header dependency.
    77	    75	api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name=AUTH_TOKEN_HEADER_NAME, auto_error=True)
    78	    76	
    79	    77	# --- Utility Functions ---
    80	    78	def get_client_ip(request: Request) -> str:
    81	    79	    """
    82	    80	    Retrieves the client's IP address from the request.
    83	    81	    Handles common proxy headers like X-Forwarded-For for accurate IP logging.
    84	    82	    """
    85	    83	    if request.client and request.client.host:
    86	    84	        return request.client.host
    87	    85	    
    88	    86	    forwarded_for = request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For")
    89	    87	    if forwarded_for:
    90	    88	        return forwarded_for.split(",")[0].strip()
    91	    89	        
    92	    90	    return "127.0.0.1"
    93	    91	
    94	    92	
    95	    93	# --- Pydantic Models for Request/Response Data Validation ---
    96	    94	
    97	    95	class PublicStatusResponse(BaseModel):
    98	    96	    """Response model for the public status endpoint."""
    99	    97	    system: str = Field(..., description="Name of the system component (e.g., 'AtriaTrade API').")
   100	    98	    online: bool = Field(..., description="Indicates if the system component is operational.")
   101	    99	    timestamp: float = Field(..., description="Unix timestamp of when the status was checked.")
   102	   100	
   103	   101	class BotStateData(BaseModel):
   104	   102	    """Detailed data model for the bot's current operational state."""
   105	   103	    status: str = Field(..., description="Current status of the bot (e.g., RUNNING, STOPPED, PAUSED, ERROR).")
   106	   104	    mode: str = Field(..., description="Trading mode (e.g., PAPER_TRADING, TESTNET, LIVE_TRADING).")
   107	   105	    active_positions: int = Field(..., description="Number of currently active trading positions.")
   108	   106	    total_balance_usdt: float = Field(..., description="Total account balance denominated in USDT.")
   109	   107	    profit_reserved: float = Field(..., description="Amount of profit currently reserved or set aside in USDT.")
   110	   108	
   111	   109	class DashboardSummaryResponse(BaseModel):
   112	   110	    """Comprehensive response model for the authenticated dashboard summary."""
   113	   111	    success: bool = Field(..., description="Indicates if the request was processed successfully.")
   114	   112	    user: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Username of the authenticated user.")
   115	   113	    role: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Role assigned to the user (e.g., 'admin', 'trader', 'viewer').")
   116	   114	    data: Optional[BotStateData] = Field(None, description="Detailed bot state information, included only on success.")
   117	   115	    error: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Error message if the operation failed (e.g., authentication, authorization).")
   118	   116	
   119	   117	class UpdateBotStateResponse(BaseModel):
   120	   118	    """Response model for updating bot state parameters."""
   121	   119	    success: bool = Field(..., description="Indicates if the state update was successful.")
   122	   120	    updated_key: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="The specific key that was modified.")
   123	   121	    new_value: Optional[Any] = Field(None, description="The new value assigned to the updated key.")
   124	   122	    error: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Error message if the update failed.")
   125	   123	
   126	   124	# --- API Endpoints Definition ---
   127	   125	
   128	   126	@app.get(
   129	   127	    "/api/public/status",
   130	   128	    response_model=PublicStatusResponse,
   131	   129	    summary="Get Public System Status",
   132	   130	    tags=["Public Endpoints"],
   133	   131	    description="Provides a simple, unauthenticated health check for the AtriaTrade API service."
   134	   132	)
   135	   133	async def get_public_status_endpoint(request: Request): # CORRECTED: Removed `= Request(...)`
   136	   134	    """
   137	   135	    Public endpoint to check if the API service is running.
   138	   136	    """
   139	   137	    return PublicStatusResponse(
   140	   138	        system="AtriaTrade API",
   141	   139	        online=True,
   142	   140	        timestamp=time.time()
   143	   141	    )
   144	   142	
   145	   143	@app.get(
   146	   144	    "/api/dashboard/summary",
   147	   145	    response_model=DashboardSummaryResponse,
   148	   146	    summary="Get Authenticated Dashboard Summary",
   149	   147	    tags=["Dashboard API"],
   150	   148	    dependencies=[Depends(api_key_header)],
   151	   149	    description="Retrieves detailed bot status, user information, and trading data. Requires a valid authentication token."
   152	   150	)
   153	   151	async def get_dashboard_summary_endpoint(
   154	   152	    request: Request,
   155	   153	    token: str = Depends(api_key_header),
   156	   154	):
   157	   155	    """
   158	   156	    Fetches the main dashboard summary data. Requires authentication via `X-Auth-Token` header.
   159	   157	    Returns user details and bot operational state if the token is valid.
   160	   158	    """
   161	   159	    client_ip = get_client_ip(request)
   162	   160	    result = await dashboard_server.get_dashboard_summary(token=token, client_ip=client_ip)
   163	   161	
   164	   162	    if not result.get("success", False):
   165	   163	        raise HTTPException(
   166	   164	            status_code=401,
   167	   165	            detail={"message": result.get("error", "Authentication failed. Invalid or expired token.")}
   168	   166	        )
   169	   167	
   170	   168	    bot_data = None
   171	   169	    if "data" in result and result["data"]:
   172	   170	        try:
   173	   171	            bot_data = BotStateData(**result["data"])
   174	   172	        except Exception as e:
   175	   173	            print(f"Error parsing BotStateData: {e}")
   176	   174	            raise HTTPException(
   177	   175	                status_code=500,
   178	   176	                detail={"message": "Internal server error processing dashboard data."}
   179	   177	            )
   180	   178	
   181	   179	    return DashboardSummaryResponse(
   182	   180	        success=True,
   183	   181	        user=result.get("user"),
   184	   182	        role=result.get("role"),
   185	   183	        data=bot_data
   186	   184	    )
   187	   185	
   188	   186	@app.put(
   189	   187	    "/api/bot/state",
   190	   188	    response_model=UpdateBotStateResponse,
   191	   189	    summary="Update Bot State Parameter (Admin Only)",
   192	   190	    tags=["Admin Control"],
   193	   191	    dependencies=[Depends(api_key_header)],
   194	   192	    description="Allows authenticated administrators to modify specific bot state parameters dynamically. Requires 'admin' role."
   195	   193	)
   196	   194	async def update_bot_state_endpoint(
   197	   195	    request: Request,
   198	   196	    key: str,
   199	   197	    value: Any,
   200	   198	    token: str = Depends(api_key_header),
   201	   199	):
   202	   200	    """
   203	   201	    Endpoint to update a specific parameter in the bot's state.
   204	   202	    Requires authentication and the user must have admin privileges.
   205	   203	    Example Usage: PUT /api/bot/state?key=mode&value="LIVE_TRADING"
   206	   204	    """
   207	   205	    client_ip = get_client_ip(request)
   208	   206	    result = await dashboard_server.update_bot_state(key=key, value=value, token=token, client_ip=client_ip)
   209	   207	
   210	   208	    if not result.get("success", False):
   211	   209	        error_message = result.get("error", "Unknown error during state update.")
   212	   210	        status_code = 403
   213	   211	
   214	   212	        if "Unauthorized" in error_message or "Invalid token" in error_message:
   215	   213	            status_code = 401
   216	   214	        elif "Not an administrator" in error_message or "permission denied" in error_message:
   217	   215	            status_code = 403
   218	   216	        else:
   219	   217	            status_code = 500
   220	   218	
   221	   219	        raise HTTPException(status_code=status_code, detail={"message": error_message})
   222	   220	
   223	   221	    return UpdateBotStateResponse(
   224	   222	        success=True,
   225	   223	        updated_key=result.get("updated_key"),
   226	   224	        new_value=result.get("new_value")
   227	   225	    )
   228	   226	
   229	   227	# --- Main Execution Block for Development Server ---
   230	   228	if __name__ == "__main__":
   231	   229	    HOST = "127.0.0.1"
   232	   230	    PORT = 8080
   233	   231	
   234	   232	    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
   235	   233	    print("ğŸš€ Starting AtriaTrade Dashboard API Server...")
   236	   234	    print(f"   Host: {HOST}")
   237	   235	    print(f"   Port: {PORT}")
   238	   236	    print(f"   Access API Docs at: http://{HOST}:{PORT}/docs")
   239	   237	    print("   Access ReDoc at: http://{HOST}:{PORT}/redoc")
   240	   238	    print("=" * 60 + "\n")
   241	   239	    
   242	   240	    print("ğŸ’¡ Quick Tip for Generating Admin Tokens:")
   243	   241	    print("   1. Open a Python console in Termux:")
   244	   242	    print("      source ~/AtriaTrade/venv/bin/activate  (if using venv)")
   245	   243	    print("      python")
   246	   244	    print("   2. Inside Python console, run:")
   247	   245	    print("      >>> from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager")
   248	   246	    print("      >>> auth_manager = SecureAuthManager()")
   249	   247	    print(f"      >>> print(auth_manager.generate_token_for_user('your_admin_username', role='admin'))")
   250	   248	    print(f"   3. Copy the generated token and replace 'SET_YOUR_ADMIN_TOKEN_HERE' in this app.py file.")
   251	   249	    print("\n")
   252	   250	
   253	   251	    try:
   254	   252	        uvicorn.run(
   255	   253	            "src.web.app:app",
   256	   254	            host=HOST,
   257	   255	            port=PORT,
   258	   256	            reload=True
   259	   257	        )
   260	   258	    except Exception as e:
   261	   259	        print(f"ğŸš¨ Server failed to start. An error occurred: {e}")
   262	   260	        print("   Please check the imports and configuration.")
   263	   261

     6	     6	class SecureAuthManager:

     8	     8	    Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª ØªÙˆÚ©Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ù…Ù†ØŒ Ú©Ù†ØªØ±Ù„ Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒØŒ ØªØ§Ø±ÛŒØ® Ø§Ù†Ù‚Ø¶Ø§ Ùˆ Ø¬Ù„ÙˆÚ¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ Ø§Ø² Ø­Ù…Ù„Ø§Øª Brute-force

    12	    12	        self.max_failed_attempts = max_failed_attempts

    47	    47	            return

    52	    52	        else:

    54	    54	            if now > record.get("locked_until", 0) and (now - record.get("last_failed", 0)) > self.lockout_seconds:

    56	    56	            else:

    90	    90	
    91	    91	    def authenticate(self, token: str, client_ip: Optional[str] = None) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str], Optional[str]]:

   140	   140	        async function fetchTelemetry() {
   141	   141	            try {
   142	   142	                const res = await fetch('/api/telemetry');
   143	   143	                const data = await res.json();
   144	   144	                
   145	   145	                document.getElementById('btc-price').innerText = '$' + data.btc_price;
   146	   146	                document.getElementById('total-equity').innerText = '$' + data.total_equity;
   147	   147	                document.getElementById('reserve-vault').innerText = '$' + data.reserve_usdt;
   148	   148	                document.getElementById('win-rate-stat').innerText = `${data.win_rate} (${data.total_profit_usdt})`;
   149	   149	                
   150	   150	                document.getElementById('rsi-val').innerText = data.rsi;
   151	   151	                document.getElementById('trend-val').innerText = data.trend;
   152	   152	
   153	   153	                const pos = data.position;
   154	   154	                if (pos.side !== 'FLAT') {
   155	   155	                    document.getElementById('active-position').innerText = `${pos.side} (${pos.amount} BTC @ ${pos.entry_price})`;
   156	   156	                    const pnlVal = parseFloat(pos.pnl);
   157	   157	                    const pnlElem = document.getElementById('pos-pnl');
   158	   158	                    pnlElem.innerText = `PnL: ${pnlVal >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${pnlVal.toFixed(2)} USDT [TP: ${pos.tp} | SL: ${pos.sl}]`;
   159	   159	                    pnlElem.className = `text-xs font-bold mt-1 ${pnlVal >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`;
   160	   160	                } else {
   161	   161	                    document.getElementById('active-position').innerText = 'Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø² (Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ ØªØ­Ù„ÛŒÙ„)';
   162	   162	                    document.getElementById('pos-pnl').innerText = 'PnL: $0.00';
   163	   163	                    document.getElementById('pos-pnl').className = 'text-xs font-bold mt-1 text-gray-500';
   164	   164	                }
   165	   165	
   166	   166	                // Update Chart
   167	   167	                const currPrice = parseFloat(data.btc_price.replace(/,/g, ''));
   168	   168	                priceChart.data.datasets[0].data.push(currPrice);
   169	   169	                priceChart.data.datasets[0].data.shift();
   170	   170	                priceChart.update('none');
   171	   171	
   172	   172	                // Update Trades
   173	   173	                const tradesContainer = document.getElementById('trades-container');
   174	   174	                if (data.recent_trades.length > 0) {
   175	   175	                    tradesContainer.innerHTML = data.recent_trades.map(t => `
   176	   176	                        <div class="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-800 pb-1">
   177	   177	                            <span>[${t.time}] ${t.action}</span>
   178	   178	                            <span class="${t.pnl.includes('+') ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'} font-bold">${t.pnl}</span>
   179	   179	                        </div>
   180	   180	                    `).join('');
   181	   181	                }
   182	   182	
   183	   183	                // Update Logs
   184	   184	                const logsContainer = document.getElementById('logs-container');
   185	   185	                logsContainer.innerHTML = data.logs.slice(-15).reverse().map(l => `<div>${l}</div>`).join('');
   186	   186	
   187	   187	            } catch (err) {
   188	   188	                console.error("Telemetry fetch error:", err);
   189	   189	            }
   190	   190	        }

   195	   195	                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   196	   196	                body: JSON.stringify({ action: action, amount: 0.02 })
   197	   197	            });
   198	   198	            fetchTelemetry();
   199	   199	        }

    82	def _atria_final_execute_paper_trade(
    83	    self,
    84	    symbol,
    85	    side,
    86	    entry_price,
    87	    exit_price,
    88	    size=0.1,
    89	    quantity=None,
    90	    **kwargs,
    91	):
    92	    qty = float(quantity if quantity is not None else size)
    93	    entry = float(entry_price)
    94	    exit_ = float(exit_price)
    95	    normalized_side = str(side).upper()
    96	
    97	    if normalized_side in ("BUY", "LONG"):
    98	        pnl = (exit_ - entry) * qty
    99	    else:
   100	        pnl = (entry - exit_) * qty
   101	
   102	    trade = {
   103	        "symbol": str(symbol),
   104	        "side": normalized_side,
   105	        "entry_price": entry,
   106	        "exit_price": exit_,
   107	        "size": qty,
   108	        "quantity": qty,
   109	        "pnl": float(pnl),
   110	        "status": "CLOSED",
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    self.trades.append(trade)
   114	    self.total_pnl = float(getattr(self, "total_pnl", 0.0)) + float(pnl)
   115	    self.current_capital = float(getattr(self, "current_capital", self.initial_capital)) + float(pnl)
   116	    _atria_final_persist_paper(self)
   117	    return trade
   118	
   119	
   120	def _atria_final_get_session_stats(self):
   121	    total = len(getattr(self, "trades", []))
   122	    wins = sum(1 for trade in self.trades if float(trade.get("pnl", 0.0)) > 0.0)
   123	    return {
   124	        "total_trades": total,
   125	        "winning_trades": wins,
   126	        "losing_trades": total - wins,
   127	        "win_rate_pct": (wins / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0,
   128	        "total_pnl": float(getattr(self, "total_pnl", 0.0)),
   129	        "current_capital": float(getattr(self, "current_capital", 0.0)),
   130	    }

   143	class _AtriaFinalRecord(dict):

   156	def _atria_final_open_position(
   157	    self,

   196	def _atria_final_close_position(self, symbol, exit_price, **kwargs):
   197	    symbol = str(symbol).upper()
   198	    position = _atria_final_get_position(self, symbol)
   199	    if position is None:
   200	        return None

   203	    position["status"] = "CLOSED"

   208	def _atria_final_check_sl_tp(self, symbol, current_price):
   209	    position = _atria_final_get_position(self, symbol)
   210	    if position is None:
   211	        return None
   212	
   213	    side = str(position.get("side", "")).upper()
   214	    price = float(current_price)
   215	    sl = position.get("sl", position.get("stop_loss"))
   216	    tp = position.get("tp", position.get("take_profit"))
   217	
   218	    if side in ("BUY", "LONG"):
   219	        if sl is not None and price <= float(sl):
   220	            return "SL"
   221	        if tp is not None and price >= float(tp):
   222	            return "TP"
   223	    elif side in ("SELL", "SHORT"):
   224	        if sl is not None and price >= float(sl):
   225	            return "SL"
   226	        if tp is not None and price <= float(tp):
   227	            return "TP"
   228	
   229	    return None
   230

   240	
   241	# ===== ATRIA_FINAL_REMAINING6: OrderManager lifecycle contract =====
   242	
   243	class _AtriaFinalOrder(dict):
   244	    """Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ Ù‡Ù…â€ŒØ²Ù…Ø§Ù† order['status'] Ùˆ order.status."""
   245	
   246	    def __getattr__(self, name):
   247	        try:
   248	            return self[name]
   249	        except KeyError as error:
   250	            raise AttributeError(name) from error
   251	
   252	    def __setattr__(self, name, value):
   253	        self[name] = value
   254	
   255	    def to_dict(self):
   256	        return dict(self)
   257	
   258	
   259	def _atria_final_order_value(value):
   260	    return getattr(value, "value", value)
   261	
   262	
   263	def _atria_final_create_order(
   264	    self,
   265	    symbol,
   266	    side,
   267	    order_type="MARKET",
   268	    quantity=None,
   269	    price=None,
   270	    **kwargs,
   271	):
   272	    normalized_symbol = str(symbol).upper()
   273	    normalized_side = str(_atria_final_order_value(side)).upper()
   274	    normalized_type = str(_atria_final_order_value(order_type)).upper()
   275	
   276	    qty = kwargs.get("amount", kwargs.get("size", quantity))
   277	    qty = float(qty if qty is not None else 0.0)
   278	
   279	    raw_price = kwargs.get("entry_price", price)
   280	    order_price = float(raw_price if raw_price is not None else 0.0)
   281	
   282	    if normalized_side not in ("BUY", "SELL", "LONG", "SHORT"):
   283	        raise ValueError("Invalid order side")
   284	    if normalized_type not in ("MARKET", "LIMIT", "STOP"):
   285	        raise ValueError("Invalid order type")
   286	    if qty <= 0.0:
   287	        raise ValueError("Quantity must be greater than zero")
   288	    if normalized_type in ("LIMIT", "STOP") and order_price <= 0.0:
   289	        raise ValueError("Price must be greater than zero for LIMIT/STOP orders")
   290	
   291	    if not hasattr(self, "orders") or self.orders is None:
   292	        self.orders = {}
   293	
   294	    # ØªØ³Øª Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø± Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯ duplicate Ø¨Ø§Ø²ØŒ ValueError Ø´Ø§Ù…Ù„ identical Ø¨Ø¯Ù‡Ø¯.
   295	    for active in self.orders.values():
   296	        active_status = str(active.get("status", "")).upper()
   297	        if (
   298	            active_status in ("OPEN", "PENDING")
   299	            and str(active.get("symbol", "")).upper() == normalized_symbol
   300	            and str(active.get("side", "")).upper() == normalized_side
   301	            and str(active.get("order_type", "")).upper() == normalized_type
   302	            and float(active.get("quantity", 0.0)) == qty
   303	            and float(active.get("price", 0.0)) == order_price
   304	        ):
   305	            raise ValueError("An identical active order already exists.")
   306	
   307	    order_id = kwargs.get("order_id", f"order_{len(self.orders) + 1}")
   308	    while order_id in self.orders:
   309	        order_id = f"order_{len(self.orders) + 1}_{len(self.orders)}"
   310	
   311	    order = _AtriaFinalOrder(
   312	        order_id=order_id,
   313	        symbol=normalized_symbol,
   314	        side=normalized_side,
   315	        order_type=normalized_type,
   316	        quantity=qty,
   317	        size=qty,
   318	        price=order_price,
   319	        status="OPEN" if normalized_type == "LIMIT" else "PENDING",
   320	        sl=kwargs.get("sl", kwargs.get("stop_loss")),
   321	        tp=kwargs.get("tp", kwargs.get("take_profit")),
   322	    )
   323	    self.orders[order_id] = order
   324	    return order
   325	
   326	
   327	def _atria_final_fill_order(self, order_id, fill_price=None, **kwargs):
   328	    try:
   329	        order = self.orders[order_id]
   330	    except KeyError as error:
   331	        raise ValueError(f"Order not found: {order_id}") from error
   332	
   333	    final_price = float(fill_price if fill_price is not None else order["price"])
   334	    order["status"] = "FILLED"
   335	    order["fill_price"] = final_price
   336	    order["filled_price"] = final_price
   337	    order["filled_quantity"] = float(order["quantity"])
   338	    return order
   339	
   340	
   341	def _atria_final_cancel_order(self, order_id, **kwargs):
   342	    try:
   343	        order = self.orders[order_id]
   344	    except KeyError as error:
   345	        raise ValueError(f"Order not found: {order_id}") from error
   346	
   347	    order["status"] = "CANCELLED"
   348	    return order
   349	
   350	
   351	def _atria_final_get_order_status(self, order_id):
   352	    order = getattr(self, "orders", {}).get(order_id)
   353	    return None if order is None else str(order.get("status", "")).upper()
   354	
   355	
   356	def _atria_final_list_open_orders(self, symbol=None):
   357	    expected_symbol = None if symbol is None else str(symbol).upper()
   358	    result = []
   359	
   360	    for order in getattr(self, "orders", {}).values():
   361	        if str(order.get("status", "")).upper() not in ("OPEN", "PENDING"):
   362	            continue
   363	        if expected_symbol is not None and str(order.get("symbol", "")).upper() != expected_symbol:
   364	            continue
   365	        result.append(order)
   366	
   367	    return result
   368	
   369	
   370	OrderManager.create_order = _atria_final_create_order
   371	OrderManager.fill_order = _atria_final_fill_order
   372	OrderManager.cancel_order = _atria_final_cancel_order
   373	OrderManager.get_order_status = _atria_final_get_order_status
   374	OrderManager.list_open_orders = _atria_final_list_open_orders
   375	''',
