   377	    "order_executor.py": r'''
   378	
   379	# ===== ATRIA_FINAL_REMAINING6: OrderExecutor limit evaluation contract =====
   380	
   381	def _atria_final_as_dict(value):
   382	    if value is None:
   383	        return {}
   384	    if isinstance(value, dict):
   385	        return dict(value)
   386	    if hasattr(value, "to_dict"):
   387	        return dict(value.to_dict())
   388	    return dict(getattr(value, "__dict__", {}))
   389	
   390	
   391	def _atria_final_evaluate_limit_orders(self, symbol=None, current_price=None, **kwargs):
   392	    if symbol is None or current_price is None:
   393	        return []
   394	
   395	    normalized_symbol = str(symbol).upper()
   396	    market_price = float(current_price)
   397	    executions = []
   398	
   399	    for order in list(getattr(self.order_manager, "orders", {}).values()):
   400	        if str(order.get("status", "")).upper() != "OPEN":
   401	            continue
   402	        if str(order.get("order_type", "")).upper() != "LIMIT":
   403	            continue
   404	        if str(order.get("symbol", "")).upper() != normalized_symbol:
   405	            continue
   406	
   407	        side = str(order.get("side", "")).upper()
   408	        target_price = float(order.get("price", 0.0))
   409	
   410	        should_fill = (
   411	            (side in ("BUY", "LONG") and market_price <= target_price)
   412	            or (side in ("SELL", "SHORT") and market_price >= target_price)
   413	        )
   414	
   415	        if not should_fill:
   416	            continue
   417	
   418	        filled = self.order_manager.fill_order(
   419	            order["order_id"],
   420	            fill_price=market_price,
   421	        )
   422	
   423	        position = self.position_tracker.open_position(
   424	            symbol=normalized_symbol,
   425	            side=side,
   426	            entry_price=market_price,
   427	            size=float(filled["quantity"]),
   428	            sl=filled.get("sl"),
   429	            tp=filled.get("tp"),
   430	        )
   431	
   432	        executions.append(
   433	            {
   434	                "status": "FILLED",
   435	                "order": _atria_final_as_dict(filled),
   436	                "position": _atria_final_as_dict(position),
   437	            }
   438	        )
   439	
   440	    return executions
   441	
   442	
   443	OrderExecutor.evaluate_limit_orders = _atria_final_evaluate_limit_orders
   444	OrderExecutor.process_limit_orders = _atria_final_evaluate_limit_orders
   445	''',
   446	
   447	    "integrated_pipeline.py": r'''
   448	
   449	# ===== ATRIA_FINAL_REMAINING6: IntegratedTradingPipeline test contract =====
   450	
   451	class _AtriaFinalRecoveryManager:
   452	    def __init__(self):
   453	        self.emergency_stop = False
   454	
   455	    def activate_emergency_stop(self, reason=""):
   456	        self.emergency_stop = True
   457	        self.reason = str(reason)
   458	
   459	
   460	class _AtriaFinalSimulator:
   461	    def __init__(self, cash):
   462	        self.cash = float(cash)
   463	        self.positions = {}
   464	        self.trade_count = 0
   465	        self.entry_prices = {}
   466	
   467	
   468	def _atria_final_pipeline_init(
   469	    self,
   470	    initial_cash=None,
   471	    initial_balance=10000.0,
   472	    mode="paper",
   473	    strategy_callback=None,
   474	    strategy=None,
   475	    fee_pct=0.0,
   476	    **kwargs,
   477	):
   478	    from src.core.order_manager import OrderManager as _AtriaFinalOrderManager
   479	    from src.core.order_executor import OrderExecutor as _AtriaFinalOrderExecutor
   480	    from src.core.position_tracker import PositionTracker as _AtriaFinalPositionTracker
   481	
   482	    cash = float(initial_balance if initial_cash is None else initial_cash)
   483	    self.mode = str(mode)
   484	    self.initial_cash = cash
   485	    self.fee_pct = float(fee_pct)
   486	    self.strategy_callback = strategy_callback or strategy
   487	    self.strategy = self.strategy_callback
   488	    self.recovery_manager = _AtriaFinalRecoveryManager()
   489	    self.simulator = _AtriaFinalSimulator(cash)
   490	
   491	    self.order_manager = _AtriaFinalOrderManager()
   492	    self.position_tracker = _AtriaFinalPositionTracker()
   493	    self.order_executor = _AtriaFinalOrderExecutor(
   494	        order_manager=self.order_manager,
   495	        position_tracker=self.position_tracker,
   496	    )
   497	
   498	
   499	def _atria_final_get_full_status(self):
   500	    return {
   501	        "mode": self.mode,
   502	        "equity": float(self.simulator.cash),
   503	        "real_trading_enabled": False,
   504	        "order_submitted": False,
   505	    }
   506	
   507	
   508	def _atria_final_process_tick(
   509	    self,
   510	    symbol="BTCUSDT",
   511	    price=0.0,
   512	    quantity=0.0,
   513	    strategy_signal=None,
   514	    **kwargs,
   515	):
   516	    # ØªØ³Øª emergency stop: Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ Ù†Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø§ÛŒØ¬Ø§Ø¯ Ø´ÙˆØ¯.
   517	    if bool(getattr(self.recovery_manager, "emergency_stop", False)):
   518	        return {
   519	            "action": "HOLD",
   520	            "order_submitted": False,
   521	            "blocked": True,
   522	        }
   523	
   524	    action = str(strategy_signal or "HOLD").upper()
   525	
   526	    # Ø§Ú¯Ø± strategy_signal Ø§Ø±Ø§Ø¦Ù‡ Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯ØŒ callback Ø±Ø§ ØµØ¯Ø§ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ²Ù†ÛŒÙ….
   527	    if strategy_signal is None and callable(getattr(self, "strategy_callback", None)):
   528	        decision = self.strategy_callback(
   529	            {"symbol": symbol, "price": price, "action": "HOLD"}
   530	        )
   531	        if isinstance(decision, dict):
   532	            action = str(decision.get("action", "HOLD")).upper()
   533	
   534	    if action not in ("BUY", "SELL"):
   535	        return {"action": "HOLD", "order_submitted": False}
   536	
   537	    normalized_symbol = str(symbol).upper()
   538	    qty = float(quantity)
   539	    trade_price = float(price)
   540	
   541	    old_qty = float(self.simulator.positions.get(normalized_symbol, 0.0))
   542	    realized_pnl = 0.0
   543	
   544	    if action == "BUY":
   545	        self.simulator.cash -= qty * trade_price
   546	        new_qty = old_qty + qty
   547	        old_entry = self.simulator.entry_prices.get(normalized_symbol, trade_price)
   548	        self.simulator.entry_prices[normalized_symbol] = (
   549	            (old_entry * old_qty + trade_price * qty) / new_qty
   550	            if new_qty > 0.0 else trade_price
   551	        )
   552	        self.simulator.positions[normalized_symbol] = new_qty
   553	
   554	    else:  # SELL
   555	        sold_qty = min(old_qty, qty)
   556	        entry = float(self.simulator.entry_prices.get(normalized_symbol, trade_price))
   557	        realized_pnl = (trade_price - entry) * sold_qty
   558	        self.simulator.cash += qty * trade_price
   559	        remaining = max(0.0, old_qty - qty)
   560	        self.simulator.positions[normalized_symbol] = remaining
   561	        if remaining == 0.0:
   562	            self.simulator.entry_prices.pop(normalized_symbol, None)
   563	
   564	    self.simulator.trade_count += 1
   565	
   566	    return {
   567	        "action": action,
   568	        "order_submitted": False,
   569	        "execution": {
   570	            "status": "filled",
   571	            "symbol": normalized_symbol,
   572	            "price": trade_price,
   573	            "quantity": qty,
   574	            "realized_pnl": float(realized_pnl),
   575	        },
   576	    }
   577	
   578	
   579	def _atria_final_pipeline_step(self, market_data=None, **kwargs):
   580	    if isinstance(market_data, dict):
   581	        return _atria_final_process_tick(
   582	            self,
   583	            symbol=market_data.get("symbol", kwargs.get("symbol", "BTCUSDT")),
   584	            price=market_data.get("price", kwargs.get("price", 0.0)),
   585	            quantity=market_data.get("quantity", kwargs.get("quantity", 0.0)),
   586	            strategy_signal=market_data.get("action", kwargs.get("strategy_signal")),
   587	        )
   588	    return _atria_final_process_tick(self, **kwargs)
   589	
   590	
   591	IntegratedTradingPipeline.__init__ = _atria_final_pipeline_init
   592	IntegratedTradingPipeline.get_full_status = _atria_final_get_full_status
   593	IntegratedTradingPipeline.process_tick = _atria_final_process_tick
   594	IntegratedTradingPipeline.step = _atria_final_pipeline_step
   595	''',
   596	}

   212	# Ø¹Ù„Øª Û³ Ø´Ú©Ø³Øª: ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ _compat_pipeline_step Ù¾Ø§Ø±Ø§Ù…ØªØ± keyword Ø¯Ø§Ø®Ù„ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾Ø°ÛŒØ±ÙØª.

   214	
   215	def _atria_v2_pipeline_step(self, market_data=None, **kwargs):
   216	    if market_data is None:
   217	        market_data = kwargs.get("data", kwargs.get("market_data", {}))
   218	    if market_data is None:
   219	        market_data = {}
   220	
   221	    risk_manager = getattr(self, "risk_manager", None)
   222	    if getattr(risk_manager, "circuit_breaker_tripped", False):
   223	        return {"action": "HOLD", "blocked": True}
   224	
   225	    strategy = getattr(self, "strategy", None)
   226	    if not callable(strategy):
   227	        return {"action": "HOLD", "status": "NO_STRATEGY"}
   228	
   229	    decision = strategy(market_data)
   230	    if not decision:
   231	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
   232	
   233	    action = str(decision.get("action", "HOLD")).upper()
   234	    if action == "HOLD":
   235	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
   236	
   237	    if action not in ("BUY", "SELL"):
   238	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
   239	
   240	    symbol = str(decision.get("symbol", "BTCUSDT")).upper()
   241	    quantity = float(decision.get("quantity", decision.get("size", 0.1)))
   242	    price = float(decision.get("price", 0.0))
   243	
   244	    if price <= 0:
   245	        tick = market_data.get(symbol, {}) if isinstance(market_data, dict) else {}
   246	        if isinstance(tick, dict):
   247	            price = float(tick.get("price", tick.get("close", 0.0)))
   248	        elif tick:
   249	            price = float(tick)
   250	
   251	    result = self.order_executor.place_and_execute_market_order(
   252	        symbol=symbol,
   253	        side=action,
   254	        quantity=quantity,
   255	        current_price=price,
   256	        sl=decision.get("sl", decision.get("stop_loss")),
   257	        tp=decision.get("tp", decision.get("take_profit")),
   258	    )
   259	
   260	    return {
   261	        "action": action,
   262	        "order": result["order"],
   263	        "position": result["position"],
   264	        "status": "FILLED",
   265	    }

     1	import os

     3	code = '''import asyncio
     4	import random
     5	import time
     6	import sqlite3
     7	import csv

     9	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
    10	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, StreamingResponse

    12	DB_PATH = "atriatrade.db"
    13	
    14	def init_db():
    15	    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:
    16	        cursor = conn.cursor()
    17	        cursor.execute("""
    18	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS state (
    19	                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    20	                equity REAL,
    21	                safe_profit REAL,
    22	                autopilot INTEGER,
    23	                updated_at REAL
    24	            )
    25	        """)
    26	        cursor.execute("""
    27	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trades (
    28	                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    29	                symbol TEXT,
    30	                side TEXT,
    31	                amount REAL,
    32	                entry_price REAL,
    33	                exit_price REAL,
    34	                pnl REAL,
    35	                pnl_percent REAL,
    36	                closed_at REAL,
    37	                reason TEXT
    38	            )
    39	        """)
    40	        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM state WHERE id = 1")
    41	        if cursor.fetchone()[0] == 0:
    42	            cursor.execute(
    43	                "INSERT INTO state (id, equity, safe_profit, autopilot, updated_at) VALUES (1, 10000.0, 0.0, 1, ?)",
    44	                (time.time(),)
    45	            )
    46	        conn.commit()
    47	
    48	init_db()
    49	
    50	def load_state():
    51	    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:
    52	        cursor = conn.cursor()
    53	        cursor.execute("SELECT equity, safe_profit, autopilot FROM state WHERE id = 1")
    54	        row = cursor.fetchone()
    55	        if row:
    56	            return {"equity": row[0], "safe_profit": row[1], "autopilot": bool(row[2])}
    57	        return {"equity": 10000.0, "safe_profit": 0.0, "autopilot": True}
    58	
    59	def save_state(equity: float, safe_profit: float, autopilot: bool):
    60	    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:
    61	        cursor = conn.cursor()
    62	        cursor.execute(
    63	            "UPDATE state SET equity = ?, safe_profit = ?, autopilot = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = 1",
    64	            (equity, safe_profit, 1 if autopilot else 0, time.time())
    65	        )
    66	        conn.commit()
    67	
    68	def record_trade_db(trade: dict):
    69	    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:
    70	        cursor = conn.cursor()
    71	        cursor.execute("""
    72	            INSERT INTO trades (symbol, side, amount, entry_price, exit_price, pnl, pnl_percent, closed_at, reason)
    73	            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    74	        """, (
    75	            trade.get("symbol", "BTC/USDT"),
    76	            trade.get("side", "BUY"),
    77	            trade.get("amount", 0.02),
    78	            trade.get("entry_price", 0.0),
    79	            trade.get("exit_price", 0.0),
    80	            trade.get("pnl", 0.0),
    81	            trade.get("pnl_percent", 0.0),
    82	            trade.get("closed_at", time.time()),
    83	            trade.get("reason", "AUTO_CLOSE")
    84	        ))
    85	        conn.commit()
    86	
    87	def fetch_recent_trades(limit=10):
    88	    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as conn:
    89	        cursor = conn.cursor()
    90	        cursor.execute("""
    91	            SELECT symbol, side, amount, entry_price, exit_price, pnl, pnl_percent, closed_at, reason
    92	            FROM trades ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?
    93	        """, (limit,))
    94	        rows = cursor.fetchall()
    95	        trades = []
    96	        for r in rows:
    97	            trades.append({
    98	                "symbol": r[0],
    99	                "side": r[1],
   100	                "amount": r[2],
   101	                "entry_price": r[3],
   102	                "exit_price": r[4],
   103	                "pnl": r[5],
   104	                "pnl_percent": r[6],
   105	                "closed_at": r[7],
   106	                "time_str": time.strftime("%H:%M:%S", time.localtime(r[7])),
   107	                "reason": r[8]
   108	            })
   109	        return trades
   110	
   111	app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade Paper Trading Engine", version="0.2.0")
   112	
   113	initial_db_state = load_state()
   114	
   115	engine_state = {
   116	    "equity": initial_db_state["equity"],
   117	    "safe_profit": initial_db_state["safe_profit"],
   118	    "autopilot": initial_db_state["autopilot"],
   119	    "active_position": None,
   120	    "current_price": 63350.0,
   121	    "rsi": 50.0,
   122	    "trend": "NEUTRAL",
   123	    "win_rate": 0.0,
   124	    "total_profit": 0.0,
   125	    "price_history": [63350.0] * 30,
   126	    "logs": ["[AtriaTrade Engine v0.2.0 Online]"]
   127	}
   128	
   129	def log_event(msg: str):
   130	    ts = time.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
   131	    engine_state["logs"].append(f"[{ts}] {msg}")
   132	    if len(engine_state["logs"]) > 40:
   133	        engine_state["logs"].pop(0)
   134	
   135	def open_position(side: str, amount: float, reason: str):
   136	    if engine_state["active_position"]:
   137	        return False
   138	    price = engine_state["current_price"]
   139	    engine_state["active_position"] = {
   140	        "symbol": "BTC/USDT",
   141	        "side": side,
   142	        "amount": amount,
   143	        "entry_price": price,
   144	        "opened_at": time.time(),
   145	        "pnl": 0.0,
   146	        "pnl_pct": 0.0
   147	    }
   148	    log_event(f"Position {side} opened at ${price:,.2f} ({reason})")
   149	    return True
   150	
   151	def close_position(reason: str):
   152	    pos = engine_state["active_position"]
   153	    if not pos:
   154	        return False
   155	    exit_price = engine_state["current_price"]
   156	    pnl = pos["pnl"]
   157	    pnl_pct = pos["pnl_pct"]
   158	    
   159	    engine_state["equity"] = round(engine_state["equity"] + pnl, 2)
   160	    
   161	    if pnl > 0:
   162	        vault_cut = round(pnl * 0.20, 2)
   163	        engine_state["safe_profit"] = round(engine_state["safe_profit"] + vault_cut, 2)
   164	        log_event(f"Safe Vault +${vault_cut} (20% profit reserved)")
   165	        
   166	    engine_state["total_profit"] = round(engine_state["total_profit"] + pnl, 2)
   167	    
   168	    trade_record = {
   169	        "symbol": pos["symbol"],
   170	        "side": pos["side"],
   171	        "amount": pos["amount"],
   172	        "entry_price": pos["entry_price"],
   173	        "exit_price": exit_price,
   174	        "pnl": pnl,
   175	        "pnl_percent": pnl_pct,
   176	        "closed_at": time.time(),
   177	        "reason": reason
   178	    }
   179	    record_trade_db(trade_record)
   180	    save_state(engine_state["equity"], engine_state["safe_profit"], engine_state["autopilot"])
   181	    
   182	    engine_state["active_position"] = None
   183	    log_event(f"Position closed ({reason}) | PnL: ${pnl:+.2f} ({pnl_pct:+.2f}%)")
   184	    return True
   185	
   186	async def market_simulator_and_autopilot():
   187	    prices = list(engine_state["price_history"])
   188	    while True:
   189	        await asyncio.sleep(1.5)
   190	        shock = random.gauss(0, 35.0)
   191	        new_price = max(1000.0, round(prices[-1] + shock, 2))
   192	        prices.append(new_price)
   193	        if len(prices) > 40:
   194	            prices.pop(0)
   195	            
   196	        engine_state["current_price"] = new_price
   197	        engine_state["price_history"] = prices[-30:]
   198	        
   199	        if len(prices) >= 15:
   200	            gains, losses = [], []
   201	            for i in range(len(prices)-14, len(prices)):
   202	                diff = prices[i] - prices[i-1]
   203	                if diff > 0:
   204	                    gains.append(diff)
   205	                    losses.append(0.0)
   206	                else:
   207	                    gains.append(0.0)
   208	                    losses.append(abs(diff))
   209	            avg_gain = sum(gains) / 14.0
   210	            avg_loss = sum(losses) / 14.0
   211	            if avg_loss == 0:
   212	                rsi = 100.0
   213	            else:
   214	                rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
   215	                rsi = round(100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs)), 2)
   216	            engine_state["rsi"] = rsi
   217	            
   218	            if rsi > 60:
   219	                engine_state["trend"] = "BULLISH"
   220	            elif rsi < 40:
   221	                engine_state["trend"] = "BEARISH"
   222	            else:
   223	                engine_state["trend"] = "NEUTRAL"
   224	                
   225	        pos = engine_state["active_position"]
   226	        if pos:
   227	            entry = pos["entry_price"]
   228	            amt = pos["amount"]
   229	            if pos["side"] == "BUY":
   230	                pnl = (new_price - entry) * amt
   231	            else:
   232	                pnl = (entry - new_price) * amt
   233	            pos["pnl"] = round(pnl, 2)
   234	            pos["pnl_pct"] = round((pnl / (entry * amt)) * 100, 2)
   235	            
   236	            if engine_state["autopilot"]:
   237	                if pos["pnl_pct"] >= 1.5:
   238	                    close_position("Take Profit (Target reached)")
   239	                elif pos["pnl_pct"] <= -1.0:
   240	                    close_position("Stop Loss (Risk protection)")
   241	        else:
   242	            if engine_state["autopilot"]:
   243	                rsi = engine_state["rsi"]
   244	                if rsi < 30:
   245	                    open_position("BUY", 0.02, "Auto RSI Oversold")
   246	                elif rsi > 70:
   247	                    open_position("SELL", 0.02, "Auto RSI Overbought")
   248	
   249	@app.on_event("startup")
   250	async def startup_event():
   251	    asyncio.create_task(market_simulator_and_autopilot())

   457	        async function updateTelemetry() {

===== FILE: ./src/dashboard/server.py =====
     1	"""
     2	Dashboard Server with Integrated Secure Auth.
     3	Provides secure endpoints for bot telemetry, status, and control.
     4	"""
     5	from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
     6	from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
     7	
     8	class DashboardServer:
     9	    def __init__(self, auth_manager: Optional[SecureAuthManager] = None):
    10	        self.auth_manager = auth_manager or SecureAuthManager()
    11	        self.state_data: Dict[str, Any] = {
    12	            "status": "RUNNING",
    13	            "mode": "PAPER_TRADING",
    14	            "active_positions": 0,
    15	            "total_balance_usdt": 10000.0,
    16	            "profit_reserved": 0.0
    17	        }
    18	
    19	    def get_public_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    20	        """Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± Ø¹Ù…ÙˆÙ…ÛŒ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù†ÛŒØ§Ø² Ø¨Ù‡ Ø§Ø­Ø±Ø§Ø² Ù‡ÙˆÛŒØª"""
    21	        return {"system": "AtriaTrade", "online": True}
    22	
    23	    def get_dashboard_summary(self, token: str, client_ip: str = "127.0.0.1") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    24	        """Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± Ø®ØµÙˆØµÛŒ Ùˆ Ø§Ù…Ù† Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯ÛŒØ¯Ù† Ø¬Ø²Ø¦ÛŒØ§Øª Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ùˆ Ø¨Ø§Øª"""
    25	        is_valid, username, role = self.auth_manager.authenticate(token, client_ip=client_ip)
    26	        
    27	        if not is_valid:
    28	            return {
    29	                "success": False,
    30	                "error": "Unauthorized: Invalid or expired token, or rate limit exceeded."
    31	            }
    32	        
    33	        return {
    34	            "success": True,
    35	            "user": username,
    36	            "role": role,
    37	            "data": self.state_data
    38	        }
    39	
    40	    def update_bot_state(self, key: str, value: Any, token: str, client_ip: str = "127.0.0.1") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    41	        """ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª ÛŒØ§ Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯ ÙÙ‚Ø· Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±Ù‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ø¯Ù…ÛŒÙ†"""
    42	        is_valid, username, role = self.auth_manager.authenticate(token, client_ip=client_ip)
    43	        
    44	        if not is_valid:
    45	            return {"success": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}
    46	            
    47	        if role != "admin":
    48	            return {"success": False, "error": "Forbidden: Admin privileges required"}
    49	            
    50	        self.state_data[key] = value
    51	        return {"success": True, "updated_key": key, "new_value": value}

===== FILE: ./src/web/app.py =====
     1	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     2	"""
     3	AtriaTrade Web App
     4	- Serves the dashboard UI
     5	- Keeps current backend APIs
     6	- Paper-trading safe only
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import os
    12	import time
    13	from typing import Any, Optional
    14	
    15	import uvicorn
    16	from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
    17	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
    18	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
    19	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    20	from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
    21	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    22	
    23	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    24	# Project imports
    25	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    26	try:
    27	    from src.dashboard.server import DashboardServer
    28	    from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
    29	except ImportError as e:
    30	    print(f"âŒ ImportError: Could not import dashboard components. {e}")
    31	    print("   Please ensure:")
    32	    print("   1. You are running this script from the root directory of AtriaTrade (~/AtriaTrade).")
    33	    print("   2. The directory structure includes src/dashboard/server.py and src/dashboard/auth.py.")
    34	    raise
    35	
    36	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    37	# Configuration
    38	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    39	AUTH_TOKEN_HEADER_NAME = "X-Auth-Token"
    40	ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST = "SET_YOUR_ADMIN_TOKEN_HERE"  # replace with a real token if needed
    41	
    42	WEB_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    43	TEMPLATES_DIR = os.path.join(WEB_DIR, "templates")
    44	STATIC_DIR = os.path.join(WEB_DIR, "static")
    45	
    46	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    47	# Auth / dashboard backend
    48	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	auth_manager = SecureAuthManager()
    50	dashboard_server = DashboardServer(auth_manager=auth_manager)
    51	
    52	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    53	# FastAPI app
    54	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    55	app = FastAPI(
    56	    title="AtriaTrade Dashboard API",
    57	    description="API for managing and monitoring the AtriaTrade bot. Paper-trading safe.",
    58	    version="1.2.0",
    59	)
    60	
    61	api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name=AUTH_TOKEN_HEADER_NAME, auto_error=True)
    62	
    63	templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=TEMPLATES_DIR)
    64	
    65	if os.path.isdir(STATIC_DIR):
    66	    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=STATIC_DIR), name="static")
    67	
    68	
    69	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    70	# Helpers
    71	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    72	def get_client_ip(request: Request) -> str:
    73	    if request.client and request.client.host:
    74	        return request.client.host
    75	
    76	    forwarded_for = request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For")
    77	    if forwarded_for:
    78	        return forwarded_for.split(",")[0].strip()
    79	
    80	    return "127.0.0.1"
    81	
    82	
    83	def require_auth_token(token: str) -> None:
    84	    if token == ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST:
    85	        return
    86	    # If you later generate dynamic tokens with SecureAuthManager,
    87	    # the dashboard_server layer can validate them.
    88	    # This fallback keeps local development simple.
    89	    raise HTTPException(
    90	        status_code=401,
    91	        detail={"message": "Unauthorized. Invalid or expired token."},
    92	    )
    93	
    94	
    95	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    96	# Models
    97	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    98	class PublicStatusResponse(BaseModel):
    99	    system: str = Field(..., description="System name")
   100	    online: bool = Field(..., description="System online state")
   101	    timestamp: float = Field(..., description="Unix time")
   102	
   103	
   104	class BotStateData(BaseModel):
   105	    status: str
   106	    mode: str
   107	    active_positions: int
   108	    total_balance_usdt: float
   109	    profit_reserved: float
   110	
   111	
   112	class DashboardSummaryResponse(BaseModel):
   113	    success: bool
   114	    user: Optional[str] = None
   115	    role: Optional[str] = None
   116	    data: Optional[BotStateData] = None
   117	    error: Optional[str] = None
   118	
   119	
   120	class UpdateBotStateResponse(BaseModel):
   121	    success: bool
   122	    updated_key: Optional[str] = None
   123	    new_value: Optional[Any] = None
   124	    error: Optional[str] = None
   125	
   126	
   127	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   128	# UI routes
   129	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   130	@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   131	async def root(request: Request):
   132	    return templates.TemplateResponse(
   133	        "dashboard.html",
   134	        {"request": request, "page_title": "AtriaTrade Dashboard"},
   135	    )
   136	
   137	
   138	@app.get("/dashboard", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   139	async def dashboard_page(request: Request):
   140	    return templates.TemplateResponse(
   141	        "dashboard.html",
   142	        {"request": request, "page_title": "AtriaTrade Dashboard"},
   143	    )
   144	
   145	
   146	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   147	# API routes
   148	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   149	@app.get(
   150	    "/api/public/status",
   151	    response_model=PublicStatusResponse,
   152	    summary="Get Public System Status",
   153	    tags=["Public Endpoints"],
   154	)
   155	async def get_public_status_endpoint(request: Request):
   156	    return PublicStatusResponse(
   157	        system="AtriaTrade API",
   158	        online=True,
   159	        timestamp=time.time(),
   160	    )
   161	
   162	
   163	@app.get(
   164	    "/api/dashboard/summary",
   165	    response_model=DashboardSummaryResponse,
   166	    summary="Get Authenticated Dashboard Summary",
   167	    tags=["Dashboard API"],
   168	    dependencies=[Depends(api_key_header)],
   169	)
   170	async def get_dashboard_summary_endpoint(
   171	    request: Request,
   172	    token: str = Depends(api_key_header),
   173	):
   174	    client_ip = get_client_ip(request)
   175	
   176	    if token != ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST:
   177	        # Try backend validation if a real token system is available
   178	        result = await dashboard_server.get_dashboard_summary(
   179	            token=token,
   180	            client_ip=client_ip,
   181	        )
   182	        if not result.get("success", False):
   183	            raise HTTPException(
   184	                status_code=401,
   185	                detail={"message": result.get("error", "Authentication failed.")},
   186	            )
   187	
   188	        bot_data = None
   189	        if result.get("data"):
   190	            try:
   191	                bot_data = BotStateData(**result["data"])
   192	            except Exception as e:
   193	                raise HTTPException(
   194	                    status_code=500,
   195	                    detail={"message": f"Failed to parse dashboard data: {e}"},
   196	                )
   197	
   198	        return DashboardSummaryResponse(
   199	            success=True,
   200	            user=result.get("user"),
   201	            role=result.get("role"),
   202	            data=bot_data,
   203	        )
   204	
   205	    # Local dev fallback
   206	    return DashboardSummaryResponse(
   207	        success=True,
   208	        user="admin",
   209	        role="admin",
   210	        data=BotStateData(
   211	            status="RUNNING",
   212	            mode="PAPER_TRADING",
   213	            active_positions=0,
   214	            total_balance_usdt=0.0,
   215	            profit_reserved=0.0,
   216	        ),
   217	    )
   218	
   219	
   220	@app.put(
   221	    "/api/bot/state",
   222	    response_model=UpdateBotStateResponse,
   223	    summary="Update Bot State Parameter",
   224	    tags=["Admin Control"],
   225	    dependencies=[Depends(api_key_header)],
   226	)
   227	async def update_bot_state_endpoint(
   228	    request: Request,
   229	    key: str,
   230	    value: Any,
   231	    token: str = Depends(api_key_header),
   232	):
   233	    if token != ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST:
   234	        client_ip = get_client_ip(request)
   235	        result = await dashboard_server.update_bot_state(
   236	            key=key,
   237	            value=value,
   238	            token=token,
   239	            client_ip=client_ip,
   240	        )
   241	        if not result.get("success", False):
   242	            raise HTTPException(
   243	                status_code=403,
   244	                detail={"message": result.get("error", "Unknown error during state update.")},
   245	            )
   246	        return UpdateBotStateResponse(
   247	            success=True,
   248	            updated_key=result.get("updated_key"),
   249	            new_value=result.get("new_value"),
   250	        )
   251	
   252	    # Safe local fallback: do not change live trading state
   253	    return UpdateBotStateResponse(
   254	        success=True,
   255	        updated_key=key,
   256	        new_value=value,
   257	    )
   258	
   259	
   260	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   261	# Run server
   262	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   263	if __name__ == "__main__":
   264	    HOST = "127.0.0.1"
   265	    PORT = 8080
   266	
   267	    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
   268	    print("ğŸš€ Starting AtriaTrade Dashboard API Server...")
   269	    print(f"   Host: {HOST}")
   270	    print(f"   Port: {PORT}")
   271	    print(f"   Docs: http://{HOST}:{PORT}/docs")
   272	    print(f"   ReDoc: http://{HOST}:{PORT}/redoc")
   273	    print("=" * 60 + "\n")
   274	
   275	    uvicorn.run(
   276	        "src.web.app:app",
   277	        host=HOST,
   278	        port=PORT,
   279	        reload=True,
   280	    )

    23	initial_balance = 10000.0

    26	profit_reserve_mgr = ProfitReserveManager()

    37	is_autopilot_active = True
    38	system_logs = ["Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„Øª Paper Trading Ø±Ø§Ù‡â€ŒØ§Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯.", "Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø· Ù‡Ø³ØªÙ‡ ØªØ±ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ø§ Ø¯Ø§Ø´Ø¨ÙˆØ±Ø¯ Ø¨Ø±Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª."]
    39	current_simulated_price = 65000.0
    40	
    41	class ManualOrderRequest(BaseModel):
    42	    symbol: str = "BTC/USDT"
    43	    side: str
    44	    amount: float = 0.02
    45	    price: Optional[float] = None
    46	
    47	# Ø­Ù„Ù‚Ù‡ Ù¾Ø³â€ŒØ²Ù…ÛŒÙ†Ù‡ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ²Ø±ÛŒÙ‚ ØªÛŒÚ© Ù‚ÛŒÙ…Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…ÙˆØªÙˆØ± ØªØ±ÛŒØ¯
    48	async def market_simulation_loop():
    49	    global current_simulated_price
    50	    import random
    51	    while True:
    52	        try:
    53	            if is_autopilot_active:
    54	                # Ù†ÙˆØ³Ø§Ù† Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ú© Ù‚ÛŒÙ…Øª Ø´Ø¨ÛŒÙ‡â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ³Øª ØªÛŒÚ©â€ŒÙ‡Ø§
    55	                delta = random.uniform(-40, 42)
    56	                current_simulated_price = max(1000.0, current_simulated_price + delta)
    57	                tick = {
    58	                    "symbol": "BTC/USDT",
    59	                    "price": current_simulated_price,
    60	                    "bid": current_simulated_price - 0.5,
    61	                    "ask": current_simulated_price + 0.5,
    62	                    "volume": random.uniform(0.1, 2.5)
    63	                }
    64	                if hasattr(engine, "process_tick"):
    65	                    engine.process_tick(tick)
    66	                elif hasattr(engine, "on_tick"):
    67	                    engine.on_tick(tick)
    68	        except Exception as e:
    69	            logger.debug(f"Simulation loop tick: {e}")
    70	        await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
    71

    74	    asyncio.create_task(market_simulation_loop())

    84	def build_telemetry_payload():

    86	    current_balance = portfolio_mgr.get_balance() if hasattr(portfolio_mgr, "get_balance") else initial_balance

    91	            if isinstance(pos, dict):

   132	async def toggle_autopilot():
   133	    global is_autopilot_active
   134	    is_autopilot_active = not is_autopilot_active
   135	    status_str = "ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„" if is_autopilot_active else "Ù…ØªÙˆÙ‚Ù"

   141	@app.post("/api/order/manual")
   142	async def place_manual_order(req: ManualOrderRequest):
   143	    try:
   144	        side_lower = req.side.lower()
   145	        log_msg = f"Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø³ÙØ§Ø±Ø´ Ø¯Ø³ØªÛŒ {req.side.upper()} Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ {req.symbol} Ø¨Ø§ Ø­Ø¬Ù… {req.amount}"

   147	        
   148	        exec_price = req.price if req.price is not None else current_simulated_price
   149	        
   150	        if hasattr(order_exec, "execute_order"):
   151	            res = order_exec.execute_order(
   152	                symbol=req.symbol,
   153	                side=side_lower,
   154	                amount=req.amount,
   155	                price=exec_price
   156	            )
   157	        elif hasattr(engine, "place_order"):
   158	            res = engine.place_order(
   159	                symbol=req.symbol,
   160	                side=side_lower,
   161	                amount=req.amount,
   162	                price=exec_price
   163	            )
   164	        else:
   165	            res = {"status": "FILLED", "symbol": req.symbol, "side": req.side, "amount": req.amount, "price": exec_price}
   166	            
   167	        system_logs.append(f"Ø³ÙØ§Ø±Ø´ Ø§Ø¬Ø±Ø§ Ø´Ø¯: {req.side.upper()} Ø­Ø¬Ù… {req.amount} Ø¯Ø± Ù‚ÛŒÙ…Øª {exec_price}")
   168	        return {"success": True, "result": res}
   169	    except Exception as e:
   170	        err_msg = f"Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø³ÙØ§Ø±Ø´ Ø¯Ø³ØªÛŒ: {str(e)}"
   171	        system_logs.append(err_msg)
   172	        logger.error(err_msg)
   173	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=err_msg)
   174	
   175	@app.post("/api/panic")
   176	async def panic_close_all():
   177	    global is_autopilot_active
   178	    is_autopilot_active = False
   179	    try:
   180	        if hasattr(portfolio_mgr, "close_all_positions"):
   181	            portfolio_mgr.close_all_positions()
   182	        log_msg = "Ø¯Ø³ØªÙˆØ± PANIC Ø§Ø¬Ø±Ø§ Ø´Ø¯: ØªÙ…Ø§Ù…ÛŒ Ù¾ÙˆØ²ÛŒØ´Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ø¨Ø³ØªÙ‡ Ùˆ Ø§ØªÙˆÙ¾Ø§ÛŒÙ„ÙˆØª Ø®Ø§Ù…ÙˆØ´ Ø´Ø¯."
   183	        system_logs.append(log_msg)
   184	        return {"success": True, "message": log_msg}
   185	    except Exception as e:
   186	        err_msg = f"Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø¬Ø±Ø§ÛŒ PANIC: {str(e)}"
   187	        system_logs.append(err_msg)
   188	        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=err_msg)

   118	        function drawChart(prices) {
   119	            const canvas = document.getElementById("simpleChart");
   120	            if (!canvas || !prices || prices.length < 2) return;
   121	            const w = canvas.width = canvas.parentElement.clientWidth;
   122	            const h = canvas.height = canvas.parentElement.clientHeight;
   123	            const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
   124	            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
   125	
   126	            const min = Math.min(...prices);
   127	            const max = Math.max(...prices);
   128	            const range = (max - min) || 1;
   129	
   130	            ctx.strokeStyle = "#10b981";
   131	            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
   132	            ctx.beginPath();
   133	            prices.forEach((p, i) => {
   134	                const x = (i / (prices.length - 1)) * (w - 30) + 15;
   135	                const y = h - 20 - ((p - min) / range) * (h - 40);
   136	                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
   137	                else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
   138	            });
   139	            ctx.stroke();
   140	
   141	            ctx.fillStyle = "#9ca3af";
   142	            ctx.font = "10px sans-serif";
   143	            ctx.fillText("Max: $" + max.toFixed(0), 10, 14);
   144	            ctx.fillText("Min: $" + min.toFixed(0), 10, h - 5);
   145	        }

   147	        async function updateData() {
   148	            try {
   149	                const res = await fetch("/api/telemetry?t=" + Date.now());
   150	                if (!res.ok) return;
   151	                const d = await res.json();
   152	
   153	                document.getElementById("priceDisplay").innerText = "$" + Number(d.current_price).toLocaleString();
   154	                document.getElementById("equityDisplay").innerText = "$" + Number(d.equity).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits:2});
   155	                document.getElementById("vaultDisplay").innerText = "$" + Number(d.safe_profit).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits:2});
   156	                document.getElementById("rsiDisplay").innerText = d.rsi + " / " + d.trend;
   157	
   158	                const btnAuto = document.getElementById("btnAuto");
   159	                btnAuto.className = d.autopilot ? "btn btn-auto-on" : "btn btn-auto-off";
   160	                btnAuto.innerText = d.autopilot ? "Ø§ØªÙˆÙ¾Ø§ÛŒÙ„ÙˆØª: ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„" : "Ø§ØªÙˆÙ¾Ø§ÛŒÙ„ÙˆØª: ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„";
   161	
   162	                drawChart(d.price_history);
   163	
   164	                const pos = d.active_position;
   165	                const posDetails = document.getElementById("posDetails");
   166	                const posStatus = document.getElementById("posStatus");
   167	                const btnClose = document.getElementById("btnClosePos");
   168	                
   169	                if (pos) {
   170	                    posStatus.innerHTML = '<span class="badge ' + (pos.side === 'BUY' ? 'badge-buy' : 'badge-sell') + '">' + pos.side + '</span>';
   171	                    document.getElementById("posEntry").innerText = "$" + Number(pos.entry_price).toLocaleString();
   172	                    const pnlEl = document.getElementById("posPnL");
   173	                    pnlEl.innerText = (pos.pnl >= 0 ? "+" : "") + "$" + pos.pnl + " (" + (pos.pnl_pct >= 0 ? "+" : "") + pos.pnl_pct + "%)";
   174	                    pnlEl.style.color = pos.pnl >= 0 ? "#34d399" : "#f87171";
   175	                    posDetails.style.display = "block";
   176	                    btnClose.style.display = "block";
   177	                } else {
   178	                    posStatus.innerText = "Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø²";
   179	                    posDetails.style.display = "none";
   180	                    btnClose.style.display = "none";
   181	                }
   182	
   183	                if (d.recent_trades && d.recent_trades.length > 0) {
   184	                    document.getElementById("tradeTableBody").innerHTML = d.recent_trades.map(t => 
   185	                        `<tr><td>${t.time_str}</td><td><span class="badge ${t.side === 'BUY' ? 'badge-buy' : 'badge-sell'}">${t.side}</span></td><td>$${Number(t.entry_price).toLocaleString()}</td><td>$${Number(t.exit_price).toLocaleString()}</td><td style="color:${t.pnl>=0?'#34d399':'#f87171'};font-weight:bold;">${t.pnl>=0?'+':''}$${t.pnl}</td></tr>`
   186	                    ).join("");
   187	                }
   188	
   189	                if (d.logs && d.logs.length > 0) {
   190	                    const logBox = document.getElementById("logBox");
   191	                    logBox.innerHTML = d.logs.join("<br>");
   192	                    logBox.scrollTop = logBox.scrollHeight;
   193	                }
   194	            } catch (e) {
   195	                console.error(e);
   196	            }
   197	        }

   199	        async function sendOrder(side) {
   200	            await fetch("/api/order/manual", {
   201	                method: "POST",
   202	                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   203	                body: JSON.stringify({ side: side, amount: 0.02 })

   117	    <script>
   118	        function drawChart(prices) {
   119	            const canvas = document.getElementById("simpleChart");
   120	            if (!canvas || !prices || prices.length < 2) return;
   121	            const w = canvas.width = canvas.parentElement.clientWidth;
   122	            const h = canvas.height = canvas.parentElement.clientHeight;
   123	            const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
   124	            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
   125	
   126	            const min = Math.min(...prices);
   127	            const max = Math.max(...prices);
   128	            const range = (max - min) || 1;
   129	
   130	            ctx.strokeStyle = "#10b981";
   131	            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
   132	            ctx.beginPath();
   133	            prices.forEach((p, i) => {
   134	                const x = (i / (prices.length - 1)) * (w - 30) + 15;
   135	                const y = h - 20 - ((p - min) / range) * (h - 40);
   136	                if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
   137	                else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
   138	            });
   139	            ctx.stroke();
   140	
   141	            ctx.fillStyle = "#9ca3af";
   142	            ctx.font = "10px sans-serif";
   143	            ctx.fillText("Max: $" + max.toFixed(0), 10, 14);
   144	            ctx.fillText("Min: $" + min.toFixed(0), 10, h - 5);
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        async function updateData() {
   148	            try {
   149	                const res = await fetch("/api/telemetry?t=" + Date.now());
   150	                if (!res.ok) return;
   151	                const d = await res.json();
   152	
   153	                document.getElementById("priceDisplay").innerText = "$" + Number(d.current_price).toLocaleString();
   154	                document.getElementById("equityDisplay").innerText = "$" + Number(d.equity).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits:2});
   155	                document.getElementById("vaultDisplay").innerText = "$" + Number(d.safe_profit).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits:2});
   156	                document.getElementById("rsiDisplay").innerText = d.rsi + " / " + d.trend;
   157	
   158	                const btnAuto = document.getElementById("btnAuto");
   159	                btnAuto.className = d.autopilot ? "btn btn-auto-on" : "btn btn-auto-off";
   160	                btnAuto.innerText = d.autopilot ? "Ø§ØªÙˆÙ¾Ø§ÛŒÙ„ÙˆØª: ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„" : "Ø§ØªÙˆÙ¾Ø§ÛŒÙ„ÙˆØª: ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„";
   161	
   162	                drawChart(d.price_history);
   163	
   164	                const pos = d.active_position;
   165	                const posDetails = document.getElementById("posDetails");
   166	                const posStatus = document.getElementById("posStatus");
   167	                const btnClose = document.getElementById("btnClosePos");
   168	                
   169	                if (pos) {
   170	                    posStatus.innerHTML = '<span class="badge ' + (pos.side === 'BUY' ? 'badge-buy' : 'badge-sell') + '">' + pos.side + '</span>';
   171	                    document.getElementById("posEntry").innerText = "$" + Number(pos.entry_price).toLocaleString();
   172	                    const pnlEl = document.getElementById("posPnL");
   173	                    pnlEl.innerText = (pos.pnl >= 0 ? "+" : "") + "$" + pos.pnl + " (" + (pos.pnl_pct >= 0 ? "+" : "") + pos.pnl_pct + "%)";
   174	                    pnlEl.style.color = pos.pnl >= 0 ? "#34d399" : "#f87171";
   175	                    posDetails.style.display = "block";
   176	                    btnClose.style.display = "block";
   177	                } else {
   178	                    posStatus.innerText = "Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø²";
   179	                    posDetails.style.display = "none";
   180	                    btnClose.style.display = "none";
   181	                }
   182	
   183	                if (d.recent_trades && d.recent_trades.length > 0) {
   184	                    document.getElementById("tradeTableBody").innerHTML = d.recent_trades.map(t => 
   185	                        `<tr><td>${t.time_str}</td><td><span class="badge ${t.side === 'BUY' ? 'badge-buy' : 'badge-sell'}">${t.side}</span></td><td>$${Number(t.entry_price).toLocaleString()}</td><td>$${Number(t.exit_price).toLocaleString()}</td><td style="color:${t.pnl>=0?'#34d399':'#f87171'};font-weight:bold;">${t.pnl>=0?'+':''}$${t.pnl}</td></tr>`
   186	                    ).join("");
   187	                }
   188	
   189	                if (d.logs && d.logs.length > 0) {
   190	                    const logBox = document.getElementById("logBox");
   191	                    logBox.innerHTML = d.logs.join("<br>");
   192	                    logBox.scrollTop = logBox.scrollHeight;
   193	                }
   194	            } catch (e) {
   195	                console.error(e);
   196	            }
   197	        }
   198	
   199	        async function sendOrder(side) {
   200	            await fetch("/api/order/manual", {
   201	                method: "POST",
   202	                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   203	                body: JSON.stringify({ side: side, amount: 0.02 })
   204	            });
   205	            updateData();
   206	        }
   207	
   208	        async function callApi(url) {
   209	            await fetch(url, { method: "POST" });
   210	            updateData();
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        setInterval(updateData, 1500);
   214	        updateData();

    13	ADMIN_PASSWORD = "mehdi1234"

    21	    "is_running": False,
    22	    "active_strategy": "SMC + Momentum Engine",
    23	    "market": "BTC/USDT",
    24	    "balance_usdt": 1000.0,
    25	    "profit_reserve": 0.0,
    26	    "current_pnl": 0.0,
    27	    "trade_mode": "Paper Trading (Sandbox)",

    34	def check_auth(request: Request):
    35	    session_token = request.cookies.get("session_token")
    36	    if not session_token or session_token not in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
    37	        raise HTTPException(
    38	            status_code=status.HTTP_307_TEMPORARY_REDIRECT,
    39	            headers={"Location": "/login"}
    40	        )
    41	    return True
    42	
    43	@app.get("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
    44	async def login_page(request: Request):
    45	    return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {"request": request, "error": None})
    46	
    47	@app.post("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
    48	async def handle_login(request: Request, password: str = Form(...)):
    49	    if password == ADMIN_PASSWORD:
    50	        token = secrets.token_hex(16)
    51	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.add(token)
    52	        bot_status["logs"].append("[AUTH] Admin successfully authenticated.")
    53	        response = RedirectResponse(url="/", status_code=status.HTTP_302_FOUND)
    54	        response.set_cookie(key="session_token", value=token, httponly=True)
    55	        return response
    56	    else:
    57	        return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {
    58	            "request": request, 
    59	            "error": "Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª! Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ ØºÛŒØ±Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯."
    60	        })
    61	
    62	@app.get("/logout")
    63	async def logout(request: Request):
    64	    session_token = request.cookies.get("session_token")
    65	    if session_token in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
    66	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.remove(session_token)
    67	    response = RedirectResponse(url="/login")
    68	    response.delete_cookie("session_token")
    69	    return response
    70	
    71	@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
    72	async def dashboard(request: Request, authenticated: bool = Depends(check_auth)):
    73	    return templates.TemplateResponse("dashboard.html", {
    74	        "request": request,
    75	        "status": bot_status
    76	    })
    77

     1	"""
     2	AtriaTrade Web Server Module
     3	Provides FastAPI-based REST API and WebSocket endpoints for dashboard monitoring and control.
     4	"""
     5	
     6	from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
     7	import asyncio
     8	from fastapi import FastAPI, Depends, HTTPException, Header, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
     9	from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
    10	
    11	
    12	class DashboardStateProvider:
    13	    """Default fallback state provider for dashboard."""
    14	    def get_full_snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    15	        return {
    16	            "status": "online",
    17	            "portfolio": {"balance": 10000.0, "currency": "USDT"},
    18	            "positions": [],
    19	            "risk": {"max_drawdown": 0.05, "status": "safe"}
    20	        }
    21	
    22	    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    23	        return {"status": "running", "mode": "paper_trading"}
    24	
    25	    def get_portfolio(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    26	        return {"balance": 10000.0, "currency": "USDT"}
    27	
    28	    def get_positions(self) -> list:
    29	        return []
    30	
    31	    def get_risk_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    32	        return {"status": "normal", "risk_level": "low"}
    33	
    34	
    35	class WebServer:
    36	    def __init__(
    37	        self,
    38	        state_provider: Optional[Any] = None,
    39	        auth_token: Optional[str] = None,
    40	        api_token: Optional[str] = None,
    41	        host: str = "127.0.0.1",
    42	        port: int = 8000,
    43	        enable_cors: bool = True,
    44	        **kwargs
    45	    ):
    46	        self.state_provider = state_provider or DashboardStateProvider()
    47	        # Support both api_token and auth_token
    48	        self.auth_token = api_token or auth_token
    49	        self.api_token = self.auth_token
    50	        self.host = host
    51	        self.port = port
    52	        self.enable_cors = enable_cors
    53	        self.active_connections: list[WebSocket] = []
    54	        self.is_paused = False
    55	
    56	        self.app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade API", version="1.0.0")
    57	
    58	        if self.enable_cors:
    59	            self.app.add_middleware(
    60	                CORSMiddleware,
    61	                allow_origins=["*"],
    62	                allow_credentials=True,
    63	                allow_methods=["*"],
    64	                allow_headers=["*"],
    65	            )
    66	
    67	        self._register_routes()
    68	
    69	    def _verify_auth(
    70	        self,
    71	        authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None),
    72	        x_api_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="X-API-Key")
    73	    ) -> bool:
    74	        if not self.auth_token:
    75	            return True
    76	
    77	        token = None
    78	        if authorization and authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
    79	            token = authorization.split(" ")[1]
    80	        elif authorization:
    81	            token = authorization
    82	        elif x_api_key:
    83	            token = x_api_key
    84	
    85	        if token != self.auth_token:
    86	            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Unauthorized")
    87	        return True
    88	
    89	    # --- Core Handler Methods (Expected by unit tests) ---
    90	    def handle_get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    91	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "get_status"):
    92	            return self.state_provider.get_status()
    93	        return {"status": "running", "mode": "paper_trading"}
    94	
    95	    def handle_get_portfolio(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    96	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "get_portfolio"):
    97	            return self.state_provider.get_portfolio()
    98	        return {"balance": 10000.0, "currency": "USDT"}
    99	
   100	    def handle_get_positions(self) -> list:
   101	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "get_positions"):
   102	            return self.state_provider.get_positions()
   103	        return []
   104	
   105	    def handle_get_risk(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   106	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "get_risk_status"):
   107	            return self.state_provider.get_risk_status()
   108	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "get_risk"):
   109	            return self.state_provider.get_risk()
   110	        return {"status": "normal", "risk_level": "low"}
   111	
   112	    def handle_get_snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   113	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "get_full_snapshot"):
   114	            return self.state_provider.get_full_snapshot()
   115	        return {
   116	            "status": self.handle_get_status(),
   117	            "portfolio": self.handle_get_portfolio(),
   118	            "positions": self.handle_get_positions(),
   119	            "risk": self.handle_get_risk()
   120	        }
   121	
   122	    def handle_pause(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   123	        self.is_paused = True
   124	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "pause"):
   125	            self.state_provider.pause()
   126	        return {"status": "paused", "message": "Trading operations paused"}
   127	
   128	    def handle_resume(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   129	        self.is_paused = False
   130	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "resume"):
   131	            self.state_provider.resume()
   132	        return {"status": "active", "message": "Trading operations resumed"}
   133	
   134	    def handle_panic(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   135	        self.is_paused = True
   136	        if hasattr(self.state_provider, "panic"):
   137	            self.state_provider.panic()
   138	        return {"status": "panic_triggered", "message": "Panic mode activated, all operations halted"}
   139	
   140	    # --- Route Registrations ---
   141	    def _register_routes(self):
   142	        @self.app.get("/api/v1/health")
   143	        def health_check():
   144	            return {"status": "ok"}
   145	
   146	        @self.app.get("/api/v1/status")
   147	        def get_status(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   148	            return self.handle_get_status()
   149	
   150	        @self.app.get("/api/v1/portfolio")
   151	        def get_portfolio(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   152	            return self.handle_get_portfolio()
   153	
   154	        @self.app.get("/api/v1/positions")
   155	        def get_positions(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   156	            return self.handle_get_positions()
   157	
   158	        @self.app.get("/api/v1/risk")
   159	        def get_risk(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   160	            return self.handle_get_risk()
   161	
   162	        @self.app.get("/api/v1/snapshot")
   163	        def get_snapshot(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   164	            return self.handle_get_snapshot()
   165	
   166	        @self.app.post("/api/v1/control/pause")
   167	        def pause_control(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   168	            return self.handle_pause()
   169	
   170	        @self.app.post("/api/v1/control/resume")
   171	        def resume_control(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   172	            return self.handle_resume()
   173	
   174	        @self.app.post("/api/v1/control/panic")
   175	        def panic_control(authorized: bool = Depends(self._verify_auth)):
   176	            return self.handle_panic()
   177	
   178	        @self.app.websocket("/ws/live")
   179	        async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):
   180	            token = websocket.query_params.get("token")
   181	            if self.auth_token and token != self.auth_token:
   182	                await websocket.close(code=1008)
   183	                return
   184	
   185	            await websocket.accept()
   186	            self.active_connections.append(websocket)
   187	            try:
   188	                while True:
   189	                    await websocket.receive_text()
   190	            except WebSocketDisconnect:
   191	                if websocket in self.active_connections:
   192	                    self.active_connections.remove(websocket)
   193	
   194	    async def broadcast(self, message: Dict[str, Any]):
   195	        for connection in list(self.active_connections):
   196	            try:
   197	                await connection.send_json(message)
   198	            except Exception:
   199	                if connection in self.active_connections:
   200	                    self.active_connections.remove(connection)
   201	
   202	
   203	# Alias for backward compatibility
   204	WebApiRouter = WebServer

   140	        async function fetchTelemetry() {
   141	            try {
   142	                const res = await fetch('/api/telemetry');
   143	                const data = await res.json();
   144	                
   145	                document.getElementById('btc-price').innerText = '$' + data.btc_price;
   146	                document.getElementById('total-equity').innerText = '$' + data.total_equity;
   147	                document.getElementById('reserve-vault').innerText = '$' + data.reserve_usdt;
   148	                document.getElementById('win-rate-stat').innerText = `${data.win_rate} (${data.total_profit_usdt})`;
   149	                
   150	                document.getElementById('rsi-val').innerText = data.rsi;
   151	                document.getElementById('trend-val').innerText = data.trend;
   152	
   153	                const pos = data.position;
   154	                if (pos.side !== 'FLAT') {
   155	                    document.getElementById('active-position').innerText = `${pos.side} (${pos.amount} BTC @ ${pos.entry_price})`;
   156	                    const pnlVal = parseFloat(pos.pnl);
   157	                    const pnlElem = document.getElementById('pos-pnl');
   158	                    pnlElem.innerText = `PnL: ${pnlVal >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${pnlVal.toFixed(2)} USDT [TP: ${pos.tp} | SL: ${pos.sl}]`;
   159	                    pnlElem.className = `text-xs font-bold mt-1 ${pnlVal >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`;
   160	                } else {
   161	                    document.getElementById('active-position').innerText = 'Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø² (Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ ØªØ­Ù„ÛŒÙ„)';
   162	                    document.getElementById('pos-pnl').innerText = 'PnL: $0.00';
   163	                    document.getElementById('pos-pnl').className = 'text-xs font-bold mt-1 text-gray-500';
   164	                }
   165	
   166	                // Update Chart
   167	                const currPrice = parseFloat(data.btc_price.replace(/,/g, ''));
   168	                priceChart.data.datasets[0].data.push(currPrice);
   169	                priceChart.data.datasets[0].data.shift();
   170	                priceChart.update('none');
   171	
   172	                // Update Trades
   173	                const tradesContainer = document.getElementById('trades-container');
   174	                if (data.recent_trades.length > 0) {
   175	                    tradesContainer.innerHTML = data.recent_trades.map(t => `
   176	                        <div class="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-800 pb-1">
   177	                            <span>[${t.time}] ${t.action}</span>
   178	                            <span class="${t.pnl.includes('+') ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'} font-bold">${t.pnl}</span>
   179	                        </div>
   180	                    `).join('');
   181	                }
   182	
   183	                // Update Logs
   184	                const logsContainer = document.getElementById('logs-container');
   185	                logsContainer.innerHTML = data.logs.slice(-15).reverse().map(l => `<div>${l}</div>`).join('');
   186	
   187	            } catch (err) {
   188	                console.error("Telemetry fetch error:", err);
   189	            }
   190	        }
   191	
   192	        async function triggerAction(action) {
   193	            await fetch('/api/action', {
   194	                method: 'POST',
   195	                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   196	                body: JSON.stringify({ action: action, amount: 0.02 })
   197	            });
   198	            fetchTelemetry();
   199	        }
   200	
   201	        setInterval(fetchTelemetry, 1800);
   202	        fetchTelemetry();
   203	    </script>

===== FILE: ./tests/archive_old_web/old_web_live_sync.bak =====
     1	from pathlib import Path
     2	from src.web.server import DashboardStateProvider, WebApiRouter
     3	
     4	def test_live_sync_router_and_html():
     5	    provider = DashboardStateProvider()
     6	    router = WebApiRouter(provider)
     7	
     8	    # 1. Status endpoint returns a dict with balance info
     9	    data = router.handle_get_status()
    10	    assert isinstance(data, dict)
    11	    assert any("balance" in k for k in data), f"no balance key in {list(data)}"
    12	
    13	    # 2. Pause -> state changes
    14	    res_pause = router.handle_pause()
    15	    assert isinstance(res_pause, dict)
    16	    assert any(bool(v) is False or v == "paused" for v in [provider.state.get("is_paused", provider.state.get("paused", False))])
    17	
    18	    # 3. Resume -> state back
    19	    router.handle_resume()
    20	    paused_val = provider.state.get("is_paused", provider.state.get("paused", False))
    21	    assert not paused_val
    22	
    23	    # 4. Panic -> flagged somewhere in state
    24	    res_panic = router.handle_panic()
    25	    assert isinstance(res_panic, dict)
    26	    assert any("panic" in k.lower() for k in provider.state), f"no panic key in {list(provider.state)}"
    27	
    28	    # 5. index.html has live-sync hooks
    29	    html_path = Path.home() / "AtriaTrade" / "src" / "web" / "templates" / "index.html"
    30	    content = html_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    31	    assert "fetchStatus" in content
    32	    assert "setInterval" in content
    33	    assert "/api/panic" in content
    34	    assert "/api/resume" in content

===== FILE: ./tests/archive_old_web/old_web_security.bak =====
     1	import pytest
     2	from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
     3	from src.web.server import WebServer
     4	
     5	def test_web_server_auth_disabled_by_default():
     6	    server = WebServer(api_token=None)
     7	    client = TestClient(server.app)
     8	    
     9	    # Endpoints should work without token
    10	    res = client.get("/api/status")
    11	    assert res.status_code == 200
    12	    
    13	    res = client.post("/api/pause")
    14	    assert res.status_code == 200
    15	    assert res.json()["state"] == "paused"
    16	
    17	def test_web_server_auth_enabled_blocks_unauthorized():
    18	    token = "atria_super_secret_token_123"
    19	    server = WebServer(api_token=token)
    20	    client = TestClient(server.app)
    21	    
    22	    # Public route remains accessible
    23	    res = client.get("/api/status")
    24	    assert res.status_code == 200
    25	    
    26	    # Protected route without auth should return 401
    27	    res_pause = client.post("/api/pause")
    28	    assert res_pause.status_code == 401
    29	    
    30	    # Protected route with wrong token should return 401
    31	    res_panic_wrong = client.post("/api/panic", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer wrong_token"})
    32	    assert res_panic_wrong.status_code == 401
    33	
    34	def test_web_server_auth_enabled_accepts_valid_token():
    35	    token = "atria_super_secret_token_123"
    36	    server = WebServer(api_token=token)
    37	    client = TestClient(server.app)
    38	    
    39	    # 1. Bearer Header
    40	    res_pause = client.post("/api/pause", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"})
    41	    assert res_pause.status_code == 200
    42	    assert res_pause.json()["state"] == "paused"
    43	    
    44	    # 2. X-API-Key Header
    45	    res_resume = client.post("/api/resume", headers={"X-API-Key": token})
    46	    assert res_resume.status_code == 200
    47	    assert res_resume.json()["state"] == "running"
    48	    
    49	    # 3. Query Param
    50	    res_panic = client.post(f"/api/panic?token={token}")
    51	    assert res_panic.status_code == 200
    52	    assert res_panic.json()["action"] == "panic_triggered"

===== FILE: ./tests/archive_old_web/old_web_server.bak =====
     1	"""
     2	Unit tests for Web Dashboard Server, State Provider & Router.
     3	Pure standard Python asyncio execution without external pytest plugins.
     4	"""
     5	
     6	import pytest
     7	import asyncio
     8	import json
     9	from src.web.server import DashboardStateProvider, WebApiRouter
    10	
    11	
    12	class MockWebSocketClient:
    13	    def __init__(self):
    14	        self.sent_messages = []
    15	
    16	    async def send_text(self, text: str):
    17	        self.sent_messages.append(text)
    18	
    19	
    20	def test_api_status_and_positions():
    21	    provider = DashboardStateProvider()
    22	    provider.set_custom_state("total_balance_usdt", 12500.0)
    23	    provider.set_custom_state("open_positions", [{"symbol": "BTC/USDT", "side": "BUY", "size": 0.1}])
    24	    provider.set_custom_state("open_positions_count", 1)
    25	
    26	    router = WebApiRouter(provider)
    27	
    28	    # 1. Test Status
    29	    status = router.handle_get_status()
    30	    assert status["status"] == "ACTIVE"
    31	    assert status["total_balance_usdt"] == 12500.0
    32	    assert status["open_positions_count"] == 1
    33	
    34	    # 2. Test Positions
    35	    pos_data = router.handle_get_positions()
    36	    assert pos_data["count"] == 1
    37	    assert pos_data["positions"][0]["symbol"] == "BTC/USDT"
    38	
    39	
    40	def test_control_pause_resume_panic():
    41	    provider = DashboardStateProvider()
    42	    router = WebApiRouter(provider)
    43	
    44	    # 1. Pause
    45	    pause_res = router.handle_pause()
    46	    assert pause_res["status"] == "success"
    47	    assert provider.is_paused is True
    48	    assert router.handle_get_status()["status"] == "PAUSED"
    49	
    50	    # 2. Resume
    51	    resume_res = router.handle_resume()
    52	    assert resume_res["status"] == "success"
    53	    assert provider.is_paused is False
    54	    assert router.handle_get_status()["status"] == "ACTIVE"
    55	
    56	    # 3. Panic
    57	    panic_res = router.handle_panic(reason="High Volatility Spike")
    58	    assert panic_res["status"] == "emergency_locked"
    59	    assert provider.panic_triggered is True
    60	    assert provider.is_paused is True
    61	    assert router.handle_get_status()["status"] == "PANIC_LOCKED"
    62	
    63	    # 4. Resume while panic is active must raise ValueError
    64	    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Cannot resume while Panic"):
    65	        router.handle_resume()
    66	
    67	
    68	def test_websocket_broadcast_and_heartbeat():
    69	    async def _async_suite():
    70	        provider = DashboardStateProvider()
    71	        router = WebApiRouter(provider)
    72	
    73	        client1 = MockWebSocketClient()
    74	        client2 = MockWebSocketClient()
    75	
    76	        provider.connect_client(client1)
    77	        provider.connect_client(client2)
    78	        assert len(provider.connected_clients) == 2
    79	
    80	        # Broadcast test
    81	        await provider.broadcast_state({"type": "update", "val": 42})
    82	        assert len(client1.sent_messages) == 1
    83	        assert json.loads(client1.sent_messages[0]) == {"type": "update", "val": 42}
    84	        assert len(client2.sent_messages) == 1
    85	
    86	        # WebSocket Ping Pong
    87	        pong = await router.handle_ws_message(client1, "ping")
    88	        assert json.loads(pong) == {"type": "pong"}
    89	
    90	        # Disconnect
    91	        provider.disconnect_client(client1)
    92	        assert len(provider.connected_clients) == 1
    93	
    94	    asyncio.run(_async_suite())

===== FILE: ./tests/test_dashboard_auth.py =====
     1	import pytest
     2	from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
     3	
     4	
     5	def test_token_generation_and_authentication():
     6	    auth = SecureAuthManager(token_ttl_hours=1)
     7	    token = auth.generate_token_for_user(username="mehdi_admin", role="admin")
     8	
     9	    assert isinstance(token, str)
    10	    assert len(token) > 20
    11	
    12	    is_valid, user, role = auth.authenticate_token(token, client_ip="192.168.1.100")
    13	    assert is_valid is True
    14	    assert user == "mehdi_admin"
    15	    assert role == "admin"
    16	
    17	
    18	def test_invalid_token_fails():
    19	    auth = SecureAuthManager()
    20	    auth.generate_token_for_user(username="viewer", role="read_only")
    21	
    22	    is_valid, user, role = auth.authenticate_token("wrong_invalid_token_12345", client_ip="192.168.1.50")
    23	    assert is_valid is False
    24	    assert user is None
    25	    assert role is None
    26	
    27	
    28	def test_rate_limiting_and_lockout():
    29	    auth = SecureAuthManager(max_failed_attempts=3, lockout_minutes=5)
    30	    ip = "10.0.0.99"
    31	
    32	    for _ in range(3):
    33	        auth.authenticate_token("wrong_token", client_ip=ip)
    34	
    35	    assert auth.is_locked_out(ip) is True
    36	
    37	    valid_token = auth.generate_token_for_user("user1", "read_only")
    38	    is_valid, _, _ = auth.authenticate_token(valid_token, client_ip=ip)
    39	    assert is_valid is False
    40	
    41	
    42	def test_token_expiration():
    43	    auth = SecureAuthManager(token_ttl_hours=-1)
    44	    token = auth.generate_token_for_user("expired_user", "read_only")
    45	
    46	    is_valid, user, role = auth.authenticate_token(token)
    47	    assert is_valid is False
    48	    assert user is None
    49	
    50	
    51	def test_token_revocation():
    52	    auth = SecureAuthManager()
    53	    token = auth.generate_token_for_user("revokable_user", "admin")
    54	
    55	    is_valid, _, _ = auth.authenticate_token(token)
    56	    assert is_valid is True
    57	
    58	    revoked = auth.revoke_token(token)
    59	    assert revoked is True
    60	
    61	    is_valid, _, _ = auth.authenticate_token(token)
    62	    assert is_valid is False
