===== FILE: ./web_runtime_sources.txt =====
     1	===== src/web/app.py =====
     2	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     3	"""
     4	AtriaTrade Web App
     5	- Serves the dashboard UI
     6	- Keeps current backend APIs
     7	- Paper-trading safe only
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import os
    13	import time
    14	from typing import Any, Optional
    15	
    16	import uvicorn
    17	from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
    18	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
    19	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
    20	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    21	from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
    22	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    23	
    24	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	# Project imports
    26	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    27	try:
    28	    from src.dashboard.server import DashboardServer
    29	    from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
    30	except ImportError as e:
    31	    print(f"âŒ ImportError: Could not import dashboard components. {e}")
    32	    print("   Please ensure:")
    33	    print("   1. You are running this script from the root directory of AtriaTrade (~/AtriaTrade).")
    34	    print("   2. The directory structure includes src/dashboard/server.py and src/dashboard/auth.py.")
    35	    raise
    36	
    37	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    38	# Configuration
    39	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    40	AUTH_TOKEN_HEADER_NAME = "X-Auth-Token"
    41	ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST = "SET_YOUR_ADMIN_TOKEN_HERE"  # replace with a real token if needed
    42	
    43	WEB_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    44	TEMPLATES_DIR = os.path.join(WEB_DIR, "templates")
    45	STATIC_DIR = os.path.join(WEB_DIR, "static")
    46	
    47	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    48	# Auth / dashboard backend
    49	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    50	auth_manager = SecureAuthManager()
    51	dashboard_server = DashboardServer(auth_manager=auth_manager)
    52	
    53	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    54	# FastAPI app
    55	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    56	app = FastAPI(
    57	    title="AtriaTrade Dashboard API",
    58	    description="API for managing and monitoring the AtriaTrade bot. Paper-trading safe.",
    59	    version="1.2.0",
    60	)
    61	
    62	api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name=AUTH_TOKEN_HEADER_NAME, auto_error=True)
    63	
    64	templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=TEMPLATES_DIR)
    65	
    66	if os.path.isdir(STATIC_DIR):
    67	    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=STATIC_DIR), name="static")
    68	
    69	
    70	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    71	# Helpers
    72	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    73	def get_client_ip(request: Request) -> str:
    74	    if request.client and request.client.host:
    75	        return request.client.host
    76	
    77	    forwarded_for = request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For")
    78	    if forwarded_for:
    79	        return forwarded_for.split(",")[0].strip()
    80	
    81	    return "127.0.0.1"
    82	
    83	
    84	def require_auth_token(token: str) -> None:
    85	    if token == ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST:
    86	        return
    87	    # If you later generate dynamic tokens with SecureAuthManager,
    88	    # the dashboard_server layer can validate them.
    89	    # This fallback keeps local development simple.
    90	    raise HTTPException(
    91	        status_code=401,
    92	        detail={"message": "Unauthorized. Invalid or expired token."},
    93	    )
    94	
    95	
    96	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    97	# Models
    98	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    99	class PublicStatusResponse(BaseModel):
   100	    system: str = Field(..., description="System name")
   101	    online: bool = Field(..., description="System online state")
   102	    timestamp: float = Field(..., description="Unix time")
   103	
   104	
   105	class BotStateData(BaseModel):
   106	    status: str
   107	    mode: str
   108	    active_positions: int
   109	    total_balance_usdt: float
   110	    profit_reserved: float
   111	
   112	
   113	class DashboardSummaryResponse(BaseModel):
   114	    success: bool
   115	    user: Optional[str] = None
   116	    role: Optional[str] = None
   117	    data: Optional[BotStateData] = None
   118	    error: Optional[str] = None
   119	
   120	
   121	class UpdateBotStateResponse(BaseModel):
   122	    success: bool
   123	    updated_key: Optional[str] = None
   124	    new_value: Optional[Any] = None
   125	    error: Optional[str] = None
   126	
   127	
   128	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   129	# UI routes
   130	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   131	@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   132	async def root(request: Request):
   133	    return templates.TemplateResponse(
   134	        "dashboard.html",
   135	        {"request": request, "page_title": "AtriaTrade Dashboard"},
   136	    )
   137	
   138	
   139	@app.get("/dashboard", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   140	async def dashboard_page(request: Request):
   141	    return templates.TemplateResponse(
   142	        "dashboard.html",
   143	        {"request": request, "page_title": "AtriaTrade Dashboard"},
   144	    )
   145	
   146	
   147	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   148	# API routes
   149	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   150	@app.get(
   151	    "/api/public/status",
   152	    response_model=PublicStatusResponse,
   153	    summary="Get Public System Status",
   154	    tags=["Public Endpoints"],
   155	)
   156	async def get_public_status_endpoint(request: Request):
   157	    return PublicStatusResponse(
   158	        system="AtriaTrade API",
   159	        online=True,
   160	        timestamp=time.time(),
   161	    )
   162	
   163	
   164	@app.get(
   165	    "/api/dashboard/summary",
   166	    response_model=DashboardSummaryResponse,
   167	    summary="Get Authenticated Dashboard Summary",
   168	    tags=["Dashboard API"],
   169	    dependencies=[Depends(api_key_header)],
   170	)
   171	async def get_dashboard_summary_endpoint(
   172	    request: Request,
   173	    token: str = Depends(api_key_header),
   174	):
   175	    client_ip = get_client_ip(request)
   176	
   177	    if token != ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST:
   178	        # Try backend validation if a real token system is available
   179	        result = await dashboard_server.get_dashboard_summary(
   180	            token=token,
   181	            client_ip=client_ip,
   182	        )
   183	        if not result.get("success", False):
   184	            raise HTTPException(
   185	                status_code=401,
   186	                detail={"message": result.get("error", "Authentication failed.")},
   187	            )
   188	
   189	        bot_data = None
   190	        if result.get("data"):
   191	            try:
   192	                bot_data = BotStateData(**result["data"])
   193	            except Exception as e:
   194	                raise HTTPException(
   195	                    status_code=500,
   196	                    detail={"message": f"Failed to parse dashboard data: {e}"},
   197	                )
   198	
   199	        return DashboardSummaryResponse(
   200	            success=True,
   201	            user=result.get("user"),
   202	            role=result.get("role"),
   203	            data=bot_data,
   204	        )
   205	
   206	    # Local dev fallback
   207	    return DashboardSummaryResponse(
   208	        success=True,
   209	        user="admin",
   210	        role="admin",
   211	        data=BotStateData(
   212	            status="RUNNING",
   213	            mode="PAPER_TRADING",
   214	            active_positions=0,
   215	            total_balance_usdt=0.0,
   216	            profit_reserved=0.0,
   217	        ),
   218	    )
   219	
   220	
   221	@app.put(
   222	    "/api/bot/state",
   223	    response_model=UpdateBotStateResponse,
   224	    summary="Update Bot State Parameter",
   225	    tags=["Admin Control"],
   226	    dependencies=[Depends(api_key_header)],
   227	)
   228	async def update_bot_state_endpoint(
   229	    request: Request,
   230	    key: str,
   231	    value: Any,
   232	    token: str = Depends(api_key_header),
   233	):
   234	    if token != ADMIN_TOKEN_FOR_TEST:
   235	        client_ip = get_client_ip(request)
   236	        result = await dashboard_server.update_bot_state(
   237	            key=key,
   238	            value=value,
   239	            token=token,
   240	            client_ip=client_ip,
   241	        )
   242	        if not result.get("success", False):
   243	            raise HTTPException(
   244	                status_code=403,
   245	                detail={"message": result.get("error", "Unknown error during state update.")},
   246	            )
   247	        return UpdateBotStateResponse(
   248	            success=True,
   249	            updated_key=result.get("updated_key"),
   250	            new_value=result.get("new_value"),
   251	        )
   252	
   253	    # Safe local fallback: do not change live trading state
   254	    return UpdateBotStateResponse(
   255	        success=True,
   256	        updated_key=key,
   257	        new_value=value,
   258	    )
   259	
   260	
   261	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   262	# Run server
   263	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   264	if __name__ == "__main__":
   265	    HOST = "127.0.0.1"
   266	    PORT = 8080
   267	
   268	    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
   269	    print("ğŸš€ Starting AtriaTrade Dashboard API Server...")
   270	    print(f"   Host: {HOST}")
   271	    print(f"   Port: {PORT}")
   272	    print(f"   Docs: http://{HOST}:{PORT}/docs")
   273	    print(f"   ReDoc: http://{HOST}:{PORT}/redoc")
   274	    print("=" * 60 + "\n")
   275	
   276	    uvicorn.run(
   277	        "src.web.app:app",
   278	        host=HOST,
   279	        port=PORT,
   280	        reload=True,
   281	    )
   282

   284	import os

   294	TEMPLATES_DIR = WEB_DIR / "templates"

   309	templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(TEMPLATES_DIR))

   319	===== src/web/server.py =====
   320	import secrets
   321	from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, Form, Depends, HTTPException, status
   322	from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse, JSONResponse
   323	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
   324	from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
   325	import uvicorn
   326	import os
   327	
   328	app = FastAPI(title="AtriaTrade Web Console")
   329	
   330	# Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª ØªÙˆÚ©Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø³Ø´Ù† Ø³Ø§Ø¯Ù‡
   331	ACTIVE_SESSIONS = set()
   332	ADMIN_PASSWORD = "mehdi1234"
   333	
   334	# ØªØ¹Ø±ÛŒÙ Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± ØªÙ…Ù¾Ù„ÛŒØªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§
   335	current_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
   336	templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=os.path.join(current_dir, "templates"))
   337	
   338	# ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ù…Ø§Ú© Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ³Øª Ø§ÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙ‡
   339	bot_status = {
   340	    "is_running": False,
   341	    "active_strategy": "SMC + Momentum Engine",
   342	    "market": "BTC/USDT",
   343	    "balance_usdt": 1000.0,
   344	    "profit_reserve": 0.0,
   345	    "current_pnl": 0.0,
   346	    "trade_mode": "Paper Trading (Sandbox)",
   347	    "logs": [
   348	        "[SYSTEM] Security layer initialized.",
   349	        "[AUTH] Console locked. Awaiting admin session."
   350	    ]
   351	}
   352	
   353	def check_auth(request: Request):
   354	    session_token = request.cookies.get("session_token")
   355	    if not session_token or session_token not in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
   356	        raise HTTPException(
   357	            status_code=status.HTTP_307_TEMPORARY_REDIRECT,
   358	            headers={"Location": "/login"}
   359	        )
   360	    return True
   361	
   362	@app.get("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   363	async def login_page(request: Request):
   364	    return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {"request": request, "error": None})
   365	
   366	@app.post("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   367	async def handle_login(request: Request, password: str = Form(...)):
   368	    if password == ADMIN_PASSWORD:
   369	        token = secrets.token_hex(16)
   370	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.add(token)
   371	        bot_status["logs"].append("[AUTH] Admin successfully authenticated.")
   372	        response = RedirectResponse(url="/", status_code=status.HTTP_302_FOUND)
   373	        response.set_cookie(key="session_token", value=token, httponly=True)
   374	        return response
   375	    else:
   376	        return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {
   377	            "request": request, 
   378	            "error": "Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª! Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ ØºÛŒØ±Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯."
   379	        })
   380	
   381	@app.get("/logout")
   382	async def logout(request: Request):
   383	    session_token = request.cookies.get("session_token")
   384	    if session_token in ACTIVE_SESSIONS:
   385	        ACTIVE_SESSIONS.remove(session_token)
   386	    response = RedirectResponse(url="/login")
   387	    response.delete_cookie("session_token")
   388	    return response
   389	
   390	@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   391	async def dashboard(request: Request, authenticated: bool = Depends(check_auth)):
   392	    return templates.TemplateResponse("dashboard.html", {
   393	        "request": request,
   394	        "status": bot_status
   395	    })
   396

   399	    return JSONResponse(content=bot_status)

   556	        async function fetchTelemetry() {
   557	            try {
   558	                const res = await fetch('/api/telemetry');
   559	                const data = await res.json();
   560	                
   561	                document.getElementById('btc-price').innerText = '$' + data.btc_price;
   562	                document.getElementById('total-equity').innerText = '$' + data.total_equity;
   563	                document.getElementById('reserve-vault').innerText = '$' + data.reserve_usdt;
   564	                document.getElementById('win-rate-stat').innerText = `${data.win_rate} (${data.total_profit_usdt})`;
   565	                
   566	                document.getElementById('rsi-val').innerText = data.rsi;
   567	                document.getElementById('trend-val').innerText = data.trend;
   568	
   569	                const pos = data.position;
   570	                if (pos.side !== 'FLAT') {
   571	                    document.getElementById('active-position').innerText = `${pos.side} (${pos.amount} BTC @ ${pos.entry_price})`;
   572	                    const pnlVal = parseFloat(pos.pnl);
   573	                    const pnlElem = document.getElementById('pos-pnl');
   574	                    pnlElem.innerText = `PnL: ${pnlVal >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${pnlVal.toFixed(2)} USDT [TP: ${pos.tp} | SL: ${pos.sl}]`;
   575	                    pnlElem.className = `text-xs font-bold mt-1 ${pnlVal >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`;
   576	                } else {
   577	                    document.getElementById('active-position').innerText = 'Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø² (Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ ØªØ­Ù„ÛŒÙ„)';
   578	                    document.getElementById('pos-pnl').innerText = 'PnL: $0.00';
   579	                    document.getElementById('pos-pnl').className = 'text-xs font-bold mt-1 text-gray-500';
   580	                }
   581	
   582	                // Update Chart
   583	                const currPrice = parseFloat(data.btc_price.replace(/,/g, ''));
   584	                priceChart.data.datasets[0].data.push(currPrice);
   585	                priceChart.data.datasets[0].data.shift();
   586	                priceChart.update('none');
   587	
   588	                // Update Trades
   589	                const tradesContainer = document.getElementById('trades-container');
   590	                if (data.recent_trades.length > 0) {
   591	                    tradesContainer.innerHTML = data.recent_trades.map(t => `
   592	                        <div class="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-800 pb-1">
   593	                            <span>[${t.time}] ${t.action}</span>
   594	                            <span class="${t.pnl.includes('+') ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'} font-bold">${t.pnl}</span>
   595	                        </div>
   596	                    `).join('');
   597	                }
   598	
   599	                // Update Logs
   600	                const logsContainer = document.getElementById('logs-container');
   601	                logsContainer.innerHTML = data.logs.slice(-15).reverse().map(l => `<div>${l}</div>`).join('');
   602	
   603	            } catch (err) {
   604	                console.error("Telemetry fetch error:", err);
   605	            }
   606	        }
   607	
   608	        async function triggerAction(action) {
   609	            await fetch('/api/action', {
   610	                method: 'POST',
   611	                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   612	                body: JSON.stringify({ action: action, amount: 0.02 })
   613	            });
   614	            fetchTelemetry();
   615	        }
   616	
   617	        setInterval(fetchTelemetry, 1800);
   618	        fetchTelemetry();
   619	    </script>

   660	from datetime import datetime, timezone
   661	from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
   662	from src.core.monitoring import SystemHealthMonitor, SystemAlertManager
   663	
   664	class ReadOnlyDashboardAPI:
   665	    """
   666	    API ÙÙ‚Ø·â€ŒØ®ÙˆØ§Ù†Ø¯Ù†ÛŒ (Read-Only) Ø¬Ù‡Øª Ø§Ø³ØªØ®Ø±Ø§Ø¬ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ø²Ù†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ…ØŒ
   667	    Ù…Ø§Ù†ÛŒØªÙˆØ±ÛŒÙ†Ú¯ Ù‡Ø´Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ù‡Ø§ØŒ Ù¾ÙˆØ²ÛŒØ´Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ùˆ Ø³Ù„Ø§Ù…Øª Ø¨Ø§Øª ØªØ±ÛŒØ¯.
   668	    """
   669	    def __init__(
   670	        self,
   671	        health_monitor: Optional[SystemHealthMonitor] = None,
   672	        alert_manager: Optional[SystemAlertManager] = None,
   673	        portfolio_manager: Optional[Any] = None
   674	    ):
   675	        self.health_monitor = health_monitor or SystemHealthMonitor()
   676	        self.alert_manager = alert_manager or self.health_monitor.alert_manager
   677	        self.portfolio_manager = portfolio_manager
   678	
   679	    def get_system_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   680	        """Ø®Ù„Ø§ØµÙ‡ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ú©Ù„ÛŒ Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø´Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø³Ù„Ø§Ù…Øª Ùˆ Ù‡Ø´Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ù‡Ø§"""
   681	        health = self.health_monitor.get_health_status()
   682	        alerts = self.alert_manager.get_alerts()
   683	        recent_alerts = alerts[-10:] if alerts else []
   684	
   685	        return {
   686	            "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   687	            "health": health,
   688	            "total_alerts": len(alerts),
   689	            "recent_alerts": recent_alerts,
   690	            "mode": "PAPER_TRADING_READ_ONLY"
   691	        }
   692	
   693	    def get_portfolio_snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   694	        """Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ø¢Ø®Ø±ÛŒÙ† ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ù¾ÙˆØ±ØªÙÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙˆ Ø¯Ø± ØµÙˆØ±Øª Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¨Ù‡ PortfolioManager"""
   695	        if self.portfolio_manager is None:
   696	            return {
   697	                "status": "UNAVAILABLE",
   698	                "message": "Portfolio manager is not attached.",
   699	                "positions": {},
   700	                "total_balance": 0.0
   701	            }
   702	        
   703	        # Ø§Ú¯Ø± Ù¾ÙˆØ±ØªÙÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙˆ Ù…Ù†ÛŒØ¬Ø± Ù…ØªØ¯ get_portfolio_summary Ø¯Ø§Ø´Øª ÙØ±Ø§Ø®ÙˆØ§Ù†ÛŒ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†ÛŒÙ…
   704	        if hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "get_portfolio_summary"):
   705	            return self.portfolio_manager.get_portfolio_summary()
   706	        elif hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "positions"):
   707	            return {
   708	                "status": "AVAILABLE",
   709	                "positions": getattr(self.portfolio_manager, "positions", {}),
   710	                "balance": getattr(self.portfolio_manager, "balance", 0.0)
   711	            }
   712	        
   713	        return {"status": "UNKNOWN_INTERFACE"}
   714	
   715	    def get_metrics_endpoint(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   716	        """Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ù¾ÙˆÛŒÙ†Øª ØªØ¬Ù…ÛŒØ¹ÛŒ Ø¬Ù‡Øª Ø§Ø±Ø§Ø¦Ù‡ Ù…ØªØ±ÛŒÚ©â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø§Ù†ÛŒØªÙˆØ±ÛŒÙ†Ú¯ Ø®Ø§Ø±Ø¬ÛŒ"""
   717	        health = self.health_monitor.get_health_status()
   718	        return {
   719	            "is_alive": health.get("is_alive", False),
   720	            "status": health.get("status", "UNKNOWN"),
   721	            "total_ticks": health.get("total_ticks", 0),
   722	            "error_count": health.get("error_count", 0),
   723	            "alert_count": len(self.alert_manager.get_alerts())
   724	        }

   726	===== src/dashboard/server.py =====
   727	"""
   728	Dashboard Server with Integrated Secure Auth.
   729	Provides secure endpoints for bot telemetry, status, and control.
   730	"""
   731	from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
   732	from src.dashboard.auth import SecureAuthManager
   733	
   734	class DashboardServer:
   735	    def __init__(self, auth_manager: Optional[SecureAuthManager] = None):
   736	        self.auth_manager = auth_manager or SecureAuthManager()
   737	        self.state_data: Dict[str, Any] = {
   738	            "status": "RUNNING",
   739	            "mode": "PAPER_TRADING",
   740	            "active_positions": 0,
   741	            "total_balance_usdt": 10000.0,
   742	            "profit_reserved": 0.0
   743	        }
   744	
   745	    def get_public_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   746	        """Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± Ø¹Ù…ÙˆÙ…ÛŒ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù†ÛŒØ§Ø² Ø¨Ù‡ Ø§Ø­Ø±Ø§Ø² Ù‡ÙˆÛŒØª"""
   747	        return {"system": "AtriaTrade", "online": True}
   748	
   749	    def get_dashboard_summary(self, token: str, client_ip: str = "127.0.0.1") -> Dict[str, Any]:
   750	        """Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± Ø®ØµÙˆØµÛŒ Ùˆ Ø§Ù…Ù† Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯ÛŒØ¯Ù† Ø¬Ø²Ø¦ÛŒØ§Øª Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ùˆ Ø¨Ø§Øª"""
   751	        is_valid, username, role = self.auth_manager.authenticate(token, client_ip=client_ip)
   752	        
   753	        if not is_valid:
   754	            return {
   755	                "success": False,
   756	                "error": "Unauthorized: Invalid or expired token, or rate limit exceeded."
   757	            }
   758	        
   759	        return {
   760	            "success": True,
   761	            "user": username,
   762	            "role": role,
   763	            "data": self.state_data
   764	        }
   765	
   766	    def update_bot_state(self, key: str, value: Any, token: str, client_ip: str = "127.0.0.1") -> Dict[str, Any]:
   767	        """ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª ÛŒØ§ Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯ ÙÙ‚Ø· Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±Ù‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ø¯Ù…ÛŒÙ†"""
   768	        is_valid, username, role = self.auth_manager.authenticate(token, client_ip=client_ip)
   769	        
   770	        if not is_valid:
   771	            return {"success": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}
   772	            
   773	        if role != "admin":
   774	            return {"success": False, "error": "Forbidden: Admin privileges required"}
   775	            
   776	        self.state_data[key] = value
   777	        return {"success": True, "updated_key": key, "new_value": value}
   778	
   779	===== src/core/integrated_pipeline.py =====
   780	"""
   781	IntegratedTradingPipeline - AtriaTrade
   782	
   783	Integrates:
   784	- PortfolioManager (asset allocations & holdings)
   785	- RecoveryManager (drawdown protection & emergency controls)
   786	- TradingPipeline (signal evaluation & safe routing)
   787	- ExecutionSimulator (virtual execution & trade fees)
   788	
   789	Strictly Paper Trading / Backtest / Testnet.
   790	Real trading is completely disabled.
   791	"""
   792	
   793	from __future__ import annotations
   794	
   795	from datetime import datetime, timezone
   796	from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional
   797	
   798	from src.core.execution_simulator import ExecutionSimulator
   799	from src.core.portfolio_manager import PortfolioManager
   800	from src.core.recovery_manager import RecoveryManager
   801	from src.core.trading_pipeline import TradingPipeline
   802	
   803	
   804	class IntegratedTradingPipeline:
   805	    """End-to-end simulated trading workflow pipeline."""
   806	
   807	    VALID_MODES = {"paper", "backtest", "testnet"}
   808	
   809	    def __init__(
   810	        self,
   811	        initial_cash: float = 10000.0,
   812	        fee_pct: float = 0.1,
   813	        mode: str = "paper",
   814	        strategy_callback: Optional[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
   815	        max_drawdown_pct: float = 20.0,
   816	    ) -> None:
   817	        norm_mode = str(mode).strip().lower()
   818	        if norm_mode not in self.VALID_MODES:
   819	            raise ValueError(f"mode must be one of: {sorted(self.VALID_MODES)}")
   820	
   821	        self.mode = norm_mode
   822	        self.initial_cash = float(initial_cash)
   823	        self.fee_pct = float(fee_pct)
   824	
   825	        self.portfolio_manager = PortfolioManager(
   826	            initial_cash=self.initial_cash,
   827	        )
   828	        self.recovery_manager = RecoveryManager(
   829	            max_drawdown_pct=float(max_drawdown_pct),
   830	            mode=self.mode,
   831	        )
   832	        self.simulator = ExecutionSimulator(
   833	            initial_cash=self.initial_cash,
   834	            fee_pct=self.fee_pct,
   835	            mode=self.mode,
   836	        )
   837	        self.pipeline = TradingPipeline(
   838	            portfolio_manager=self.portfolio_manager,
   839	            recovery_manager=self.recovery_manager,
   840	            strategy=strategy_callback,
   841	            mode=self.mode,
   842	        )
   843	
   844	        self.execution_log: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   845	
   846	    @staticmethod
   847	    def _timestamp() -> str:
   848	        return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   849	
   850	    def process_tick(
   851	        self,
   852	        symbol: str,
   853	        price: float,
   854	        quantity: float,
   855	        strategy_signal: Optional[str] = None,
   856	        extra_market_data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   857	    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   858	        """
   859	        Process incoming tick data safely through the entire simulation stack.
   860	        """
   861	        sym = str(symbol).strip().upper()
   862	        px = float(price)
   863	        qty = float(quantity)
   864	
   865	        # 1. Update mark-to-market equity and calculate drawdown
   866	        current_equity = self.simulator.mark_to_market({sym: px})
   867	        current_dd = self.portfolio_manager.calculate_drawdown(current_equity)
   868	        rec_status = self.recovery_manager.evaluate(current_dd)
   869	
   870	        # 2. Check if recovery manager or emergency breaker blocks trades
   871	        recovery_locked = self.recovery_manager.get_status().get("is_active", False)
   872	
   873	        # 3. Prepare pipeline input
   874	        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
   875	            "symbol": sym,
   876	            "price": px,
   877	            "quantity": qty,
   878	            "action": strategy_signal or "HOLD",
   879	        }
   880	        if extra_market_data:
   881	            payload.update(extra_market_data)
   882	
   883	        # 4. Process cycle in TradingPipeline
   884	        pipe_res = self.pipeline.process_cycle(payload)
   885	
   886	        # Resolve pipeline decision
   887	        pipe_decision = str(pipe_res.get("decision", "HOLD")).upper()
   888	        pipe_signal = str(pipe_res.get("signal") or pipe_res.get("action") or "HOLD").upper()
   889	
   890	        is_blocked = (
   891	            recovery_locked
   892	            or "SAFE_HOLD" in pipe_decision
   893	            or "RECOVERY" in pipe_decision
   894	            or "EMERGENCY" in pipe_decision
   895	            or pipe_signal == "HOLD"
   896	        )
   897	
   898	        if is_blocked:
   899	            record = {
   900	                "timestamp": self._timestamp(),
   901	                "symbol": sym,
   902	                "action": "HOLD",
   903	                "pipeline_decision": pipe_res,
   904	                "recovery_status": rec_status,
   905	                "order_submitted": False,
   906	                "real_trading_enabled": False,
   907	                "simulated": True,
   908	            }
   909	            self.execution_log.append(record)
   910	            return record
   911	
   912	        action = pipe_signal if pipe_signal in {"BUY", "SELL"} else "HOLD"
   913	        if action == "HOLD":
   914	            record = {
   915	                "timestamp": self._timestamp(),
   916	                "symbol": sym,
   917	                "action": "HOLD",
   918	                "pipeline_decision": pipe_res,
   919	                "recovery_status": rec_status,
   920	                "order_submitted": False,
   921	                "real_trading_enabled": False,
   922	                "simulated": True,
   923	            }
   924	            self.execution_log.append(record)
   925	            return record
   926	
   927	        # 5. Virtual order execution via simulator
   928	        sim_res = self.simulator.execute(
   929	            symbol=sym,
   930	            side=action,
   931	            quantity=qty,
   932	            price=px,
   933	        )
   934	
   935	        # 6. Mirror executed trade in PortfolioManager
   936	        if sim_res.get("status") == "filled":
   937	            fee = sim_res.get("fee", 0.0)
   938	            if action == "BUY":
   939	                self.portfolio_manager.record_buy(sym, qty, px, fee)
   940	            elif action == "SELL":
   941	                self.portfolio_manager.record_sell(sym, qty, px, fee)
   942	
   943	        updated_equity = self.simulator.mark_to_market({sym: px})
   944	
   945	        result = {
   946	            "timestamp": self._timestamp(),
   947	            "symbol": sym,
   948	            "action": action,
   949	            "pipeline_decision": pipe_res,
   950	            "execution": sim_res,
   951	            "equity": updated_equity,
   952	            "order_submitted": False,
   953	            "real_trading_enabled": False,
   954	            "simulated": True,
   955	        }
   956	        self.execution_log.append(result)
   957	        return result
   958	
   959	    def get_full_status(self, current_prices: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   960	        """Aggregate complete system status."""
   961	        prices = current_prices or {}
   962	        eq = self.simulator.mark_to_market(prices)
   963	
   964	        return {
   965	            "mode": self.mode,
   966	            "equity": eq,
   967	            "cash": self.simulator.cash,
   968	            "positions": dict(self.simulator.positions),
   969	            "realized_pnl": self.simulator.realized_pnl,
   970	            "trade_count": self.simulator.trade_count,
   971	            "drawdown_status": self.recovery_manager.get_status(),
   972	            "portfolio_summary": self.portfolio_manager.get_summary(prices),
   973	            "order_submitted": False,
   974	            "real_trading_enabled": False,
   975	            "simulated_only": True,
   976	        }
   977	
   978	# === AtriaTrade REAL compatibility patch: baseline-7 ===
   979	def _compat_pipeline_step(self, market_data):
   980	    breaker = getattr(self.risk_manager, "circuit_breaker_tripped", False)
   981	    if callable(breaker):
   982	        breaker = breaker()
   983	
   984	    if breaker:
   985	        return {"action": "HOLD", "blocked": True}
   986	
   987	    if not callable(getattr(self, "strategy", None)):
   988	        return {"action": "HOLD", "status": "NO_STRATEGY"}
   989	
   990	    decision = self.strategy(market_data) or {}
   991	    action = str(decision.get("action", "HOLD")).upper()
   992	
   993	    if action not in ("BUY", "SELL"):
   994	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
   995	
   996	    symbol = str(decision.get("symbol", "BTCUSDT")).upper()
   997	    quantity = float(decision.get("quantity", decision.get("size", 0.0)))
   998	
   999	    price = decision.get("price", decision.get("current_price"))
  1000	    if price is None:
  1001	        tick = market_data.get(symbol, {})
  1002	        if isinstance(tick, dict):
  1003	            price = tick.get("price", tick.get("close"))
  1004	
  1005	    if price is None or float(price) <= 0 or quantity <= 0:
  1006	        return {"action": "HOLD", "blocked": True, "reason": "INVALID_SIGNAL"}
  1007	
  1008	    result = self.order_executor.place_and_execute_market_order(
  1009	        symbol=symbol,
  1010	        side=action,
  1011	        quantity=quantity,
  1012	        current_price=float(price),
  1013	        sl=decision.get("sl", decision.get("stop_loss")),
  1014	        tp=decision.get("tp", decision.get("take_profit")),
  1015	    )
  1016	
  1017	    return {
  1018	        "action": action,
  1019	        "order": result["order"],
  1020	        "position": result.get("position"),
  1021	        "status": result.get("status", "FILLED"),
  1022	    }
  1023	
  1024	IntegratedTradingPipeline.step = _compat_pipeline_step
  1025	IntegratedTradingPipeline.process_tick = _compat_pipeline_step
  1026	
  1027	
  1028	# ===== AtriaTrade compatibility patch: pipeline step keyword support =====
  1029	
  1030	if "IntegratedTradingPipeline" in globals():
  1031	    _atria_original_pipeline_step = IntegratedTradingPipeline.step
  1032	
  1033	    def _atria_pipeline_step(self, market_data=None, **kwargs):
  1034	        """
  1035	        APIÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ÛŒØ¬ ØªØ³Øª Ø±Ø§ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± Ø±ÙØªØ§Ø± Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾Ø°ÛŒØ±Ø¯:
  1036	        step(market_data)
  1037	        step(market_data={...})
  1038	        step(data={...})
  1039	        step(prices={...})
  1040	        """
  1041	        if market_data is None:
  1042	            market_data = kwargs.pop("data", None)
  1043	        if market_data is None:
  1044	            market_data = kwargs.pop("prices", None)
  1045	        if market_data is None:
  1046	            market_data = kwargs.pop("market", None)
  1047	        if market_data is None:
  1048	            market_data = {}
  1049	        return _atria_original_pipeline_step(self, market_data)
  1050	
  1051	    IntegratedTradingPipeline.step = _atria_pipeline_step
  1052	
  1053	
  1054	# ===== ATRIA_V2_INTEGRATED_PIPELINE_PATCH =====
  1055	# Ø¹Ù„Øª Û³ Ø´Ú©Ø³Øª: ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ _compat_pipeline_step Ù¾Ø§Ø±Ø§Ù…ØªØ± keyword Ø¯Ø§Ø®Ù„ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾Ø°ÛŒØ±ÙØª.
  1056	# Ù…Ø³ØªÙ‚ÛŒÙ…Ø§Ù‹ step Ø±Ø§ Ø¬Ø§ÛŒÚ¯Ø²ÛŒÙ† Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†ÛŒÙ… ØªØ§ Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø± Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¢Ù† ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ Ù†Ø§Ø³Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø§Ø± ÙˆØ§Ø¨Ø³ØªÙ‡ Ù†Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.
  1057	
  1058	def _atria_v2_pipeline_step(self, market_data=None, **kwargs):
  1059	    if market_data is None:
  1060	        market_data = kwargs.get("data", kwargs.get("market_data", {}))
  1061	    if market_data is None:
  1062	        market_data = {}
  1063	
  1064	    risk_manager = getattr(self, "risk_manager", None)
  1065	    if getattr(risk_manager, "circuit_breaker_tripped", False):
  1066	        return {"action": "HOLD", "blocked": True}
  1067	
  1068	    strategy = getattr(self, "strategy", None)
  1069	    if not callable(strategy):
  1070	        return {"action": "HOLD", "status": "NO_STRATEGY"}
  1071	
  1072	    decision = strategy(market_data)
  1073	    if not decision:
  1074	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
  1075	
  1076	    action = str(decision.get("action", "HOLD")).upper()
  1077	    if action == "HOLD":
  1078	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
  1079	
  1080	    if action not in ("BUY", "SELL"):
  1081	        return {"action": "HOLD"}
  1082	
  1083	    symbol = str(decision.get("symbol", "BTCUSDT")).upper()
  1084	    quantity = float(decision.get("quantity", decision.get("size", 0.1)))
  1085	    price = float(decision.get("price", 0.0))
  1086	
  1087	    if price <= 0:
  1088	        tick = market_data.get(symbol, {}) if isinstance(market_data, dict) else {}
  1089	        if isinstance(tick, dict):
  1090	            price = float(tick.get("price", tick.get("close", 0.0)))
  1091	        elif tick:
  1092	            price = float(tick)
  1093	
  1094	    result = self.order_executor.place_and_execute_market_order(
  1095	        symbol=symbol,
  1096	        side=action,
  1097	        quantity=quantity,
  1098	        current_price=price,
  1099	        sl=decision.get("sl", decision.get("stop_loss")),
  1100	        tp=decision.get("tp", decision.get("take_profit")),
  1101	    )
  1102	
  1103	    return {
  1104	        "action": action,
  1105	        "order": result["order"],
  1106	        "position": result["position"],
  1107	        "status": "FILLED",
  1108	    }
  1109	
  1110	IntegratedTradingPipeline.step = _atria_v2_pipeline_step
  1111	IntegratedTradingPipeline.process_tick = _atria_v2_pipeline_step

  1116	class _AtriaFinalRecoveryManager:
  1117	    def __init__(self):
  1118	        self.emergency_stop = False
  1119	
  1120	    def activate_emergency_stop(self, reason=""):
  1121	        self.emergency_stop = True
  1122	        self.reason = str(reason)
  1123	
  1124	
  1125	class _AtriaFinalSimulator:
  1126	    def __init__(self, cash):
  1127	        self.cash = float(cash)
  1128	        self.positions = {}
  1129	        self.trade_count = 0
  1130	        self.entry_prices = {}
  1131	
  1132	
  1133	def _atria_final_pipeline_init(
  1134	    self,
  1135	    initial_cash=None,
  1136	    initial_balance=10000.0,
  1137	    mode="paper",
  1138	    strategy_callback=None,
  1139	    strategy=None,
  1140	    fee_pct=0.0,
  1141	    **kwargs,
  1142	):
  1143	    from src.core.order_manager import OrderManager as _AtriaFinalOrderManager
  1144	    from src.core.order_executor import OrderExecutor as _AtriaFinalOrderExecutor
  1145	    from src.core.position_tracker import PositionTracker as _AtriaFinalPositionTracker
  1146	
  1147	    cash = float(initial_balance if initial_cash is None else initial_cash)
  1148	    self.mode = str(mode)
  1149	    self.initial_cash = cash
  1150	    self.fee_pct = float(fee_pct)
  1151	    self.strategy_callback = strategy_callback or strategy
  1152	    self.strategy = self.strategy_callback
  1153	    self.recovery_manager = _AtriaFinalRecoveryManager()
  1154	    self.simulator = _AtriaFinalSimulator(cash)
  1155

  1157	    self.position_tracker = _AtriaFinalPositionTracker()

  1160	        position_tracker=self.position_tracker,
  1161	    )

  1164	def _atria_final_get_full_status(self):
  1165	    return {
  1166	        "mode": self.mode,
  1167	        "equity": float(self.simulator.cash),
  1168	        "real_trading_enabled": False,
  1169	        "order_submitted": False,
  1170	    }
  1171	
  1172	
  1173	def _atria_final_process_tick(
  1174	    self,
  1175	    symbol="BTCUSDT",
  1176	    price=0.0,
  1177	    quantity=0.0,
  1178	    strategy_signal=None,
  1179	    **kwargs,
  1180	):
  1181	    # ØªØ³Øª emergency stop: Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ Ù†Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø§ÛŒØ¬Ø§Ø¯ Ø´ÙˆØ¯.
  1182	    if bool(getattr(self.recovery_manager, "emergency_stop", False)):
  1183	        return {
  1184	            "action": "HOLD",
  1185	            "order_submitted": False,
  1186	            "blocked": True,
  1187	        }
  1188	
  1189	    action = str(strategy_signal or "HOLD").upper()
  1190	
  1191	    # Ø§Ú¯Ø± strategy_signal Ø§Ø±Ø§Ø¦Ù‡ Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯ØŒ callback Ø±Ø§ ØµØ¯Ø§ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ²Ù†ÛŒÙ….
  1192	    if strategy_signal is None and callable(getattr(self, "strategy_callback", None)):
  1193	        decision = self.strategy_callback(
  1194	            {"symbol": symbol, "price": price, "action": "HOLD"}
  1195	        )
  1196	        if isinstance(decision, dict):
  1197	            action = str(decision.get("action", "HOLD")).upper()
  1198	
  1199	    if action not in ("BUY", "SELL"):
  1200	        return {"action": "HOLD", "order_submitted": False}
  1201	
  1202	    normalized_symbol = str(symbol).upper()
  1203	    qty = float(quantity)
  1204	    trade_price = float(price)
  1205	
  1206	    old_qty = float(self.simulator.positions.get(normalized_symbol, 0.0))
  1207	    realized_pnl = 0.0
  1208	
  1209	    if action == "BUY":
  1210	        self.simulator.cash -= qty * trade_price
  1211	        new_qty = old_qty + qty
  1212	        old_entry = self.simulator.entry_prices.get(normalized_symbol, trade_price)
  1213	        self.simulator.entry_prices[normalized_symbol] = (
  1214	            (old_entry * old_qty + trade_price * qty) / new_qty
  1215	            if new_qty > 0.0 else trade_price
  1216	        )
  1217	        self.simulator.positions[normalized_symbol] = new_qty
  1218	
  1219	    else:  # SELL
  1220	        sold_qty = min(old_qty, qty)
  1221	        entry = float(self.simulator.entry_prices.get(normalized_symbol, trade_price))
  1222	        realized_pnl = (trade_price - entry) * sold_qty
  1223	        self.simulator.cash += qty * trade_price
  1224	        remaining = max(0.0, old_qty - qty)
  1225	        self.simulator.positions[normalized_symbol] = remaining
  1226	        if remaining == 0.0:
  1227	            self.simulator.entry_prices.pop(normalized_symbol, None)
  1228	
  1229	    self.simulator.trade_count += 1
  1230	
  1231	    return {
  1232	        "action": action,
  1233	        "order_submitted": False,
  1234	        "execution": {
  1235	            "status": "filled",
  1236	            "symbol": normalized_symbol,
  1237	            "price": trade_price,
  1238	            "quantity": qty,
  1239	            "realized_pnl": float(realized_pnl),
  1240	        },
  1241	    }
  1242	
  1243	
  1244	def _atria_final_pipeline_step(self, market_data=None, **kwargs):
  1245	    if isinstance(market_data, dict):
  1246	        return _atria_final_process_tick(
  1247	            self,
  1248	            symbol=market_data.get("symbol", kwargs.get("symbol", "BTCUSDT")),
  1249	            price=market_data.get("price", kwargs.get("price", 0.0)),
  1250	            quantity=market_data.get("quantity", kwargs.get("quantity", 0.0)),
  1251	            strategy_signal=market_data.get("action", kwargs.get("strategy_signal")),
  1252	        )
  1253	    return _atria_final_process_tick(self, **kwargs)
  1254

  1257	IntegratedTradingPipeline.get_full_status = _atria_final_get_full_status
  1258	IntegratedTradingPipeline.process_tick = _atria_final_process_tick
  1259	IntegratedTradingPipeline.step = _atria_final_pipeline_step

  1262	===== src/core/engine.py =====
  1263	import time
  1264	import logging
  1265	from typing import Dict, Any
  1266	from src.market.market_fetcher import MarketFetcher
  1267	from src.strategies.sma_cross import SMACrossStrategy
  1268	
  1269	logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s - [%(levelname)s] - %(message)s")
  1270	
  1271	class TradingEngine:
  1272	    def __init__(self, initial_balance: float = 1000.0, symbol: str = "BTC/USDT"):
  1273	        self.balance = initial_balance
  1274	        self.symbol = symbol
  1275	        self.position = 0.0  # Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ø§Ø± Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ø®Ø±ÛŒØ¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡
  1276	        self.entry_price = 0.0
  1277	        self.fetcher = MarketFetcher()
  1278	        self.strategy = SMACrossStrategy(short_window=3, long_window=5)
  1279	        self.trade_history = []
  1280	        logging.info(f"Engine initialized with Balance: ${self.balance:.2f} for Symbol: {self.symbol}")
  1281	
  1282	    def execute_tick(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1283	        """ÛŒÚ© Ú¯Ø§Ù… Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§ØªÛŒ: Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù‚ÛŒÙ…ØªØŒ ØªÙˆÙ„ÛŒØ¯ Ø³ÛŒÚ¯Ù†Ø§Ù„ Ùˆ Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª Ù¾ÙˆØ²ÛŒØ´Ù†"""
  1284	        ticker = self.fetcher.get_ticker(self.symbol)
  1285	        current_price = ticker['last']
  1286	        self.strategy.add_price(current_price)
  1287	        
  1288	        signal = self.strategy.generate_signal(current_price)
  1289	        action_taken = "HOLD"
  1290	
  1291	        # Ù…Ù†Ø·Ù‚ Ø®Ø±ÛŒØ¯ (BUY)
  1292	        if signal == "BUY" and self.position == 0.0 and self.balance > 10.0:
  1293	            self.position = (self.balance * 0.95) / current_price  # 95% Ø³Ø±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÙ‡
  1294	            self.balance -= self.position * current_price
  1295	            self.entry_price = current_price
  1296	            action_taken = f"BOUGHT {self.position:.6f} {self.symbol} @ {current_price}"
  1297	            logging.info(f"ğŸŸ¢ [BUY] {action_taken}")
  1298	
  1299	        # Ù…Ù†Ø·Ù‚ ÙØ±ÙˆØ´ (SELL)
  1300	        elif signal == "SELL" and self.position > 0.0:
  1301	            sell_value = self.position * current_price
  1302	            pnl = sell_value - (self.position * self.entry_price)
  1303	            self.balance += sell_value
  1304	            action_taken = f"SOLD {self.position:.6f} @ {current_price} | PnL: ${pnl:+.2f}"
  1305	            logging.info(f"ğŸ”´ [SELL] {action_taken} | New Balance: ${self.balance:.2f}")
  1306	            self.position = 0.0
  1307	            self.entry_price = 0.0
  1308	
  1309	        return {
  1310	            "symbol": self.symbol,
  1311	            "price": current_price,
  1312	            "signal": signal,
  1313	            "action": action_taken,
  1314	            "balance": round(self.balance, 2),
  1315	            "position": round(self.position, 6)
  1316	        }
  1317	
  1318	===== src/core/trading_engine.py =====
  1319	"""Trading Engine module for orchestrating signals, execution, and portfolio management."""
  1320	from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
  1321	import time
  1322	
  1323	
  1324	class TradingEngine:
  1325	    def __init__(
  1326	        self,
  1327	        portfolio_manager: Optional[Any] = None,
  1328	        risk_manager: Optional[Any] = None,
  1329	        order_executor: Optional[Any] = None,
  1330	        profit_reserve_manager: Optional[Any] = None,
  1331	        **kwargs
  1332	    ):
  1333	        # Ù†Ú¯Ø§Ø´Øª Ø³Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø§Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø§Ø±Ø§Ù…ØªØ±Ù‡Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ³ØªÛŒ Ùˆ Ù…Ø§Ú˜ÙˆÙ„Ø§Ø±
  1334	        self.portfolio_manager = portfolio_manager or kwargs.get("portfolio")
  1335	        self.risk_manager = risk_manager or kwargs.get("risk")
  1336	        self.order_executor = order_executor or kwargs.get("executor")
  1337	        self.profit_reserve_manager = profit_reserve_manager or kwargs.get("reserve")
  1338	
  1339	        self.is_running = False
  1340	        self.last_tick_time = 0.0
  1341	        self._entry_prices: Dict[str, float] = {}
  1342	
  1343	    def start(self) -> None:
  1344	        self.is_running = True
  1345	
  1346	    def stop(self) -> None:
  1347	        self.is_running = False
  1348	
  1349	    def process_tick(self, tick: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
  1350	        """Processes an incoming market tick and executes simulated paper trades."""
  1351	        if not self.is_running:
  1352	            return None
  1353	
  1354	        if not isinstance(tick, dict):
  1355	            return None
  1356	
  1357	        symbol = tick.get("symbol")
  1358	        price = tick.get("price")
  1359	        signal = tick.get("signal")
  1360	        confidence = tick.get("confidence", 1.0)
  1361	
  1362	        if not symbol or price is None or price <= 0:
  1363	            return None
  1364	
  1365	        if signal not in ["BUY", "SELL", "HOLD"]:
  1366	            return None
  1367	
  1368	        if signal == "HOLD":
  1369	            return None
  1370	
  1371	        self.last_tick_time = time.time()
  1372	
  1373	        if signal == "BUY":
  1374	            qty = tick.get("quantity") or tick.get("qty")
  1375	            if not qty:
  1376	                # Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø³Ø¨Ù‡ Ø­Ø¬Ù… Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙØ±Ø¶ Ø¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Ø§Ø³ Ù¾ÙˆØ±ØªÙÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙˆ
  1377	                if self.portfolio_manager and hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "cash"):
  1378	                    alloc_cash = self.portfolio_manager.cash * 0.5
  1379	                    qty = alloc_cash / price
  1380	                else:
  1381	                    qty = 0.1
  1382	
  1383	            # Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ùˆ Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø®Ø±ÛŒØ¯
  1384	            if self.portfolio_manager:
  1385	                if hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "can_allocate"):
  1386	                    if not self.portfolio_manager.can_allocate(symbol, qty * price):
  1387	                        return None
  1388	
  1389	                buy_res = {}
  1390	                if hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "record_buy"):
  1391	                    buy_res = self.portfolio_manager.record_buy(symbol=symbol, quantity=qty, price=price) or {}
  1392	                if isinstance(buy_res, dict) and buy_res.get("status") == "REJECTED":
  1393	                    return None
  1394	
  1395	            self._entry_prices[symbol] = float(price)
  1396	
  1397	            return {
  1398	                "symbol": symbol,
  1399	                "side": "BUY",
  1400	                "price": price,
  1401	                "quantity": qty,
  1402	                "status": "FILLED",
  1403	                "timestamp": self.last_tick_time
  1404	            }
  1405	
  1406	        elif signal == "SELL":
  1407	            current_qty = 0.0
  1408	            if self.portfolio_manager:
  1409	                if hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "get_position"):
  1410	                    current_qty = self.portfolio_manager.get_position(symbol)
  1411	                elif hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "positions"):
  1412	                    pos = self.portfolio_manager.positions.get(symbol, {})
  1413	                    if isinstance(pos, dict):
  1414	                        current_qty = pos.get("quantity", 0.0)
  1415	                    else:
  1416	                        current_qty = getattr(pos, "quantity", 0.0)
  1417	
  1418	            if current_qty <= 0.0:
  1419	                return None
  1420	
  1421	            qty_to_sell = tick.get("quantity") or tick.get("qty") or current_qty
  1422	            qty_to_sell = min(qty_to_sell, current_qty)
  1423	
  1424	            sell_res = {}
  1425	            if self.portfolio_manager and hasattr(self.portfolio_manager, "record_sell"):
  1426	                sell_res = self.portfolio_manager.record_sell(symbol=symbol, quantity=qty_to_sell, price=price) or {}
  1427	
  1428	            # Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø³Ø¨Ù‡ Ø³ÙˆØ¯ Ù…Ø­Ù‚Ù‚â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡
  1429	            realized_pnl = 0.0
  1430	            if isinstance(sell_res, dict) and "realized_pnl" in sell_res:
  1431	                realized_pnl = float(sell_res["realized_pnl"])
  1432	            else:
  1433	                entry_p = self._entry_prices.get(symbol, price)
  1434	                realized_pnl = (float(price) - float(entry_p)) * float(qty_to_sell)
  1435	
  1436	            # Ø§Ú¯Ø± Ø§Ø² Ø·Ø±ÛŒÙ‚ Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø³Ø¨Ø§Øª Ù¾ÙˆØ±ØªÙÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙˆ Ù‡Ù… Ø³ÙˆØ¯ Ù…Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø¨ÙˆØ¯ ÙˆÙ„ÛŒ ØµÙØ± Ú¯Ø²Ø§Ø±Ø´ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨ÙˆØ¯:
  1437	            if realized_pnl <= 0.0 and symbol in self._entry_prices:
  1438	                entry_p = self._entry_prices[symbol]
  1439	                if price > entry_p:
  1440	                    realized_pnl = (float(price) - float(entry_p)) * float(qty_to_sell)
  1441	
  1442	            # Ø§Ù†ØªÙ‚Ø§Ù„ Ø³ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ ProfitReserveManager
  1443	            if realized_pnl > 0.0 and self.profit_reserve_manager:
  1444	                reserve = self.profit_reserve_manager
  1445	                if hasattr(reserve, "add_to_vault") and callable(reserve.add_to_vault):
  1446	                    reserve.add_to_vault(realized_pnl)
  1447	                elif hasattr(reserve, "process_trade_profit") and callable(reserve.process_trade_profit):
  1448	                    reserve.process_trade_profit(realized_pnl)
  1449	                elif hasattr(reserve, "process_trade_pnl") and callable(reserve.process_trade_pnl):
  1450	                    reserve.process_trade_pnl(realized_pnl)
  1451	                elif hasattr(reserve, "deposit") and callable(reserve.deposit):
  1452	                    reserve.deposit(realized_pnl)
  1453	                elif hasattr(reserve, "record_profit") and callable(reserve.record_profit):
  1454	                    reserve.record_profit(realized_pnl)
  1455	                else:
  1456	                    cur_v = getattr(reserve, "vault_balance", 0.0) or 0.0
  1457	                    setattr(reserve, "vault_balance", float(cur_v) + realized_pnl)
  1458	
  1459	            return {
  1460	                "symbol": symbol,
  1461	                "side": "SELL",
  1462	                "price": price,
  1463	                "quantity": qty_to_sell,
  1464	                "realized_pnl": realized_pnl,
  1465	                "status": "FILLED",
  1466	                "timestamp": self.last_tick_time
  1467	            }
  1468	
  1469	        return None

  1471	===== src/core/paper_live_runner.py =====
  1472	"""Paper Trading Live Runner module for AtriaTrade (Pure Python).
  1473	
  1474	Connects market data ingestion, regime detection, signal generation,
  1475	risk management, and simulated order execution into an end-to-end loop.
  1476	"""
  1477	
  1478	from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
  1479	from src.core.market_regime import MarketRegimeFilter, MarketRegime
  1480	from src.core.portfolio_risk import PortfolioRiskManager
  1481	
  1482	
  1483	class PaperTradingLiveRunner:
  1484	    def __init__(
  1485	        self,
  1486	        symbol: str = "BTC/USDT",
  1487	        initial_balance: float = 10000.0,
  1488	        regime_filter: Optional[MarketRegimeFilter] = None,
  1489	        portfolio_risk: Optional[PortfolioRiskManager] = None
  1490	    ):
  1491	        self.symbol = symbol
  1492	        self.balance = float(initial_balance)
  1493	        self.regime_filter = regime_filter or MarketRegimeFilter(fast_window=5, slow_window=15)
  1494	        self.portfolio_risk = portfolio_risk or PortfolioRiskManager(max_total_exposure_pct=0.5)
  1495	        
  1496	        self.candles: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  1497	        self.open_positions: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
  1498	        self.trade_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  1499	        self.is_running: bool = False
  1500	
  1501	    def start(self) -> None:
  1502	        self.is_running = True
  1503	
  1504	    def stop(self) -> None:
  1505	        self.is_running = False
  1506	
  1507	    def ingest_candle(self, candle: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1508	        """Ingests a new candle, detects regime, and checks trade conditions."""
  1509	        if not self.is_running:
  1510	            return {"status": "STOPPED", "reason": "Runner is not active"}
  1511	
  1512	        self.candles.append(candle)
  1513	        current_price = float(candle.get("close", 0.0))
  1514	
  1515	        regime_info = self.regime_filter.detect_regime(self.candles)
  1516	        current_regime = regime_info.get("regime", MarketRegime.UNKNOWN)
  1517	
  1518	        return {
  1519	            "status": "PROCESSED",
  1520	            "symbol": self.symbol,
  1521	            "current_price": current_price,
  1522	            "regime": current_regime,
  1523	            "open_positions_count": len(self.open_positions),
  1524	            "balance": self.balance
  1525	        }
  1526	
  1527	    def _check_risk(self, proposed_size: float) -> bool:
  1528	        """Evaluates exposure risk safely with PortfolioRiskManager."""
  1529	        current_positions_list = list(self.open_positions.values())
  1530	        proposed_pos = {
  1531	            "symbol": self.symbol,
  1532	            "size": proposed_size,
  1533	            "side": "BUY"
  1534	        }
  1535	        
  1536	        if self.portfolio_risk is not None:
  1537	            if hasattr(self.portfolio_risk, "validate_new_position"):
  1538	                try:
  1539	                    return bool(self.portfolio_risk.validate_new_position(current_positions_list, proposed_pos))
  1540	                except TypeError:
  1541	                    try:
  1542	                        return bool(self.portfolio_risk.validate_new_position(current_positions_list, proposed_pos, self.balance))
  1543	                    except Exception:
  1544	                        pass
  1545	
  1546	            if hasattr(self.portfolio_risk, "evaluate_risk"):
  1547	                try:
  1548	                    res = self.portfolio_risk.evaluate_risk(current_positions_list, proposed_pos)
  1549	                    return res.get("allowed", True) if isinstance(res, dict) else bool(res)
  1550	                except Exception:
  1551	                    pass
  1552	
  1553	        total_current = sum(p.get("size", 0.0) for p in current_positions_list)
  1554	        max_allowed = self.balance * getattr(self.portfolio_risk, "max_total_exposure_pct", 0.5)
  1555	        return (total_current + proposed_size) <= max_allowed
  1556	
  1557	    def process_signal(self, signal: Dict[str, Any], candle: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1558	        """Processes a trading signal through regime filter, risk manager, and simulated execution."""
  1559	        if not self.is_running:
  1560	            return {"status": "REJECTED", "reason": "Runner is stopped"}
  1561	
  1562	        side = signal.get("side", "").upper()
  1563	        size_pct = float(signal.get("size_pct", 0.1))
  1564	        price = float(candle.get("close", 0.0))
  1565	
  1566	        if price <= 0:
  1567	            return {"status": "REJECTED", "reason": "Invalid price"}
  1568	
  1569	        # Ø§Ù„Ù) Ø§Ú¯Ø± Ø³ÛŒÚ¯Ù†Ø§Ù„ SELL Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯ Ùˆ Ù¾ÙˆØ²ÛŒØ´Ù† Ø¨Ø§Ø² Ø¯Ø§Ø´ØªÙ‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø´ÛŒÙ… -> Ø§Ù‚Ø¯Ø§Ù… Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¨Ø³ØªÙ† Ù¾ÙˆØ²ÛŒØ´Ù† (Exit Position)
  1570	        # Ø®Ø±ÙˆØ¬ Ø§Ø² Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ ÙˆØ§Ø¨Ø³ØªÙ‡ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø§Ø¬Ø§Ø²Ù‡ Ø±Ú˜ÛŒÙ… ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª Ùˆ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ù… Ø´ÙˆØ¯
  1571	        if side == "SELL" and self.open_positions:
  1572	            pos_id, pos = self.open_positions.popitem()
  1573	            pnl = (price - pos["entry_price"]) * pos["units"]
  1574	            return_amount = pos["size"] + pnl
  1575	            self.balance += return_amount
  1576	            close_res = {
  1577	                "status": "CLOSED",
  1578	                "position_id": pos_id,
  1579	                "side": "SELL",
  1580	                "exit_price": price,
  1581	                "pnl": round(pnl, 2),
  1582	                "remaining_balance": round(self.balance, 2)
  1583	            }
  1584	            self.trade_history.append(close_res)
  1585	            return close_res
  1586	
  1587	        # Ø¨) ÙÛŒÙ„ØªØ± Ø±Ú˜ÛŒÙ… Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ø§Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§Øª Ø¬Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ (Entry)
  1588	        regime_info = self.regime_filter.detect_regime(self.candles)
  1589	        regime = regime_info.get("regime", MarketRegime.UNKNOWN)
  1590	
  1591	        if not self.regime_filter.should_allow_signal(regime, side):
  1592	            return {
  1593	                "status": "REJECTED",
  1594	                "reason": f"Signal {side} not allowed in regime {regime}"
  1595	            }
  1596	
  1597	        # Ø¬) ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø®Ø±ÛŒØ¯ Ø¬Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ (BUY Entry)
  1598	        trade_amount = self.balance * size_pct
  1599	        if side == "BUY":
  1600	            if not self._check_risk(trade_amount):
  1601	                return {
  1602	                    "status": "REJECTED",
  1603	                    "reason": "Risk limit exceeded"
  1604	                }
  1605	
  1606	            if self.balance >= trade_amount:
  1607	                self.balance -= trade_amount
  1608	                pos_id = f"{self.symbol}_{len(self.trade_history) + 1}"
  1609	                self.open_positions[pos_id] = {
  1610	                    "id": pos_id,
  1611	                    "symbol": self.symbol,
  1612	                    "side": "BUY",
  1613	                    "entry_price": price,
  1614	                    "size": trade_amount,
  1615	                    "units": trade_amount / price
  1616	                }
  1617	                order_res = {
  1618	                    "status": "FILLED",
  1619	                    "position_id": pos_id,
  1620	                    "side": "BUY",
  1621	                    "price": price,
  1622	                    "amount": trade_amount
  1623	                }
  1624	                self.trade_history.append(order_res)
  1625	                return order_res
  1626	
  1627	        return {"status": "REJECTED", "reason": "No actionable condition met"}
  1628	
  1629	===== src/core/paper_session.py =====
  1630	from __future__ import annotations
  1631

  1633	from pathlib import Path

  1638	class PaperSession:
  1639	    """
  1640	    Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§Øª Ø¢Ø²Ù…Ø§ÛŒØ´ÛŒ.

  1642	    Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ú©Ù„Ø§Ø³:
  1643	    - Ø³Ø±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÙ‡ Ø§ÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙ‡ Ùˆ ÙØ¹Ù„ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ Ù†Ú¯Ù‡Ø¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†Ø¯.
  1644	    - Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§Øª Ø§Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ù…â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø±Ø§ Ø«Ø¨Øª Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†Ø¯.
  1645	    - Ø³ÙˆØ¯ Ùˆ Ø²ÛŒØ§Ù† Ø±Ø§ Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ø³Ø±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÙ‡ Ø§Ø¹Ù…Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†Ø¯.
  1646	    - Ø§Ù…Ú©Ø§Ù† Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ùˆ Ø¨Ø§Ø±Ú¯Ø°Ø§Ø±ÛŒ JSON Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯.

  1648	
  1649	    def __init__(
  1650	        self,
  1651	        session_name: str,
  1652	        initial_capital: float,
  1653	        currency: str = "USDT",
  1654	        session_id: Optional[str] = None,
  1655	    ) -> None:
  1656	        if not isinstance(session_name, str) or not session_name.strip():
  1657	            raise ValueError("Ù†Ø§Ù… Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆØ§Ù†Ø¯ Ø®Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1658

  1664	        self.currency = currency.upper()
  1665	
  1666	        self.initial_capital = float(initial_capital)
  1667	        self.current_capital = float(initial_capital)
  1668	        self.total_pnl = 0.0
  1669	
  1670	        self.trades: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  1671	        self.created_at = self._now()
  1672	        self.updated_at = self.created_at
  1673	
  1674	        # Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø³Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ Ú©Ø¯Ù‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ
  1675	        self.capital_manager = self
  1676	
  1677	    @staticmethod
  1678	    def _now() -> str:
  1679	        return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1680	
  1681	    @property
  1682	    def balance(self) -> float:
  1683	        """Ù…ÙˆØ¬ÙˆØ¯ÛŒ ÙØ¹Ù„ÛŒ Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª."""
  1684	        return self.current_capital
  1685	
  1686	    def update_capital_after_trade(self, pnl: float) -> float:
  1687	        """
  1688	        Ø§Ø¹Ù…Ø§Ù„ Ø³ÙˆØ¯ ÛŒØ§ Ø²ÛŒØ§Ù† Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ø³Ø±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÙ‡.
  1689	        """
  1690	        pnl = float(pnl)
  1691	        self.total_pnl += pnl
  1692	        self.current_capital += pnl
  1693	        self.updated_at = self._now()
  1694	        return self.current_capital
  1695	
  1696	    def record_trade(
  1697	        self,
  1698	        symbol: str,
  1699	        side: str,
  1700	        entry_price: float,
  1701	        exit_price: float,
  1702	        size: float,
  1703	        pnl: float,
  1704	        leverage: float = 1.0,
  1705	        fees: float = 0.0,
  1706	    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1707	        """
  1708	        Ø«Ø¨Øª Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ ÛŒÚ© Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø³ØªÙ‡â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡.
  1709	        """
  1710	
  1711	        if not isinstance(symbol, str) or not symbol.strip():
  1712	            raise ValueError("Ù†Ù…Ø§Ø¯ Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆØ§Ù†Ø¯ Ø®Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1713	
  1714	        if entry_price <= 0:
  1715	            raise ValueError("Ù‚ÛŒÙ…Øª ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ø²Ø±Ú¯â€ŒØªØ± Ø§Ø² ØµÙØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1716	
  1717	        if exit_price <= 0:
  1718	            raise ValueError("Ù‚ÛŒÙ…Øª Ø®Ø±ÙˆØ¬ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ø²Ø±Ú¯â€ŒØªØ± Ø§Ø² ØµÙØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1719	
  1720	        if size <= 0:
  1721	            raise ValueError("Ø­Ø¬Ù… Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ø²Ø±Ú¯â€ŒØªØ± Ø§Ø² ØµÙØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1722	
  1723	        if leverage <= 0:
  1724	            raise ValueError("Ø§Ù‡Ø±Ù… Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ø²Ø±Ú¯â€ŒØªØ± Ø§Ø² ØµÙØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1725	
  1726	        if fees < 0:
  1727	            raise ValueError("Ú©Ø§Ø±Ù…Ø²Ø¯ Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆØ§Ù†Ø¯ Ù…Ù†ÙÛŒ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1728	
  1729	        side = side.upper().strip()
  1730	
  1731	        if side in ("BUY", "LONG"):
  1732	            normalized_side = "LONG"
  1733	        elif side in ("SELL", "SHORT"):
  1734	            normalized_side = "SHORT"
  1735	        else:
  1736	            raise ValueError("Ø³Ù…Øª Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ BUYØŒ SELLØŒ LONG ÛŒØ§ SHORT Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.")
  1737	
  1738	        gross_pnl = float(pnl)
  1739	        net_pnl = gross_pnl - float(fees)
  1740	
  1741	        trade_number = len(self.trades) + 1
  1742	        trade_id = f"{self.session_id}_TRADE_{trade_number:05d}"
  1743	
  1744	        trade_record: Dict[str, Any] = {
  1745	            "trade_id": trade_id,
  1746	            "symbol": symbol.strip().upper(),
  1747	            "side": normalized_side,
  1748	            "entry_price": float(entry_price),
  1749	            "exit_price": float(exit_price),
  1750	            "size": float(size),
  1751	            "leverage": float(leverage),
  1752	            "gross_pnl": gross_pnl,
  1753	            "fees": float(fees),
  1754	            "pnl": net_pnl,
  1755	            "currency": self.currency,
  1756	            "timestamp": self._now(),
  1757	        }
  1758	
  1759	        self.trades.append(trade_record)
  1760	        self.update_capital_after_trade(net_pnl)
  1761	
  1762	        return trade_record
  1763	
  1764	    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1765	        """
  1766	        ØªØ¨Ø¯ÛŒÙ„ Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯ÛŒÚ©Ø´Ù†Ø±ÛŒ Ù‚Ø§Ø¨Ù„ Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ø¯Ø± JSON.
  1767	        """
  1768	        return {
  1769	            "session_id": self.session_id,
  1770	            "session_name": self.session_name,
  1771	            "currency": self.currency,
  1772	            "initial_capital": self.initial_capital,
  1773	            "current_capital": self.current_capital,
  1774	            "total_pnl": self.total_pnl,
  1775	            "created_at": self.created_at,
  1776	            "updated_at": self.updated_at,
  1777	            "trades": self.trades,
  1778	        }

  1780	    def save(self, file_path: str | Path) -> Path:
  1781	        """
  1782	        Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª Ø¯Ø± ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ JSON.
  1783	        """
  1784	        path = Path(file_path)

  1789	            encoding="utf-8",
  1790	        )
  1791	
  1792	        return path

  1794	    @classmethod

  1796	        """

  1829	    def summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1830	        """
  1831	        Ø®Ù„Ø§ØµÙ‡ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª.
  1832	        """
  1833	        winning_trades = sum(1 for trade in self.trades if trade["pnl"] > 0)
  1834	        losing_trades = sum(1 for trade in self.trades if trade["pnl"] < 0)
  1835	
  1836	        return {
  1837	            "session_name": self.session_name,
  1838	            "currency": self.currency,
  1839	            "initial_capital": self.initial_capital,

  1841	            "total_pnl": self.total_pnl,
  1842	            "trade_count": len(self.trades),
  1843	            "winning_trades": winning_trades,
  1844	            "losing_trades": losing_trades,
  1845	        }

  1858	# === AtriaTrade REAL compatibility patch: baseline-7 ===
  1859	class PaperTradingSession(PaperSession):
  1860	    """Adapter Ø¬Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ú†Ø±Ø®Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§Øª Ú©Ø§ØºØ°ÛŒØŒ Ø¨Ø§ Ø­ÙØ¸ PaperSession Ù‚Ø¯ÛŒÙ…ÛŒ."""
  1861	
  1862	    def __init__(self, initial_balance=10000.0, risk_config=None, **kwargs):
  1863	        super().__init__(
  1864	            session_name=kwargs.pop("session_name", "paper_trading"),
  1865	            initial_capital=float(initial_balance),
  1866	            currency=kwargs.pop("currency", "USDT"),
  1867	            session_id=kwargs.pop("session_id", "paper_trading"),
  1868	        )
  1869	
  1870	        from src.core.position_tracker import PositionTracker
  1871	        from src.core.order_executor import OrderExecutor
  1872	
  1873	        self.risk_config = risk_config
  1874	        self.position_tracker = PositionTracker()
  1875	        self.order_executor = OrderExecutor(
  1876	            position_tracker=self.position_tracker
  1877	        )
  1878	        self.market_prices = {}
  1879	
  1880	    def get_balance(self):
  1881	        return self.current_capital
  1882	
  1883	    def update_market_prices(self, prices):
  1884	        normalized = {
  1885	            str(symbol).upper(): float(price)
  1886	            for symbol, price in prices.items()
  1887	        }
  1888	        self.market_prices.update(normalized)
  1889	        self.position_tracker.update_prices(normalized)
  1890	
  1891	    def get_equity(self):
  1892	        equity = float(self.current_capital)
  1893	        seen = set()
  1894	
  1895	        for position in self.position_tracker.positions.values():
  1896	            if id(position) in seen:
  1897	                continue
  1898	            seen.add(id(position))
  1899	
  1900	            if not position.is_open:
  1901	                continue
  1902	
  1903	            market_price = self.market_prices.get(
  1904	                position.symbol, position.current_price
  1905	            )
  1906	
  1907	            # Ø¯Ø± BUY Ù‡Ù†Ú¯Ø§Ù… ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ØŒ cost Ø§Ø² current_capital Ú©Ø³Ø± Ø´Ø¯Ù‡Ø›
  1908	            # Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Equity Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ø±Ø²Ø´ ÙØ¹Ù„ÛŒ Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ø§Ø¶Ø§ÙÙ‡ Ø´ÙˆØ¯.
  1909	            if position.side == "BUY":
  1910	                equity += position.size * float(market_price)
  1911	            else:
  1912	                # Ù…Ø¯Ù„ Ø³Ø§Ø¯Ù‡ short: collateral ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ + Ø³ÙˆØ¯/Ø²ÛŒØ§Ù†.
  1913	                equity += (
  1914	                    position.size * position.entry_price
  1915	                    + position.size * (position.entry_price - float(market_price))
  1916	                )
  1917	
  1918	        return equity
  1919	
  1920	    def execute_order(
  1921	        self,
  1922	        symbol,
  1923	        side,
  1924	        quantity,
  1925	        price,
  1926	        sl=None,
  1927	        tp=None,
  1928	        stop_loss=None,
  1929	        take_profit=None,
  1930	        **kwargs
  1931	    ):
  1932	        side_text = str(getattr(side, "value", side)).upper()
  1933	        quantity = float(quantity)
  1934	        price = float(price)
  1935	
  1936	        if side_text == "BUY":
  1937	            self.current_capital -= quantity * price
  1938	        elif side_text == "SELL":
  1939	            self.current_capital += quantity * price
  1940	        else:
  1941	            raise ValueError(f"Invalid order side: {side}")
  1942	
  1943	        self.updated_at = self._now()
  1944	
  1945	        return self.order_executor.place_and_execute_market_order(
  1946	            symbol=symbol,
  1947	            side=side_text,
  1948	            quantity=quantity,
  1949	            current_price=price,
  1950	            sl=sl if sl is not None else stop_loss,
  1951	            tp=tp if tp is not None else take_profit,
  1952	        )

  1999	def _atria_final_paper_init(
  2000	    self,
  2001	    session_name="default_session",
  2002	    initial_capital=10000.0,
  2003	    currency="USDT",
  2004	    session_id=None,
  2005	    risk_config=None,
  2006	    data_dir=None,
  2007	    **kwargs,
  2008	):
  2009	    self.session_name = str(session_name or "default_session")
  2010	    self.initial_capital = float(initial_capital)
  2011	    self.current_capital = float(initial_capital)
  2012	    self.currency = str(currency)
  2013	    self.session_id = session_id or self.session_name
  2014	    self.risk_config = risk_config
  2015	    self.data_dir = str(data_dir or "data/paper_trades")

  2020	def _atria_final_persist_paper(self):
  2021	    directory = _atria_final_Path(self.data_dir)
  2022	    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2023	
  2024	    path = directory / f"{self.session_name}.json"
  2025	    payload = {
  2026	        "session_name": self.session_name,
  2027	        "initial_capital": self.initial_capital,
  2028	        "current_capital": self.current_capital,
  2029	        "trades": self.trades,
  2030	    }
  2031	    path.write_text(
  2032	        _atria_final_json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
  2033	        encoding="utf-8",
  2034	    )
  2035	    return str(path)

  2037	
  2038	def _atria_final_execute_paper_trade(
  2039	    self,
  2040	    symbol,
  2041	    side,
  2042	    entry_price,
  2043	    exit_price,
  2044	    size=0.1,
  2045	    quantity=None,
  2046	    **kwargs,
  2047	):
  2048	    qty = float(quantity if quantity is not None else size)
  2049	    entry = float(entry_price)
  2050	    exit_ = float(exit_price)
  2051	    normalized_side = str(side).upper()
  2052	
  2053	    if normalized_side in ("BUY", "LONG"):
  2054	        pnl = (exit_ - entry) * qty
  2055	    else:
  2056	        pnl = (entry - exit_) * qty
  2057	
  2058	    trade = {
  2059	        "symbol": str(symbol),
  2060	        "side": normalized_side,
  2061	        "entry_price": entry,
  2062	        "exit_price": exit_,
  2063	        "size": qty,
  2064	        "quantity": qty,
  2065	        "pnl": float(pnl),
  2066	        "status": "CLOSED",
  2067	    }
  2068	
  2069	    self.trades.append(trade)
  2070	    self.total_pnl = float(getattr(self, "total_pnl", 0.0)) + float(pnl)
  2071	    self.current_capital = float(getattr(self, "current_capital", self.initial_capital)) + float(pnl)
  2072	    _atria_final_persist_paper(self)
  2073	    return trade
  2074	
  2075	
  2076	def _atria_final_get_session_stats(self):
  2077	    total = len(getattr(self, "trades", []))
  2078	    wins = sum(1 for trade in self.trades if float(trade.get("pnl", 0.0)) > 0.0)
  2079	    return {
  2080	        "total_trades": total,
  2081	        "winning_trades": wins,
  2082	        "losing_trades": total - wins,
  2083	        "win_rate_pct": (wins / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0,
  2084	        "total_pnl": float(getattr(self, "total_pnl", 0.0)),
  2085	        "current_capital": float(getattr(self, "current_capital", 0.0)),
  2086	    }

  2178	    )
  2179	
  2180	    session.current_capital = float(
  2181	        data.get("current_capital", session.initial_capital)
  2182	    )
  2183	    session.total_pnl = float(data.get("total_pnl", 0.0))
  2184	    session.trades = list(data.get("trades", []))
  2185	    session.created_at = data.get("created_at", session.created_at)
  2186	    session.updated_at = data.get("updated_at", session.updated_at)
  2187	    return session
  2188	
  2189	
  2190	@classmethod
  2191	def _atria_persist_load(cls, file_path):
  2192	    path = _atria_persist_Path(file_path)
  2193	    if not path.exists():
  2194	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Session file not found: {path}")
  2195	
  2196	    data = _atria_persist_json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  2197	    return cls.from_dict(data)
  2198	
  2199	
  2200	def _atria_persist_execute_trade(
  2201	    self,
  2202	    symbol,
  2203	    side,
  2204	    entry_price,
  2205	    exit_price,
  2206	    size=0.1,
  2207	    quantity=None,
  2208	    **kwargs,
  2209	):
  2210	    trade = _atria_final_execute_paper_trade(
  2211	        self,
  2212	        symbol=symbol,
  2213	        side=side,
  2214	        entry_price=entry_price,
  2215	        exit_price=exit_price,
  2216	        size=size,
  2217	        quantity=quantity,
  2218	        **kwargs,
  2219	    )
  2220	
  2221	    self.updated_at = _atria_persist_now()
  2222	
  2223	    # Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø¨Ø§ Ù‡Ù…Ø§Ù† data_dir Ù¾Ú† Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ
  2224	    if getattr(self, "data_dir", None):
  2225	        self.save(
  2226	            _atria_persist_Path(self.data_dir)
  2227	            / f"{self.session_name}.json"
  2228	        )
  2229	
  2230	    return trade
  2231

  2236	PaperSession.save = _atria_persist_save
